    /* ====================== GAME STATE & CORE ====================== */    const canvas = document.getElementById('gameCanvas');

    const minimapCanvas = document.getElementById('minimap');

    let gameState = GameState.TITLE;
    let previousState = null;
    let selectedClass = -1;
    let player = null;
    let currentArea = 'village';
    let cameraX = 0;
    let cameraY = 0;
    let keys = {};
    let animFrame = 0;

    let deltaTime = 0;
    let combatData = null;
    let dialogueData = null;
    let shopData = null;

    /* ====================== PLAYER CLASS ====================== */    class Player {        constructor(classId) {            this.classId = classId;
            this.name = CLASS_NAMES[classId];
            this.level = 1;
            this.exp = 0;
            this.gold = 50;
            this.x = 0;
            this.y = 0;
            this.direction = Direction.DOWN;
            this.baseStats = {...BASE_STATS[classId]};
            this.growth = {...STAT_GROWTH[classId]};
            this.maxHp = this.baseStats.hp;
            this.maxMp = this.baseStats.mp;
            this.hp = this.maxHp;
            this.mp = this.maxMp;
            this.atk = this.baseStats.atk;
            this.def = this.baseStats.def;
            this.int = this.baseStats.int;
            this.spd = this.baseStats.spd;
            this.luck = this.baseStats.luck;
            this.weapon = null;
            this.armor = null;
            this.accessory = null;
            this.inventory = [];
            this.skills = [];
            this.skillPoints = 1;
            this.statusEffects = [];
            this.moveTimer = 0;
            this.moveSpeed = 150;        }        getExpToNext() {            if (this.level >= EXP_TABLE.length) return 999999;
            return EXP_TABLE[this.level];        }        gainExp(amount) {            this.exp += amount;
            while (this.exp >= this.getExpToNext() && this.level < 40) {                this.exp -= this.getExpToNext();
                this.levelUp();            }
        }        levelUp() {            this.level++;
            this.maxHp += Math.floor(this.growth.hp);
            this.maxMp += Math.floor(this.growth.mp);
            this.atk += Math.floor(this.growth.atk);
            this.def += Math.floor(this.growth.def);
            this.int += Math.floor(this.growth.int);
            this.spd += Math.floor(this.growth.spd);
            this.luck += Math.floor(this.growth.luck);
            this.hp = this.maxHp;
            this.mp = this.maxMp;
            this.skillPoints++;        }        getStat(name) {            let val = this[name];
            if (this.weapon && this.weapon[name]) val += this.weapon[name];
            if (this.armor && this.armor[name]) val += this.armor[name];
            if (this.accessory && this.accessory[name]) val += this.accessory[name];
            return Math.max(1, val);        }        equipItem(item) {            if (item.type === 'weapon') {                if (this.weapon) this.inventory.push(this.weapon);
                this.weapon = item;            }
 else if (item.type === 'armor') {                if (this.armor) this.inventory.push(this.armor);
                this.armor = item;            }
 else if (item.type === 'accessory') {                if (this.accessory) this.inventory.push(this.accessory);
                this.accessory = item;            }
            const idx = this.inventory.indexOf(item);
            if (idx > -1) this.inventory.splice(idx, 1);
}        addItem(itemId, qty) {            for (let i = 0; i < qty; i++) {                this.inventory.push({...ItemDatabase.find(it => it.id === itemId), uid: Math.random()});
}
        }        removeItem(item) {            const idx = this.inventory.indexOf(item);
            if (idx > -1) this.inventory.splice(idx, 1);
}        hasItem(itemId) {            return this.inventory.some(it => it.id === itemId);
}        countItem(itemId) {            return this.inventory.filter(it => it.id === itemId).length;
}        isDead() { return this.hp <= 0; }        heal(amount) { this.hp = Math.min(this.maxHp, this.hp + amount); }        restoreMp(amount) { this.mp = Math.min(this.maxMp, this.mp + amount); }        takeDamage(amount) {            const dmg = Math.max(1, amount - Math.floor(this.getStat('def') / 2));
            this.hp = Math.max(0, this.hp - dmg);
            return dmg;        }        takeMagicalDamage(amount, element) {            let dmg = Math.max(1, amount - Math.floor(this.getStat('int') / 3));
            if (element !== Element.NONE) {                const weak = ELEMENT_WEAKNESS[element];
                if (weak && this.accessory && this.accessory.element === weak) dmg = Math.floor(dmg * 0.5);            }            this.hp = Math.max(0, this.hp - dmg);
            return dmg;        }
    }
    /* ====================== ENEMY INSTANCE ====================== */    class Enemy {        constructor(templateId) {            const t = EnemyDatabase.find(e => e.id === templateId);
Object.assign(this, {...t});
            this.currentHp = this.hp;
            this.currentMp = this.mp;
            this.statusEffects = [];
            this.isAlive = () => this.currentHp > 0;
            this.takeDamage = function(dmg) {                const actual = Math.max(1, dmg - Math.floor(this.def / 2));
                this.currentHp = Math.max(0, this.currentHp - actual);
                return actual;            };
            this.takeMagicalDamage = function(dmg) {                const actual = Math.max(1, dmg - Math.floor(this.int / 3));
                this.currentHp = Math.max(0, this.currentHp - actual);
                return actual;            };        }
    }
    /* ====================== BOSS INSTANCE ====================== */    class Boss {        constructor(bossId) {            const template = BossDatabase.find(b => b.id === bossId);
            this.templateId = bossId;
            this.name = template.name;
            this.level = template.level;
            this.phases = template.phases.map(p => ({...p}));
            this.currentPhase = 0;
            this.currentHp = this.phases[0].hp;
            this.maxHp = this.phases[0].hp;
            this.atk = this.phases[0].atk;
            this.def = this.phases[0].def;
            this.int = this.phases[0].int;
            this.spd = this.phases[0].spd;
            this.statusEffects = [];
            this.spriteColors = template.spriteColors;        }        advancePhase() {            if (this.currentPhase < this.phases.length - 1) {                this.currentPhase++;
                const p = this.phases[this.currentPhase];
                this.currentHp = p.hp;
                this.maxHp = p.hp;
                this.atk = p.atk;
                this.def = p.def;
                this.int = p.int;
                this.spd = p.spd;
                return true;            }
            return false;
}        getPhase() { return this.phases[this.currentPhase]; }        isAlive() { return this.currentHp > 0; }        takeDamage(dmg) {            const actual = Math.max(1, dmg - Math.floor(this.def / 2));
            this.currentHp = Math.max(0, this.currentHp - actual);
            return actual;        }        takeMagicalDamage(dmg) {            const actual = Math.max(1, dmg - Math.floor(this.int / 3));
            this.currentHp = Math.max(0, this.currentHp - actual);
            return actual;        }
    }
    /* ====================== UI MODULE ====================== */    const UI = {        showOverlay(id) {            document.querySelectorAll('.ui-overlay').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('active'));
            const el = document.getElementById(id);
            if (el) el.classList.add('active');
},        hideAllOverlays() {            document.querySelectorAll('.ui-overlay').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('active'));
},        updateHUD() {            if (!player) return;
            document.getElementById('hud-hp').textContent = `${player.hp}/${player.maxHp}`;
            document.getElementById('hud-mp').textContent = `${player.mp}/${player.maxMp}`;
            document.getElementById('hud-exp').textContent = `${player.exp}/${player.getExpToNext()}`;
            document.getElementById('hud-hp-bar').style.width = `${(player.hp/player.maxHp)*100}%`;
            document.getElementById('hud-mp-bar').style.width = `${(player.mp/player.maxMp)*100}%`;
            document.getElementById('hud-exp-bar').style.width = `${(player.exp/player.getExpToNext())*100}%`;
            document.getElementById('hud-level').textContent = `Lv. ${player.level}`;
            document.getElementById('hud-gold').textContent = `Gold: ${player.gold}`;
            document.getElementById('hud-location').textContent = currentArea.replace(/([A-Z])/g,' $1').replace(/^./,s=>s.toUpperCase());
},        openInventory() {            previousState = gameState;

            this.showOverlay('gameHUD');        },        openSkills() {            previousState = gameState;            gameState = GameState.SKILLS;
            this.showOverlay('skillUI');            SkillTree.render();        },        closeSkills() {            gameState = previousState || GameState.EXPLORE;
            this.showOverlay('gameHUD');        },        openQuests() {            previousState = gameState;            gameState = GameState.QUEST_LOG;
            this.showOverlay('questUI');            Quest.render();        },        closeQuests() {            gameState = previousState || GameState.EXPLORE;
            this.showOverlay('gameHUD');        },        openMap() {            previousState = gameState;            gameState = GameState.MAP_VIEW;
            this.showOverlay('mapUI');            MapUI.render();        },        closeMap() {            gameState = previousState || GameState.EXPLORE;
            this.showOverlay('gameHUD');        },        openSave() {            previousState = gameState;            gameState = GameState.SAVE_MENU;
            this.showOverlay('saveUI');            SaveUI.render();        },        closeSave() {            gameState = previousState || GameState.EXPLORE;
            this.showOverlay('gameHUD');        },        openPause() {            previousState = gameState;            gameState = GameState.PAUSE;
            this.showOverlay('pauseMenu');        },        closePause() {            gameState = GameState.EXPLORE;
            this.showOverlay('gameHUD');        },        openCraft() {            previousState = gameState;            gameState = GameState.CRAFT;
            this.showOverlay('craftUI');            CraftUI.render();        },        closeCraft() {            gameState = previousState || GameState.EXPLORE;
            this.showOverlay('gameHUD');
},        openShop(shopName, items) {            previousState = gameState;
gameState = GameState.SHOP;
shopData = { name: shopName, items: items };
            this.showOverlay('shopUI');            ShopUI.render();        },        closeShop() {            gameState = GameState.EXPLORE;            shopData = null;
            this.showOverlay('gameHUD');        },        returnToTitle() {            gameState = GameState.TITLE;            gameStarted = false;
            this.showOverlay('titleScreen');        },        drawPortrait(canvasId, colors, type) {            const c = document.getElementById(canvasId);
            if (!c) return;
            const cx = c.getContext('2d');
            cx.clearRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);
            cx.fillStyle = '#1a0a2e';
            cx.fillRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);
            cx.fillStyle = colors[0];
            cx.fillRect(20,10,40,35);
            cx.fillStyle = colors[2];
            cx.fillRect(25,45,30,30);
            cx.fillStyle = colors[1];
            cx.fillRect(30,15,20,10);
            cx.fillStyle = '#f0c8a0';
            cx.fillRect(28,20,24,16);
            cx.fillStyle = '#333';
            cx.fillRect(32,22,4,4);
            cx.fillRect(44,22,4,4);
            cx.fillStyle = '#c9a959';
            cx.fillRect(36,30,8,3);        }
    };
    /* ====================== INVENTORY MODULE ====================== */    const Inventory = {        currentTab: 'all',        selectedItem: null,        render() {            this.filterTab(this.currentTab);
},        filterTab(tab) {            this.currentTab = tab;
            document.querySelectorAll('#inventoryUI .inv-tab').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
            const tabs = document.querySelectorAll('#inventoryUI .inv-tab');
            const tabMap = {'all':0,'weapon':1,'armor':2,'accessory':3,'consumable':4};
            if (tabs[tabMap[tab]]) tabs[tabMap[tab]].classList.add('active');
            const grid = document.getElementById('inventory-grid');            grid.innerHTML = '';
            let items = player.inventory;
            if (tab !== 'all') items = items.filter(i => i.type === tab);
items.forEach((item, idx) => {                const slot = document.createElement('div');
slot.className = 'inv-slot' + (this.selectedItem === item ? ' selected' : '');
slot.innerHTML = `<span class="item-icon">${ITEM_ICONS[item.type]||'?'}
</span>`;                slot.onclick = () => { this.selectedItem = item;
 this.render();
 this.showDetails(item); };                grid.appendChild(slot);            });
            for (let i = items.length; i < 30; i++) {                const slot = document.createElement('div');
slot.className = 'inv-slot';
grid.appendChild(slot);
}
            if (this.selectedItem) this.showDetails(this.selectedItem);        },        showDetails(item) {            const det = document.getElementById('item-details');
            let html = `<h3>${item.name}
</h3>`;            html += `<div class="item-type">${item.type.toUpperCase()}${item.classReq !== undefined ? ' - '+CLASS_NAMES[item.classReq] : ''}
</div>`;            html += `<div class="item-desc">${item.description}
</div>`;
            if (item.type === 'consumable') {                html += `<button class="btn btn-sm btn-primary" onclick="Inventory.useItem()">Use</button>`;            }
 else {                html += `<button class="btn btn-sm" onclick="Inventory.equipItem()">Equip</button>`;
}            html += `<button class="btn btn-sm btn-danger" onclick="Inventory.sellItem()">Sell (${item.sellPrice || 0}g)</button>`;
            let stats = [];
            if (item.atk) stats.push(`<span class="stat-line text-fire">ATK +${item.atk}
</span>`);
            if (item.def) stats.push(`<span class="stat-line text-green">DEF +${item.def}
</span>`);
            if (item.hp) stats.push(`<span class="stat-line text-red">HP ${item.hp > 0 ? '+' : ''}${item.hp}
</span>`);
            if (item.mp) stats.push(`<span class="stat-line text-blue">MP +${item.mp}
</span>`);
            if (item.spd) stats.push(`<span class="stat-line text-lightning">SPD +${item.spd}
</span>`);
            if (item.luck) stats.push(`<span class="stat-line text-gold">LUCK +${item.luck}
</span>`);
            if (stats.length) html += `<div class="item-stats mt-1">${stats.join('')}
</div>`;
            if (item.healAmount && item.type === 'consumable') {                html += `<div class="mt-1 text-green">Restores ${item.healAmount} ${item.subtype === 'heal_mp' ? 'MP' : 'HP'}
</div>`;            }            det.innerHTML = html;        },        useItem() {            if (!this.selectedItem || this.selectedItem.type !== 'consumable') return;
            const item = this.selectedItem;
            if (item.subtype === 'heal_hp') {                player.heal(item.healAmount);                CombatLog.add(`Used ${item.name}. Restored ${item.healAmount} HP.`);            }
 else if (item.subtype === 'heal_mp') {                player.restoreMp(item.healAmount);                CombatLog.add(`Used ${item.name}. Restored ${item.healAmount} MP.`);            }
 else if (item.subtype === 'heal_both') {                player.heal(item.healAmount);
                player.restoreMp(item.healAmountMP || 0);                CombatLog.add(`Used ${item.name}.`);            }
 else if (item.subtype === 'full_heal') {                player.hp = player.maxHp;
                player.mp = player.maxMp;                CombatLog.add(`Used ${item.name}. Fully restored!`);            }
 else if (item.subtype === 'cure') {                player.statusEffects = player.statusEffects.filter(s => s.type !== item.cureStatus);
CombatLog.add(`Used ${item.name}. Status cured.`);
}
 else if (item.subtype === 'cure_all') {                player.statusEffects = [];
CombatLog.add(`Used ${item.name}. All status effects cured.`);
}            player.removeItem(item);
            this.selectedItem = null;
            this.render();            UI.updateHUD();        },        equipItem() {            if (!this.selectedItem || this.selectedItem.type === 'consumable') return;
            if (this.selectedItem.classReq !== undefined && this.selectedItem.classReq !== player.classId) {                CombatLog.add('Cannot equip: wrong class!');
return;
}            player.equipItem(this.selectedItem);
            this.selectedItem = null;
            this.render();            UI.updateHUD();        },        sellItem() {            if (!this.selectedItem) return;
            player.gold += this.selectedItem.sellPrice || 0;
            player.removeItem(this.selectedItem);
            this.selectedItem = null;
            this.render();            UI.updateHUD();        }
    };
    /* ====================== SKILL TREE MODULE ====================== */    const SkillTree = {        render() {            document.getElementById('skill-points').textContent = player.skillPoints;
            const container = document.getElementById('skill-container');            container.innerHTML = '';
            const branches = ['blade','fortress','warcry'];
            const branchNames = ['Blade','Fortress','War Cry'];
            const branchColors = ['#ff6b6b','#6b9fff','#ffff44'];
            if (player.classId === CharacterClass.MAGE) {                branches[0]='fire'; branches[1]='ice'; branches[2]='arcane';
branchNames[0]='Fire'; branchNames[1]='Ice'; branchNames[2]='Arcane';
branchColors[0]='#ff4444'; branchColors[1]='#44ccff'; branchColors[2]='#d4a8ff';
}
 else if (player.classId === CharacterClass.ROGUE) {                branches[0]='shadow'; branches[1]='poison'; branches[2]='fortune';
branchNames[0]='Shadow'; branchNames[1]='Poison'; branchNames[2]='Fortune';
branchColors[0]='#aa44ff'; branchColors[1]='#44aa44'; branchColors[2]='#c9a959';
}
            for (let b = 0; b < 3; b++) {                const treeDiv = document.createElement('div');
treeDiv.className = 'skill-tree';
treeDiv.innerHTML = `<h3 style="color:${branchColors[b]}">${branchNames[b]}
</h3>`;
                const branchSkills = SkillDatabase.filter(s => s.class === player.classId && s.branch === branches[b]);
branchSkills.sort((a,b) => a.unlockLevel - b.unlockLevel);
branchSkills.forEach((skill, idx) => {                    const isUnlocked = player.skills.includes(skill.id);
                    const prereqMet = !skill.prerequisite || player.skills.includes(skill.prerequisite);
                    const canUnlock = !isUnlocked && prereqMet && player.level >= skill.unlockLevel && player.skillPoints > 0;
                    const node = document.createElement('div');
node.className = 'skill-node' + (isUnlocked ? ' unlocked' : '') + (canUnlock ? ' available' : '');
node.innerHTML = `<h4>${skill.name} ${isUnlocked ? '(Learned)' : ''}
</h4><p>${skill.description}
</p><p class="text-blue">MP: ${skill.mpCost} | Power: ${skill.power}
</p>`;
                    if (canUnlock) {                        node.onclick = () => { player.skills.push(skill.id);
 player.skillPoints--;
 this.render(); };                    }                    treeDiv.appendChild(node);
                    if (idx < branchSkills.length - 1) {                        const conn = document.createElement('div');
conn.className = 'skill-connections';
conn.textContent = 'â†“';
treeDiv.appendChild(conn);
}
                });                container.appendChild(treeDiv);            }
        }
    };
    /* ====================== QUEST MODULE ====================== */    const Quest = {        currentTab: 'active',        filterTab(tab) {            this.currentTab = tab;
            document.querySelectorAll('#questUI .inv-tab').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
            const tabs = document.querySelectorAll('#questUI .inv-tab');
            if (tab === 'active' && tabs[0]) tabs[0].classList.add('active');
            if (tab === 'completed' && tabs[1]) tabs[1].classList.add('active');
            this.render();        },        render() {            const list = document.getElementById('quest-list');            list.innerHTML = '';
            const isComplete = this.currentTab === 'completed';            QuestDatabase.forEach(q => {                const completed = questFlags['quest_done_'+q.id];
                const active = questFlags['quest_active_'+q.id];
                if (isComplete && !completed) return;
                if (!isComplete && !active) return;
                const item = document.createElement('div');                item.className = 'quest-item' + (completed ? ' completed' : ' active');
                let objHtml = '';                q.objectives.forEach(obj => {                    const prog = questFlags[q.id+'_'+obj.target] || 0;
                    const done = prog >= obj.required;
objHtml += `<div class="quest-objective${done?' done':''}">${done?'âœ“':'â—‹'} ${obj.desc} (${Math.min(prog,obj.required)}/${obj.required})</div>`;
});
item.innerHTML = `<span class="quest-type ${q.type}">${q.type==='main'?'Main':'Side'}
</span><h4>${q.name}
</h4><p class="text-gray">${q.description}
</p><div class="quest-objectives">${objHtml}
</div><div class="quest-rewards">Reward: ${q.rewards.exp} EXP, ${q.rewards.gold} Gold</div>`;                list.appendChild(item);            });
            if (list.children.length === 0) {                list.innerHTML = `<p class="text-center text-gray mt-3">${isComplete?'No completed quests yet.':'No active quests.'}
</p>`;            }
        },        acceptQuest(questId) {            questFlags['quest_active_'+questId] = true;
questFlags[questId+'_accepted'] = true;
},        completeQuest(questId) {            const q = QuestDatabase.find(qq => qq.id === questId);
            if (!q) return;
            player.gainExp(q.rewards.exp);
            player.gold += q.rewards.gold;
            if (q.rewards.items) {                q.rewards.items.forEach(ri => player.addItem(ri.id, ri.qty));
}            questFlags['quest_active_'+questId] = false;
questFlags['quest_done_'+questId] = true;
},        updateObjective(target, amount) {            QuestDatabase.forEach(q => {                if (!questFlags['quest_active_'+q.id]) return;
q.objectives.forEach(obj => {                    if (obj.target === target) {                        const key = q.id+'_'+target;
questFlags[key] = (questFlags[key]||0) + amount;
}
                });            });        },        isQuestActive(id) { return !!questFlags['quest_active_'+id]; },        isQuestDone(id) { return !!questFlags['quest_done_'+id]; }
    };
    /* ====================== COMBAT LOG ====================== */    const CombatLog = {        messages: [],        add(msg) {            this.messages.push(msg);
            if (this.messages.length > 50) this.messages.shift();
            const log = document.getElementById('combat-log');
            if (log) {                const p = document.createElement('p');
p.textContent = msg;
log.appendChild(p);
log.scrollTop = log.scrollHeight;
}
        },        clear() {            this.messages = [];
            const log = document.getElementById('combat-log');
            if (log) log.innerHTML = '';        }
    };
    /* ====================== COMBAT MODULE ====================== */    const Combat = {        enemies: [],        turnOrder: [],        currentTurn: 0,        playerTurn: false,        boss: null,        bossBattle: false,        actionPending: null,        startBattle(enemyIds, isBoss, bossId) {            gameState = GameState.COMBAT;
            this.bossBattle = isBoss || false;
            this.boss = null;
            this.enemies = [];
            if (this.bossBattle && bossId !== undefined) {                this.boss = new Boss(bossId);            }
 else {                enemyIds.forEach(id => this.enemies.push(new Enemy(id)));            }            CombatLog.clear();            CombatLog.add('Battle Start!');
            if (this.bossBattle) {                CombatLog.add(`${this.boss.name} appears!`);            }
 else {                CombatLog.add(`${this.enemies.map(e=>e.name).join(', ')} appeared!`);            }            UI.showOverlay('combatUI');
            this.renderEnemies();
            this.startTurn();        },        renderEnemies() {            const container = document.getElementById('combat-enemies');            container.innerHTML = '';
            if (this.bossBattle && this.boss) {                const div = document.createElement('div');
div.className = 'combat-enemy';
div.innerHTML = `<canvas class="enemy-sprite" width="64" height="64" id="boss-sprite">
</canvas><div class="enemy-name">${this.boss.name} (Phase ${this.boss.currentPhase+1})</div><div class="enemy-hp-bar"><div class="enemy-hp-fill" style="width:${(this.boss.currentHp/this.boss.maxHp)*100}%">
</div>
</div><div style="font-size:7px;color:#aaa">${this.boss.currentHp}/${this.boss.maxHp}
</div>`;                container.appendChild(div);
                this.drawBossSprite('boss-sprite', this.boss.spriteColors);            }
 else {                this.enemies.forEach((e, i) => {                    if (!e.isAlive()) return;
                    const div = document.createElement('div');
div.className = 'combat-enemy';
div.innerHTML = `<canvas class="enemy-sprite" width="64" height="64" id="enemy-sprite-${i}">
</canvas><div class="enemy-name">${e.name}
</div><div class="enemy-hp-bar"><div class="enemy-hp-fill" style="width:${(e.currentHp/e.hp)*100}%">
</div>
</div><div style="font-size:7px;color:#aaa">${e.currentHp}/${e.hp}
</div>`;                    container.appendChild(div);
                    this.drawEnemySprite('enemy-sprite-'+i, e.spriteColors);                });            }
        },        drawEnemySprite(canvasId, colors) {            const c = document.getElementById(canvasId);
            if (!c) return;
            const cx = c.getContext('2d');
            cx.clearRect(0,0,64,64);
            cx.fillStyle = colors[0];
            cx.fillRect(12,8,40,48);
            cx.fillStyle = colors[1];
            cx.fillRect(18,16,28,32);
            cx.fillStyle = colors[2];
            cx.fillRect(22,12,20,16);
            cx.fillStyle = '#ff0000';
            cx.fillRect(26,18,5,5);
            cx.fillRect(38,18,5,5);
            cx.fillStyle = '#000';
            cx.fillRect(28,20,2,2);
            cx.fillRect(40,20,2,2);        },        drawBossSprite(canvasId, colors) {            const c = document.getElementById(canvasId);
            if (!c) return;
            const cx = c.getContext('2d');
            cx.clearRect(0,0,64,64);
            cx.fillStyle = colors[3] || colors[0];
            cx.fillRect(8,4,48,56);
            cx.fillStyle = colors[0];
            cx.fillRect(14,12,36,40);
            cx.fillStyle = colors[1];
            cx.fillRect(20,20,24,20);
            cx.fillStyle = colors[2];
            cx.fillRect(10,8,44,12);
            cx.fillStyle = '#ff0000';
            cx.fillRect(26,24,6,6);
            cx.fillRect(36,24,6,6);
            cx.fillStyle = '#ffff00';
            cx.fillRect(28,26,2,2);
            cx.fillRect(38,26,2,2);        },        startTurn() {            const aliveEnemies = this.bossBattle ? [this.boss] : this.enemies.filter(e => e.isAlive());
            if (aliveEnemies.length === 0 || (this.bossBattle && !this.boss.isAlive())) {                this.endBattle(true);                return;            }
            if (player.isDead()) {                this.endBattle(false);                return;            }            this.turnOrder = [];
            this.turnOrder.push({ type:'player', spd: player.getStat('spd') });            aliveEnemies.forEach((e,i) => this.turnOrder.push({ type:'enemy', index:i, spd: e.spd }));
            this.turnOrder.sort((a,b) => b.spd - a.spd);
            this.currentTurn = 0;
            this.processTurn();        },        processTurn() {            if (this.currentTurn >= this.turnOrder.length) {                this.startTurn();                return;            }
            const unit = this.turnOrder[this.currentTurn];
            if (unit.type === 'player') {                if (player.isDead()) { this.currentTurn++;
 this.processTurn(); return; }                this.playerTurn = true;
                document.getElementById('combat-menu').style.display = 'grid';            }
 else {                const enemy = this.bossBattle ? this.boss : this.enemies[unit.index];
                if (!enemy.isAlive() || (this.bossBattle && !this.boss.isAlive())) {                    this.currentTurn++;
                    this.processTurn();                    return;                }                this.playerTurn = false;                setTimeout(() => this.enemyAction(enemy), 800);            }
        },        playerAttack() {            if (!this.playerTurn) return;
            document.getElementById('combat-menu').style.display = 'none';
            const targets = this.bossBattle ? [this.boss] : this.enemies.filter(e => e.isAlive());
            if (targets.length === 0) { this.endBattle(true); return; }            this.showTargetSelection(targets, (target) => {                const damage = player.getStat('atk') + Math.floor(Math.random() * 5);
                const isCrit = Math.random() * 100 < player.getStat('luck');
                const finalDmg = isCrit ? damage * 2 : damage;
                const actual = target.takeDamage(finalDmg);                CombatLog.add(`${player.name} attacks ${target.name}
 for ${actual} damage!${isCrit ? ' CRITICAL!' : ''}`);
                this.renderEnemies();
                if (!target.isAlive()) {                    CombatLog.add(`${target.name} is defeated!`);
                    if (this.bossBattle) {                        if (this.boss.advancePhase()) {                            CombatLog.add(`${this.boss.name} enters Phase ${this.boss.currentPhase+1}!`);
                            this.boss.currentHp = Math.min(this.boss.currentHp, this.boss.phases[this.boss.currentPhase].hp);                        }
                    }
 else {                        Quest.updateObjective('kill_'+currentArea.toLowerCase().replace(/\s/g,'_'), 1);                    }
                }                this.currentTurn++;                setTimeout(() => this.processTurn(), 600);            });        },        openSkills() {            if (!this.playerTurn) return;
            document.getElementById('combat-menu').style.display = 'none';
            const menu = document.getElementById('combat-menu');            menu.innerHTML = '';
            const available = player.skills.map(sid => SkillDatabase.find(s => s.id === sid)).filter(s => s && player.mp >= s.mpCost);
available.forEach(skill => {                const btn = document.createElement('button');
btn.className = 'combat-btn magic';
btn.innerHTML = `${skill.name}
 <span class="mp-cost">MP:${skill.mpCost}
</span>`;                btn.onclick = () => this.useSkill(skill);                menu.appendChild(btn);            });
            const backBtn = document.createElement('button');
backBtn.className = 'combat-btn';
backBtn.textContent = 'Back';
backBtn.onclick = () => this.restoreMenu();
menu.appendChild(backBtn);
menu.style.display = 'grid';
},        useSkill(skill) {            player.mp -= skill.mpCost;
UI.updateHUD();
            if (skill.target === 'self') {                CombatLog.add(`${player.name} uses ${skill.name}!`);
                if (skill.type === 'buff') {                    player.statusEffects.push({ type: skill.statusEffect, turns: 5 });
CombatLog.add(`${player.name} gains ${STATUS_NAMES[skill.statusEffect]}!`);
}
 else if (skill.type === 'heal') {                    const healAmt = skill.power || Math.floor(player.getStat('int') * 2);
                    player.heal(healAmt);                    CombatLog.add(`${player.name} heals for ${healAmt} HP!`);                }                this.currentTurn++;
                this.restoreMenu();                setTimeout(() => this.processTurn(), 600);                return;            }
            const targets = skill.target === 'all'                ? (this.bossBattle ? [this.boss] : this.enemies.filter(e => e.isAlive()))                : (this.bossBattle ? [this.boss] : this.enemies.filter(e => e.isAlive()));
            if (targets.length === 0) { this.endBattle(true); return; }            this.showTargetSelection(skill.target === 'all' ? targets.slice(0,1) : targets, (target) => {                CombatLog.add(`${player.name} uses ${skill.name}!`);
                const hit = Math.random() * 100 < skill.accuracy;
                if (!hit) {                    CombatLog.add('Miss!');
                    this.currentTurn++;
                    this.restoreMenu();                    setTimeout(() => this.processTurn(), 600);                    return;                }
                if (skill.target === 'all') {                    const allTargets = this.bossBattle ? [this.boss] : this.enemies.filter(e => e.isAlive());
allTargets.forEach(t => {                        let dmg;
                        if (skill.type === 'magical') {                            dmg = player.getStat('int') + skill.power;
                            const actual = t.takeMagicalDamage(dmg);
CombatLog.add(`${t.name} takes ${actual} ${ELEMENT_NAMES[skill.element]} damage!`);
}
 else {                            dmg = player.getStat('atk') + skill.power;
                            const actual = t.takeDamage(dmg);                            CombatLog.add(`${t.name} takes ${actual} damage!`);                        }
                        if (skill.statusEffect && Math.random()*100 < skill.statusChance) {                            t.statusEffects = t.statusEffects || [];
t.statusEffects.push({ type: skill.statusEffect, turns: 3 });
CombatLog.add(`${t.name} is affected by ${STATUS_NAMES[skill.statusEffect]}!`);
}
                    });                }
 else {                    let dmg;
                    if (skill.type === 'magical') {                        dmg = player.getStat('int') + skill.power;
                        const actual = target.takeMagicalDamage(dmg);
CombatLog.add(`${target.name} takes ${actual} ${ELEMENT_NAMES[skill.element]} damage!`);
}
 else {                        dmg = player.getStat('atk') + skill.power;
                        const actual = target.takeDamage(dmg);                        CombatLog.add(`${target.name} takes ${actual} damage!`);                    }
                    if (skill.statusEffect && Math.random()*100 < skill.statusChance) {                        target.statusEffects = target.statusEffects || [];
target.statusEffects.push({ type: skill.statusEffect, turns: 3 });
CombatLog.add(`${target.name} is affected by ${STATUS_NAMES[skill.statusEffect]}!`);
}
                }                this.renderEnemies();
                const aliveAll = this.bossBattle ? this.boss.isAlive() : this.enemies.some(e => e.isAlive());
                if (!aliveAll) {                    this.endBattle(true);                    return;                }                this.currentTurn++;
                this.restoreMenu();                setTimeout(() => this.processTurn(), 600);            });        },        openItems() {            if (!this.playerTurn) return;
            document.getElementById('combat-menu').style.display = 'none';
            const menu = document.getElementById('combat-menu');            menu.innerHTML = '';
            const consumables = player.inventory.filter(i => i.type === 'consumable');
consumables.forEach(item => {                const btn = document.createElement('button');
btn.className = 'combat-btn item';
btn.textContent = item.name;
btn.onclick = () => this.useItem(item);
menu.appendChild(btn);
});
            const backBtn = document.createElement('button');
backBtn.className = 'combat-btn';
backBtn.textContent = 'Back';
backBtn.onclick = () => this.restoreMenu();
menu.appendChild(backBtn);
menu.style.display = 'grid';
},        useItem(item) {            if (item.subtype === 'heal_hp') {                player.heal(item.healAmount);
CombatLog.add(`${player.name} uses ${item.name}. Restored ${item.healAmount} HP!`);
}
 else if (item.subtype === 'heal_mp') {                player.restoreMp(item.healAmount);
CombatLog.add(`${player.name} uses ${item.name}. Restored ${item.healAmount} MP!`);
}
 else if (item.subtype === 'heal_both') {                player.heal(item.healAmount);
                player.restoreMp(item.healAmountMP || 0);                CombatLog.add(`${player.name} uses ${item.name}!`);            }
 else if (item.subtype === 'damage') {                const targets = this.bossBattle ? [this.boss] : this.enemies.filter(e => e.isAlive());
targets.forEach(t => {                    const actual = t.takeMagicalDamage(item.healAmount);
CombatLog.add(`${item.name} hits ${t.name}
 for ${actual} damage!`);                });            }
 else if (item.subtype === 'escape') {                CombatLog.add('Escaped successfully!');
                player.removeItem(item);                gameState = GameState.EXPLORE;                UI.showOverlay('gameHUD');                return;            }
 else if (item.subtype === 'full_heal') {                player.hp = player.maxHp;
                player.mp = player.maxMp;
                player.statusEffects = [];                CombatLog.add(`${player.name} uses ${item.name}. Fully restored!`);            }
 else if (item.subtype === 'cure') {                player.statusEffects = player.statusEffects.filter(s => s.type !== item.cureStatus);
CombatLog.add(`${player.name} uses ${item.name}.`);
}
 else if (item.subtype === 'cure_all') {                player.statusEffects = [];
CombatLog.add(`${player.name} uses ${item.name}. All effects cured!`);
}            player.removeItem(item);
UI.updateHUD();
            this.renderEnemies();
            this.currentTurn++;
            this.restoreMenu();            setTimeout(() => this.processTurn(), 600);        },        flee() {            if (!this.playerTurn) return;
            if (this.bossBattle) {                CombatLog.add('Cannot flee from a boss battle!');                return;            }
            const chance = 50 + (player.getStat('spd') * 2);
            if (Math.random() * 100 < chance) {                CombatLog.add('Escaped successfully!');
setTimeout(() => {                    gameState = GameState.EXPLORE;
UI.showOverlay('gameHUD');
}, 500);
}
 else {                CombatLog.add('Failed to escape!');
                this.currentTurn++;
                this.restoreMenu();                setTimeout(() => this.processTurn(), 600);            }
        },        restoreMenu() {            const menu = document.getElementById('combat-menu');
menu.innerHTML = `                <button class="combat-btn attack" onclick="Combat.playerAttack()">Attack</button>
                <button class="combat-btn magic" onclick="Combat.openSkills()">Magic</button>
                <button class="combat-btn item" onclick="Combat.openItems()">Item</button>
                <button class="combat-btn flee" onclick="Combat.flee()">Flee</button>            `;
menu.style.display = 'grid';
},        showTargetSelection(targets, callback) {            const menu = document.getElementById('combat-menu');
menu.innerHTML = '<div class="combat-targets" id="combat-targets">
</div>';
            const container = document.getElementById('combat-targets');
            if (this.bossBattle) {                const btn = document.createElement('button');
btn.className = 'target-btn';
btn.textContent = this.boss.name;
btn.onclick = () => callback(this.boss);
container.appendChild(btn);
}
 else {                targets.forEach((e, i) => {                    const btn = document.createElement('button');
btn.className = 'target-btn';
btn.textContent = `${e.name} (${e.currentHp}/${e.hp})`;
btn.onclick = () => callback(e);
container.appendChild(btn);
});
}
            const backBtn = document.createElement('button');
backBtn.className = 'combat-btn';
backBtn.textContent = 'Back';
backBtn.style.gridColumn = '1 / -1';
backBtn.onclick = () => this.restoreMenu();
container.appendChild(backBtn);
menu.style.display = 'block';
},        enemyAction(enemy) {            const skills = enemy.skills || ['Attack'];
            const skillName = skills[Math.floor(Math.random() * skills.length)];
            const isPhysical = !skillName.includes('Fire') && !skillName.includes('Ice') && !skillName.includes('Shadow') && !skillName.includes('Dark') && !skillName.includes('Curse') && !skillName.includes('Bolt') && !skillName.includes('Drain') && !skillName.includes('Thunder') && !skillName.includes('Nova') && !skillName.includes('Storm') && !skillName.includes('Beam');
            const hit = Math.random() * 100 < 90;
            if (!hit) {                CombatLog.add(`${enemy.name} uses ${skillName} but misses!`);
                this.currentTurn++;
                this.processTurn();                return;            }
            if (isPhysical) {                const dmg = enemy.atk + Math.floor(Math.random() * 5);
                const actual = player.takeDamage(dmg);                CombatLog.add(`${enemy.name} uses ${skillName}! ${player.name} takes ${actual} damage!`);            }
 else {                const dmg = enemy.int + Math.floor(Math.random() * 8);
                const actual = player.takeMagicalDamage(dmg, Element.SHADOW);
CombatLog.add(`${enemy.name} uses ${skillName}! ${player.name} takes ${actual} damage!`);
}            UI.updateHUD();
            this.renderEnemies();
            this.currentTurn++;
            this.processTurn();        },        endBattle(won) {            setTimeout(() => {                if (won) {                    let totalExp = 0, totalGold = 0;
                    if (this.bossBattle) {                        totalExp = this.boss.level * 100;                        totalGold = this.boss.level * 50;
                        if (this.boss.name === 'Forest Guardian') Quest.updateObjective('boss_forest', 1);
                        if (this.boss.name === 'Lich King') Quest.updateObjective('boss_lich', 1);
                        if (this.boss.name === 'Shadow Lord') {                            Quest.updateObjective('boss_shadow', 1);
gameState = GameState.VICTORY;
UI.showOverlay('dialogueUI');
DialogueSystem.start('game_complete');
return;
}
                    }
 else {                        this.enemies.forEach(e => { totalExp += e.exp; totalGold += e.gold; });
                        if (currentArea === 'Dark Forest') {                            Quest.updateObjective('kill_forest', this.enemies.length);
                            if (this.enemies.some(e => e.id === 1)) Quest.updateObjective('kill_goblin', this.enemies.filter(e=>e.id===1).length);                        }
                        if (currentArea === 'Ruined Castle') Quest.updateObjective('kill_castle', this.enemies.length);                    }                    player.gainExp(totalExp);
                    player.gold += totalGold;
CombatLog.add(`Victory! Gained ${totalExp} EXP and ${totalGold} Gold!`);
UI.updateHUD();
setTimeout(() => {                        gameState = GameState.EXPLORE;
UI.showOverlay('gameHUD');
}, 1500);
}
 else {                    gameState = GameState.GAME_OVER;                    UI.showOverlay('gameOverUI');                }
            }, 1000);        }
    };
    /* ====================== DIALOGUE SYSTEM ====================== */    const DialogueSystem = {        currentTree: null,        currentNode: null,        start(treeKey) {            this.currentTree = DialogueTrees[treeKey];
            if (!this.currentTree) return;            gameState = GameState.DIALOGUE;            UI.showOverlay('dialogueUI');
            this.showNode(this.currentTree);        },        startNPC(npcId) {            const npc = NPCDatabase.find(n => n.id === npcId);
            if (!npc) return;
            let dialogueKey = 'default';
            for (let qid in questFlags) {                if (qid.startsWith('quest_done_')) {                    const num = qid.replace('quest_done_','');
                    if (npc.dialogue['quest_complete_'+num]) {                        dialogueKey = 'quest_complete_'+num;                        break;                    }
                }
            }
            const node = npc.dialogue[dialogueKey] || npc.dialogue.default;
            this.currentNode = node;
            this.currentNPC = npc;            gameState = GameState.DIALOGUE;            UI.showOverlay('dialogueUI');
            document.getElementById('dialogue-speaker').textContent = npc.name;
            document.getElementById('dialogue-text').textContent = node.text;            UI.drawPortrait('dialogue-portrait', npc.portraitColors);
            const choicesDiv = document.getElementById('dialogue-choices');            choicesDiv.innerHTML = '';
            if (node.choices) {                node.choices.forEach(choice => {                    const btn = document.createElement('button');
btn.className = 'dialogue-choice';
btn.textContent = choice.text;
btn.onclick = () => this.selectChoice(choice);
choicesDiv.appendChild(btn);
});
}
        },        showNode(node) {            document.getElementById('dialogue-speaker').textContent = 'Narrator';
            document.getElementById('dialogue-text').textContent = node.text;
            const choicesDiv = document.getElementById('dialogue-choices');            choicesDiv.innerHTML = '';
            if (node.choices) {                node.choices.forEach(choice => {                    const btn = document.createElement('button');
btn.className = 'dialogue-choice';
btn.textContent = choice.text;
btn.onclick = () => this.selectChoice(choice);
choicesDiv.appendChild(btn);
});
}
        },        selectChoice(choice) {            if (choice.action === 'shop_weapon') {                let items = [];
                if (player.classId === CharacterClass.WARRIOR) items = ShopInventory.marcus_warrior_weapons;
                else if (player.classId === CharacterClass.MAGE) items = ShopInventory.fiona_mage_weapons;
                else items = ShopInventory.marcus_rogue_weapons;
                if (player.level >= 15) items = items.concat(ShopInventory.late_shop_weapons);                UI.openShop('Weapon Shop', items);                gameState = GameState.SHOP;            }
 else if (choice.action === 'shop_armor') {                let items = [];
                if (player.classId === CharacterClass.WARRIOR) items = ShopInventory.marcus_warrior_armor;
                else if (player.classId === CharacterClass.MAGE) items = ShopInventory.fiona_mage_armor;
                else items = ShopInventory.marcus_rogue_armor;
                if (player.level >= 15) items = items.concat(ShopInventory.late_shop_armor);                UI.openShop('Armor Shop', items);                gameState = GameState.SHOP;            }
 else if (choice.action === 'shop_accessory') {                let items = ShopInventory.marcus_accessories;
                if (player.level >= 15) items = items.concat(ShopInventory.late_shop_accessory);
UI.openShop('Accessory Shop', items);
gameState = GameState.SHOP;
}
 else if (choice.action === 'start_boss_0') {                gameState = GameState.EXPLORE;                UI.showOverlay('gameHUD');                Combat.startBattle([], true, 0);            }
 else if (choice.action === 'start_boss_1') {                gameState = GameState.EXPLORE;                UI.showOverlay('gameHUD');                Combat.startBattle([], true, 1);            }
 else if (choice.action === 'start_boss_2') {                gameState = GameState.EXPLORE;                UI.showOverlay('gameHUD');                Combat.startBattle([], true, 2);            }
 else if (choice.action === 'new_game') {                Game.startNewGame();            }
 else if (choice.action === 'title') {                UI.returnToTitle();            }
 else if (choice.next) {                const nextNode = this.currentNPC ? this.currentNPC.dialogue[choice.next] : DialogueTrees[choice.next];
                if (nextNode) {                    this.currentNode = nextNode;
                    this.showNode(nextNode);                }
 else {                    this.close();                }
            }
 else {                this.close();            }
        },        close() {            gameState = GameState.EXPLORE;            UI.showOverlay('gameHUD');        }
    };
    /* ====================== SHOP UI ====================== */    const ShopUI = {        render() {            document.getElementById('shop-name').textContent = shopData.name;
            document.getElementById('shop-gold').textContent = player.gold;
            const list = document.getElementById('shop-items');
list.innerHTML = '';
shopData.items.forEach(itemId => {                const item = ItemDatabase.find(i => i.id === itemId);
                if (!item) return;
                if (item.classReq !== undefined && item.classReq !== player.classId) return;
                const div = document.createElement('div');                div.className = 'shop-item';                div.innerHTML = `<div class="shop-item-info"><h4>${item.name}
</h4><p class="text-gray" style="font-size:7px">${item.description}
</p>
</div><div class="shop-item-price">${item.price}g</div>`;
div.onclick = () => this.buyItem(item);
list.appendChild(div);
});
},        buyItem(item) {            if (player.gold < item.price) {                CombatLog.add('Not enough gold!');
return;
}            player.gold -= item.price;
            player.addItem(item.id, 1);
            document.getElementById('shop-gold').textContent = player.gold;            UI.updateHUD();            CombatLog.add(`Bought ${item.name}!`);        }
    };
    /* ====================== MAP UI ====================== */    const MapUI = {        render() {            const c = document.getElementById('map-canvas');

        }

    /* ====================== SAVE UI ====================== */    const SaveUI = {        render() {            const container = document.getElementById('save-slots');

    /* ====================== CRAFT UI ====================== */    const CraftUI = {        render() {            const list = document.getElementById('recipe-list');

},        craft(recipe) {            if (player.gold < recipe.cost) { CombatLog.add('Not enough gold!'); return; }
            let canCraft = true;            recipe.ingredients.forEach(ing => {                if (ing.itemId && player.countItem(ing.itemId) < ing.qty) canCraft = false;            });
            if (!canCraft) { CombatLog.add('Missing ingredients!'); return; }            player.gold -= recipe.cost;
recipe.ingredients.forEach(ing => {                if (ing.itemId) {                    const item = player.inventory.find(i => i.id === ing.itemId);
                    if (item) player.removeItem(item);                }
            });
            player.addItem(recipe.result.itemId, recipe.result.qty);
Quest.updateObjective('craft_potion', 1);
UI.updateHUD();
CombatLog.add(`Crafted ${recipe.name}!`);
            this.render();        }
    };
    /* ====================== MINIMAP ====================== */    const Minimap = {        render() {            if (!MapTileData[currentArea]) return;

            }

    /* ====================== WORLD / RENDERING ====================== */    const World = {        getTile(areaName, x, y) {            const data = MapTileData[areaName];
            if (!data || x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= data.width || y >= data.height) return TileType.WALL;
            return data.tiles[y * data.width + x];        },        setTile(areaName, x, y, tile) {            const data = MapTileData[areaName];
            if (!data) return;            data.tiles[y * data.width + x] = tile;        },        isWalkable(areaName, x, y) {            const tile = this.getTile(areaName, x, y);
            return TILE_WALKABLE[tile];        },        render() {            if (!MapTileData[currentArea]) return;
            const data = MapTileData[currentArea];
            const startTileX = Math.max(0, Math.floor(player.x - MAP_VIEW_TILES_X / 2));
            const startTileY = Math.max(0, Math.floor(player.y - MAP_VIEW_TILES_Y / 2));
            const offsetX = Math.floor((CANVAS_W - MAP_VIEW_TILES_X * TILE_SIZE) / 2);
            const offsetY = Math.floor((CANVAS_H - MAP_VIEW_TILES_Y * TILE_SIZE) / 2);
            ctx.fillStyle = '#000';
            ctx.fillRect(0, 0, CANVAS_W, CANVAS_H);
            for (let ty = 0; ty < MAP_VIEW_TILES_Y + 1; ty++) {                for (let tx = 0; tx < MAP_VIEW_TILES_X + 1; tx++) {                    const mapX = startTileX + tx;
                    const mapY = startTileY + ty;
                    const tile = this.getTile(currentArea, mapX, mapY);
                    const color = TILE_COLORS[tile] || '#000';
                    ctx.fillStyle = color;
                    const drawX = offsetX + tx * TILE_SIZE;
                    const drawY = offsetY + ty * TILE_SIZE;
                    ctx.fillRect(drawX, drawY, TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE);
                    if (tile === TileType.CHEST && !openChests.has(currentArea+'_'+mapX+'_'+mapY)) {                        ctx.fillStyle = '#c9a959';
                        ctx.fillRect(drawX + 8, drawY + 10, 16, 12);
                        ctx.fillStyle = '#aa8929';
                        ctx.fillRect(drawX + 12, drawY + 14, 8, 4);                    }                    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.1)';
                    ctx.strokeRect(drawX, drawY, TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE);                }
            }
            if (data.npcs) {                data.npcs.forEach(npc => {                    const npcData = NPCDatabase.find(n => n.id === npc.npcId);
                    if (!npcData) return;
                    const screenX = offsetX + (npc.x - startTileX) * TILE_SIZE;
                    const screenY = offsetY + (npc.y - startTileY) * TILE_SIZE;
                    if (screenX < -TILE_SIZE || screenX > CANVAS_W + TILE_SIZE || screenY < -TILE_SIZE || screenY > CANVAS_H + TILE_SIZE) return;
                    ctx.fillStyle = npcData.portraitColors[2];
                    ctx.fillRect(screenX + 4, screenY + 2, 24, 28);
                    ctx.fillStyle = '#f0c8a0';
                    ctx.fillRect(screenX + 8, screenY + 4, 16, 12);
                    ctx.fillStyle = npcData.portraitColors[1];
                    ctx.fillRect(screenX + 8, screenY + 2, 16, 6);
                    ctx.fillStyle = '#333';
                    ctx.fillRect(screenX + 11, screenY + 8, 3, 3);
                    ctx.fillRect(screenX + 18, screenY + 8, 3, 3);                });            }
            const playerScreenX = offsetX + (player.x - startTileX) * TILE_SIZE;
            const playerScreenY = offsetY + (player.y - startTileY) * TILE_SIZE;
            const colors = SPRITE_COLORS[CLASS_NAMES[player.classId].toLowerCase()];
            ctx.fillStyle = colors.armor;
            ctx.fillRect(playerScreenX + 6, playerScreenY + 14, 20, 16);
            ctx.fillStyle = colors.skin;
            ctx.fillRect(playerScreenX + 8, playerScreenY + 2, 16, 14);
            ctx.fillStyle = colors.hair;
            ctx.fillRect(playerScreenX + 8, playerScreenY, 16, 6);
            ctx.fillStyle = '#333';
            ctx.fillRect(playerScreenX + 11, playerScreenY + 7, 3, 3);
            ctx.fillRect(playerScreenX + 18, playerScreenY + 7, 3, 3);
            ctx.fillStyle = '#c9a959';
            ctx.fillRect(playerScreenX + 13, playerScreenY + 11, 6, 2);
            ctx.fillStyle = colors.detail;
            ctx.fillRect(playerScreenX + 10, playerScreenY + 28, 5, 6);
            ctx.fillRect(playerScreenX + 17, playerScreenY + 28, 5, 6);        },        checkInteractions() {            const data = MapTileData[currentArea];
            if (!data) return;
            for (let dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++) {                for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {                    const tx = player.x + dx;
                    const ty = player.y + dy;
                    const tile = this.getTile(currentArea, tx, ty);
                    if (tile === TileType.CHEST) {                        const key = currentArea+'_'+tx+'_'+ty;
                        if (!openChests.has(key)) {                            openChests.add(key);
                            if (data.chests) {                                const chest = data.chests.find(c => c.x === tx && c.y === ty);
                                if (chest) {                                    chest.contents.forEach(c => player.addItem(c.itemId, c.qty));
CombatLog.add('Found items in the chest!');
}
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    };
    /* ====================== GAME ENGINE ====================== */    const Game = {        startNewGame() {            document.getElementById('titleScreen').classList.remove('active');
            document.getElementById('charSelect').classList.add('active');
gameState = GameState.CHAR_SELECT;
UI.drawPortrait('portrait-warrior', SPRITE_COLORS.warrior);
UI.drawPortrait('portrait-mage', SPRITE_COLORS.mage);
UI.drawPortrait('portrait-rogue', SPRITE_COLORS.rogue);
},        continueGame() {            const data = localStorage.getItem('ros_save_1') || localStorage.getItem('ros_save_2') || localStorage.getItem('ros_save_3');
            if (!data) { CombatLog.add('No save data found!'); return; }            this.loadSaveData(JSON.parse(data));        },        selectClass(classId) {            selectedClass = classId;
            document.querySelectorAll('.char-card').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('selected'));
            document.querySelectorAll('.char-card')[classId].classList.add('selected');
},        confirmClass() {            if (selectedClass === -1) return;
player = new Player(selectedClass);
            player.addItem(100, 1);
            player.addItem(0, 3);
            player.addItem(4, 2);            gameStarted = true;            gameState = GameState.EXPLORE;            currentArea = 'village';
            const startData = MapTileData.village;
            player.x = startData.playerStart.x;
            player.y = startData.playerStart.y;
            document.getElementById('charSelect').classList.remove('active');
UI.showOverlay('gameHUD');
UI.updateHUD();
Minimap.render();
Quest.acceptQuest(0);
questFlags['quest_active_0'] = true;
},        loadSaveData(data) {            player = new Player(data.classId);
            player.level = data.level;
            player.exp = data.exp;
            player.gold = data.gold;
            player.hp = data.hp;
            player.maxHp = data.maxHp;
            player.mp = data.mp;
            player.maxMp = data.maxMp;
            player.atk = data.atk;
            player.def = data.def;
            player.int = data.int;
            player.spd = data.spd;
            player.luck = data.luck;
            player.skills = data.skills || [];
            player.skillPoints = data.skillPoints || 0;
            if (data.weapon) player.weapon = ItemDatabase.find(i => i.id === data.weapon);
            if (data.armor) player.armor = ItemDatabase.find(i => i.id === data.armor);
            if (data.accessory) player.accessory = ItemDatabase.find(i => i.id === data.accessory);
            player.inventory = (data.inventory || []).map(id => ({...ItemDatabase.find(i => i.id === id), uid: Math.random()}));            currentArea = data.area || 'village';
            player.x = data.x;
            player.y = data.y;
questFlags = data.questFlags || {};
openChests = new Set(data.openChests || []);
gameStarted = true;
gameState = GameState.EXPLORE;
UI.showOverlay('gameHUD');
UI.updateHUD();
Minimap.render();
},        loadCheckpoint() {            const data = localStorage.getItem('ros_save_1') || localStorage.getItem('ros_save_2') || localStorage.getItem('ros_save_3');
            if (data) this.loadSaveData(JSON.parse(data));
            else this.returnToTitle();        },        showSettings() {            CombatLog.add('Settings coming soon!');        },        returnToTitle() {            UI.returnToTitle();        }
    };
    /* ====================== INPUT HANDLING ====================== */    document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {        keys[e.key] = true;
        if (e.key === 'Escape') {            if (gameState === GameState.EXPLORE) UI.openPause();
            else if (gameState === GameState.PAUSE) UI.closePause();
            else if (gameState === GameState.INVENTORY) UI.closeInventory();
            else if (gameState === GameState.SKILLS) UI.closeSkills();
            else if (gameState === GameState.QUEST_LOG) UI.closeQuests();
            else if (gameState === GameState.MAP_VIEW) UI.closeMap();
            else if (gameState === GameState.SAVE_MENU) UI.closeSave();
            else if (gameState === GameState.CRAFT) UI.closeCraft();
            else if (gameState === GameState.SHOP) UI.closeShop();
            else if (gameState === GameState.DIALOGUE) DialogueSystem.close();        }
        if (e.key === 'i' && gameState === GameState.EXPLORE) UI.openInventory();
        if (e.key === 'k' && gameState === GameState.EXPLORE) UI.openSkills();
        if (e.key === 'q' && gameState === GameState.EXPLORE) UI.openQuests();
        if (e.key === 'm' && gameState === GameState.EXPLORE) UI.openMap();
        if (e.key === 'c' && gameState === GameState.EXPLORE) UI.openCraft();    });
    document.addEventListener('keyup', (e) => { keys[e.key] = false; });
    /* ====================== GAME LOOP ====================== */    function gameLoop(timestamp) {        deltaTime = timestamp - lastTime;        lastTime = timestamp;        animFrame++;
        if (gameState === GameState.EXPLORE && player && gameStarted) {            handlePlayerMovement();            World.checkInteractions();            World.render();            Minimap.render();
            if (animFrame % 60 === 0) UI.updateHUD();        }
        if (gameState === GameState.TITLE) {            ctx.fillStyle = '#0a0512';
            ctx.fillRect(0, 0, CANVAS_W, CANVAS_H);
            for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) {                const x = (Math.sin(timestamp/1000 + i) * 200 + 400);
                const y = (Math.cos(timestamp/800 + i*2) * 150 + 300);
                const size = Math.sin(timestamp/500 + i) * 3 + 4;
                ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(170,68,255,${0.2 + Math.sin(timestamp/1000+i)*0.1})`;
                ctx.beginPath();
                ctx.arc(x, y, size, 0, Math.PI*2);
                ctx.fill();            }
        }        requestAnimationFrame(gameLoop);    }
    function handlePlayerMovement() {        player.moveTimer -= deltaTime;
        if (player.moveTimer > 0) return;
        let dx = 0, dy = 0;
        if (keys['ArrowUp'] || keys['w'] || keys['W']) { dy = -1;
 player.direction = Direction.UP; }
        else if (keys['ArrowDown'] || keys['s'] || keys['S']) { dy = 1;
 player.direction = Direction.DOWN; }
        else if (keys['ArrowLeft'] || keys['a'] || keys['A']) { dx = -1;
 player.direction = Direction.LEFT; }
        else if (keys['ArrowRight'] || keys['d'] || keys['D']) { dx = 1;
 player.direction = Direction.RIGHT; }
        if (dx === 0 && dy === 0) return;
        const nx = player.x + dx;
        const ny = player.y + dy;
        if (World.isWalkable(currentArea, nx, ny)) {            player.x = nx;
            player.y = ny;
            player.moveTimer = player.moveSpeed;            checkAreaTransitions();            checkNPCInteract();            checkRandomEncounter();        }
    }
    function checkAreaTransitions() {        const data = MapTileData[currentArea];
        if (!data) return;
        const tile = World.getTile(currentArea, player.x, player.y);
        if (tile === TileType.DOOR) {            if (currentArea === 'village') {                if (player.x <= 1) { changeArea('darkForest', 28, 9); }
                else if (player.x >= 18) { changeArea('darkForest', 1, 9); }
                else if (player.y <= 1) { changeArea('darkForest', 14, 18); }
                else if (player.y >= 13) { changeArea('darkForest', 14, 1); }
            }
 else if (currentArea === 'darkForest') {                if (player.x <= 1) { changeArea('village', 17, 6); }
                else if (player.x >= 28) { changeArea('ancientCaves', 1, 8); }
            }
 else if (currentArea === 'ancientCaves') {                if (player.x <= 1) { changeArea('darkForest', 27, 9); }
                else if (player.x >= 28) { changeArea('ruinedCastle', 1, 5); }
                else if (player.y <= 2) { changeArea('ruinedCastle', 13, 17); }
            }
 else if (currentArea === 'ruinedCastle') {                if (player.x <= 1) { changeArea('ancientCaves', 27, 8); }
                else if (player.x >= 28) { changeArea('shadowRealm', 1, 8); }
                else if (player.y >= 17) { changeArea('ancientCaves', 13, 1); }
            }
 else if (currentArea === 'shadowRealm') {                if (player.x <= 1) { changeArea('ruinedCastle', 27, 5); }
                if (player.x >= 15 && player.y <= 2 && !Quest.isQuestDone(6)) {                    DialogueSystem.start('boss_intro_2');                }
            }
        }
    }
    function changeArea(newArea, px, py) {        currentArea = newArea;
        player.x = px;
        player.y = py;        UI.updateHUD();        Minimap.render();        checkBossTriggers();    }
    function checkBossTriggers() {        if (currentArea === 'darkForest' && player.x >= 13 && player.x <= 16 && player.y >= 8 && player.y <= 11 && !Quest.isQuestDone(2)) {            DialogueSystem.start('boss_intro_0');
}
        if (currentArea === 'ancientCaves' && player.x >= 13 && player.x <= 16 && player.y >= 8 && player.y <= 11 && !Quest.isQuestDone(4)) {            DialogueSystem.start('boss_intro_1');        }
    }
    function checkNPCInteract() {        const data = MapTileData[currentArea];
        if (!data || !data.npcs) return;
data.npcs.forEach(npc => {            if (Math.abs(player.x - npc.x) <= 1 && Math.abs(player.y - npc.y) <= 1) {                if (keys['e'] || keys['E'] || keys[' ']) {                    DialogueSystem.startNPC(npc.npcId);
keys['e'] = false;
keys['E'] = false;
keys[' '] = false;
}
            }
        });    }
    function checkRandomEncounter() {        if (currentArea === 'village') return;
        if (Math.random() > 0.06) return;
        const areaEnemies = AREA_ENEMIES[currentArea.replace(/\s/g,' ')];
        if (!areaEnemies || areaEnemies.length === 0) return;
        const numEnemies = 1 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 3);
        const encounterIds = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < numEnemies; i++) {            encounterIds.push(areaEnemies[Math.floor(Math.random() * areaEnemies.length)]);        }        Combat.startBattle(encounterIds, false);    }
    /* ====================== INIT ====================== */    requestAnimationFrame(gameLoop);
/* ============================================================   AUDIO SYSTEM - Web Audio API Procedural Sound Engine   ============================================================ */const AudioSystem = {    ctx: null,    masterGain: null,    musicGain: null,    sfxGain: null,    currentMusicOscs: [],    musicPlaying: false,    initialized: false,    musicVolume: 0.25,    sfxVolume: 0.5,    _musicLoopTimer: null,    _currentMelody: null,    init() {        try {            this.ctx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
            this.masterGain = this.ctx.createGain();
            this.musicGain = this.ctx.createGain();
            this.sfxGain = this.ctx.createGain();
            this.musicGain.connect(this.masterGain);
            this.sfxGain.connect(this.masterGain);
            this.masterGain.connect(this.ctx.destination);
            this.musicGain.gain.value = this.musicVolume;
            this.sfxGain.gain.value = this.sfxVolume;
            this.initialized = true;        }
 catch (e) { console.warn('Web Audio API not available'); }
    },    resume() {        if (!this.initialized) this.init();
        if (this.ctx && this.ctx.state === 'suspended') this.ctx.resume();    },    _osc(type, freq, vol, dest, start, dur) {        if (!this.ctx) return;
        const o = this.ctx.createOscillator();
        const g = this.ctx.createGain();
o.type = type;
o.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, start);
g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol, start);
g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, start + dur);
o.connect(g);
g.connect(dest || this.sfxGain);
o.start(start);
o.stop(start + dur);
},    _noise(dur, vol, start, filterFreq) {        if (!this.ctx) return;
        const sr = this.ctx.sampleRate;
        const len = Math.floor(sr * dur);
        const buf = this.ctx.createBuffer(1, len, sr);
        const d = buf.getChannelData(0);
        for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) d[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * (1 - i / len);
        const src = this.ctx.createBufferSource();        src.buffer = buf;
        const g = this.ctx.createGain();        g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol, start);        g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, start + dur);
        if (filterFreq) {            const f = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
f.type = 'bandpass';
f.frequency.value = filterFreq;
src.connect(f);
f.connect(g);
}
 else {            src.connect(g);
}        g.connect(this.sfxGain);
src.start(start);
src.stop(start + dur);
},    playSound(name) {        if (!this.initialized) this.resume();
        if (!this.ctx) return;
        const t = this.ctx.currentTime;
        const S = {            sword_slash() {                AudioSystem._noise(0.12, 0.25, t, 2500);
AudioSystem._osc('sawtooth', 500, 0.18, null, t, 0.1);
AudioSystem._osc('sawtooth', 300, 0.12, null, t + 0.02, 0.08);
},            magic_cast() {                for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {                    AudioSystem._osc('sine', 300 + i * 200, 0.12, null, t + i * 0.06, 0.35);
AudioSystem._osc('sine', 600 + i * 300, 0.08, null, t + i * 0.06 + 0.1, 0.25);
}
            },            healing() {                [523, 587, 659, 784, 1047].forEach(function(f, i) {                    AudioSystem._osc('sine', f, 0.1, null, t + i * 0.08, 0.25);
});
},            menu_select() {                AudioSystem._osc('square', 800, 0.08, null, t, 0.05);
AudioSystem._osc('square', 1000, 0.08, null, t + 0.05, 0.05);
},            menu_cursor() {                AudioSystem._osc('square', 600, 0.06, null, t, 0.03);
},            damage_taken() {                AudioSystem._osc('sawtooth', 200, 0.2, null, t, 0.15);
AudioSystem._osc('sawtooth', 80, 0.15, null, t + 0.05, 0.15);
AudioSystem._noise(0.08, 0.15, t, 1500);
},            critical_hit() {                AudioSystem._osc('square', 1200, 0.12, null, t, 0.04);
AudioSystem._osc('square', 1600, 0.12, null, t + 0.04, 0.04);
AudioSystem._osc('sawtooth', 800, 0.15, null, t + 0.1, 0.2);
AudioSystem._noise(0.06, 0.1, t + 0.1, 3000);
},            level_up() {                var notes = [523, 659, 784, 1047, 784, 1047, 1319];
notes.forEach(function(f, i) {                    AudioSystem._osc('square', f, 0.08, null, t + i * 0.1, 0.2);
AudioSystem._osc('sine', f, 0.06, null, t + i * 0.1, 0.2);
});
[523, 659, 784, 1047].forEach(function(f) {                    AudioSystem._osc('sine', f, 0.08, null, t + 0.8, 0.5);
});
},            boss_encounter() {                for (var i = 0; i < 8; i++) {                    AudioSystem._osc('sawtooth', i % 2 === 0 ? 400 : 300, 0.12, null, t + i * 0.07, 0.07);
}                AudioSystem._osc('sine', 55, 0.2, null, t, 0.7);
},            item_pickup() {                [880, 1100, 1320].forEach(function(f, i) {                    AudioSystem._osc('sine', f, 0.1, null, t + i * 0.06, i === 2 ? 0.12 : 0.08);
});
},            death() {                for (var i = 0; i < 5; i++) {                    AudioSystem._osc('sawtooth', 400 - i * 60, 0.1, null, t + i * 0.12, 0.18);
}                AudioSystem._osc('sine', 60, 0.25, null, t + 0.7, 0.8);
},            victory() {                var mel = [523, 523, 523, 698, 880, 784, 698, 880, 1047];
                var dur = [0.1, 0.1, 0.1, 0.2, 0.1, 0.1, 0.2, 0.1, 0.4];
                var tm = t;
mel.forEach(function(f, i) {                    AudioSystem._osc('square', f, 0.08, null, tm, dur[i] * 1.3);
AudioSystem._osc('sine', f, 0.06, null, tm, dur[i] * 1.3);
tm += dur[i];
});
},            chest_open() {                [400, 500, 600, 800].forEach(function(f, i) {                    AudioSystem._osc('sine', f, 0.08, null, t + i * 0.08, i === 3 ? 0.2 : 0.1);
});
},            door_open() {                var o = AudioSystem.ctx.createOscillator();
                var g = AudioSystem.ctx.createGain();
o.type = 'triangle';
o.frequency.setValueAtTime(200, t);
o.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(400, t + 0.12);
g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.12, t);
g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + 0.18);
o.connect(g);
g.connect(AudioSystem.sfxGain);
o.start(t);
o.stop(t + 0.18);
},            area_transition() {                for (var i = 0; i < 5; i++) {                    AudioSystem._osc('sine', 400 + i * 80, 0.06, null, t + i * 0.04, 0.25);
}
            }
        };
        if (S[name]) S[name]();    },    playMusic(name) {        if (!this.initialized) this.resume();
        if (!this.ctx) return;
        this.stopMusic();
        this.musicPlaying = true;
        var melodies = {            village: { n: [262, 330, 392, 330, 262, 294, 349, 294, 262, 330, 392, 523, 392, 330, 262, 330], t: 0.25, w: 'sine' },            forest: { n: [196, 220, 262, 220, 196, 175, 196, 220, 262, 294, 262, 220, 196, 165, 196, 220], t: 0.3, w: 'triangle' },            ancientCaves: { n: [131, 165, 131, 110, 131, 165, 196, 165, 131, 110, 98, 110, 131, 165, 131, 110], t: 0.35, w: 'sine' },            ruinedCastle: { n: [196, 185, 175, 165, 175, 185, 196, 220, 262, 220, 196, 185, 175, 165, 147, 131], t: 0.28, w: 'sawtooth' },            shadowRealm: { n: [98, 110, 98, 82, 98, 110, 131, 110, 98, 82, 73, 82, 98, 110, 131, 147], t: 0.32, w: 'triangle' },            boss: { n: [196, 196, 233, 196, 196, 262, 196, 196, 294, 262, 233, 196, 175, 165, 175, 196], t: 0.18, w: 'sawtooth' },            victory_m: { n: [523, 523, 523, 698, 880, 784, 698, 880, 1047], t: 0.2, w: 'square' }
        };
        this._currentMelody = melodies[name];
        if (!this._currentMelody) return;
        this._loopMelody(this._currentMelody);    },    _loopMelody(mel) {        if (!this.musicPlaying || !this.ctx) return;
        var self = this;
        var t = this.ctx.currentTime;
        var total = mel.n.length * mel.t;
mel.n.forEach(function(f, i) {            self._osc(mel.w, f, 0.06, self.musicGain, t + i * mel.t, mel.t * 0.85);
self._osc('sine', f * 0.5, 0.03, self.musicGain, t + i * mel.t, mel.t * 0.85);
});
        this._musicLoopTimer = setTimeout(function() {            if (self.musicPlaying) self._loopMelody(mel);        }, total * 1000);    },    stopMusic() {        this.musicPlaying = false;
        if (this._musicLoopTimer) {            clearTimeout(this._musicLoopTimer);
            this._musicLoopTimer = null;        }        this.currentMusicOscs.forEach(function(o) { try { o.stop(); }
 catch (e) {}
 });
        this.currentMusicOscs = [];    },    fadeInMusic(name, dur) {        if (!this.ctx) return;
        var d = dur || 1;
        this.musicGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0, this.ctx.currentTime);
        this.musicGain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(this.musicVolume, this.ctx.currentTime + d);
        this.playMusic(name);    },    fadeOutMusic(dur) {        if (!this.ctx) return;
        var d = dur || 1;
        var self = this;
        this.musicGain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0, this.ctx.currentTime + d);
setTimeout(function() { self.stopMusic(); }, d * 1000);
},    setMasterVolume(v) { if (this.masterGain) this.masterGain.gain.value = v; },    setSfxVolume(v) { this.sfxVolume = v;
 if (this.sfxGain) this.sfxGain.gain.value = v; },    setMusicVolume(v) { this.musicVolume = v;
 if (this.musicGain) this.musicGain.gain.value = v; }
};
/* ============================================================   INPUT HANDLER - Keyboard, Mouse, Touch Input System   ============================================================ */const InputHandler = {    keysCurrent: {},    keysPrev: {},    keysJustPressed: {},    keysJustReleased: {},    mouseX: 0,    mouseY: 0,    mouseDown: false,    prevMouseDown: false,    mouseJustClicked: false,    touchActive: false,    touchStartX: 0,    touchStartY: 0,    inputBuffer: [],    maxBufferSize: 10,    keyMap: {        up: ['ArrowUp', 'w', 'W'],        down: ['ArrowDown', 's', 'S'],        left: ['ArrowLeft', 'a', 'A'],        right: ['ArrowRight', 'd', 'D'],        confirm: ['Enter', ' '],        cancel: ['Escape'],        interact: ['e', 'E'],        inventory: ['i', 'I'],        skills: ['k', 'K'],        quests: ['q', 'Q'],        map: ['m', 'M'],        pause: ['Escape'],        tab: ['Tab']    },    init() {        var self = this;
        document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {            self.keysCurrent[e.key] = true;        });
        document.addEventListener('keyup', function(e) {            self.keysCurrent[e.key] = false;        });
        document.addEventListener('mousedown', function(e) {            self.mouseDown = true;            self.mouseX = e.clientX;            self.mouseY = e.clientY;        });
        document.addEventListener('mouseup', function() {            self.mouseDown = false;        });
        document.addEventListener('mousemove', function(e) {            self.mouseX = e.clientX;            self.mouseY = e.clientY;        });
        document.addEventListener('touchstart', function(e) {            self.touchActive = true;
            var touch = e.touches[0];
self.touchStartX = touch.clientX;
self.touchStartY = touch.clientY;
self.mouseX = touch.clientX;
self.mouseY = touch.clientY;
self.mouseDown = true;
}, { passive: true });
        document.addEventListener('touchend', function() {            self.touchActive = false;            self.mouseDown = false;        });
        document.addEventListener('touchmove', function(e) {            var touch = e.touches[0];
self.mouseX = touch.clientX;
self.mouseY = touch.clientY;
}, { passive: true });
},    update() {        var key;
        for (key in this.keysCurrent) {            this.keysJustPressed[key] = this.keysCurrent[key] && !this.keysPrev[key];
            this.keysJustReleased[key] = !this.keysCurrent[key] && this.keysPrev[key];        }
        for (key in this.keysPrev) {            if (!(key in this.keysCurrent)) {                this.keysJustReleased[key] = this.keysPrev[key];            }
        }        this.mouseJustClicked = this.mouseDown && !this.prevMouseDown;
