        this.prevMouseDown = this.mouseDown;
        this.keysPrev = {};
        for (key in this.keysCurrent) {            this.keysPrev[key] = this.keysCurrent[key];        }
    },    isKeyPressed(key) { return !!this.keysCurrent[key]; },    isKeyJustPressed(key) { return !!this.keysJustPressed[key]; },    isKeyJustReleased(key) { return !!this.keysJustReleased[key]; },    isActionPressed(action) {        var keys = this.keyMap[action];
        if (!keys) return false;
        for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {            if (this.keysJustPressed[keys[i]]) return true;        }
        return false;    },    isActionHeld(action) {        var keys = this.keyMap[action];
        if (!keys) return false;
        for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {            if (this.keysCurrent[keys[i]]) return true;        }
        return false;
},    getMousePos() { return { x: this.mouseX, y: this.mouseY }; },    isMouseDown() { return this.mouseDown; },    isMouseClick() {        var clicked = this.mouseJustClicked;
        this.mouseJustClicked = false;
        return clicked;    },    getTouchSwipe() {        if (!this.touchActive) return null;
        var dx = this.mouseX - this.touchStartX;
        var dy = this.mouseY - this.touchStartY;
        var dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
        if (dist < 30) return null;
        if (Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy)) {            return dx > 0 ? 'right' : 'left';        }
 else {            return dy > 0 ? 'down' : 'up';        }
    },    clearInputBuffer() { this.inputBuffer = []; },    addToBuffer(action) {        this.inputBuffer.push({ action: action, time: Date.now() });
        if (this.inputBuffer.length > this.maxBufferSize) this.inputBuffer.shift();    },    getBufferedInput() {        if (this.inputBuffer.length === 0) return null;
        return this.inputBuffer.shift();    },    getMovementDirection() {        var dx = 0, dy = 0;
        if (this.isActionHeld('up')) dy = -1;
        else if (this.isActionHeld('down')) dy = 1;
        else if (this.isActionHeld('left')) dx = -1;
        else if (this.isActionHeld('right')) dx = 1;
        return { dx: dx, dy: dy };    },    isMovementKey(key) {        var movKeys = ['ArrowUp', 'ArrowDown', 'ArrowLeft', 'ArrowRight', 'w', 'W', 'a', 'A', 's', 'S', 'd', 'D'];
        return movKeys.indexOf(key) !== -1;    },    hasAnyInput() {        for (var key in this.keysCurrent) {            if (this.keysCurrent[key]) return true;        }
        return this.mouseDown;    }
};
/* ============================================================   GAME ENGINE - State Machine, Transitions, Clock, Encounters   ============================================================ */const GameEngine = {    state: GameState.TITLE,    prevState: null,    transitioning: false,    transitionType: 'fade',    transitionAlpha: 0,    transitionSpeed: 0,    transitionCallback: null,    gameClock: 0,    dayNightCycle: 0,    encounterTimer: 0,    encounterCooldown: 0,    fps: 0,    frameCount: 0,    fpsTimer: 0,    lastTimestamp: 0,    globalTimer: 0,    screenFlash: { active: false, color: '#fff', alpha: 0, duration: 0, timer: 0 },    screenFreeze: { active: false, timer: 0 },    battleCount: 0,    totalExpGained: 0,    totalGoldGained: 0,    playTime: 0,    encounterRates: {        village: 0,        darkForest: 0.06,        ancientCaves: 0.08,        ruinedCastle: 0.07,        shadowRealm: 0.09    },    init() {        this.state = GameState.TITLE;
        this.gameClock = 0;
        this.dayNightCycle = 0;
        this.encounterTimer = 0;
        this.encounterCooldown = 0;
        this.battleCount = 0;
        this.totalExpGained = 0;
        this.totalGoldGained = 0;
        this.playTime = 0;    },    changeState(newState, callback) {        if (this.transitioning) return;
        if (newState === this.state) return;
        this.prevState = this.state;
        this.transitioning = true;
        this.transitionType = 'fade';
        this.transitionAlpha = 0;
        this.transitionSpeed = 3;
        this.transitionCallback = function() {            GameEngine.state = newState;            gameState = newState;
            if (callback) callback();            GameEngine.transitioning = false;        };    },    updateTransitions(dt) {        if (!this.transitioning) return;
        if (this.transitionAlpha < 1 && this.transitionCallback) {            this.transitionAlpha += this.transitionSpeed * dt;
            if (this.transitionAlpha >= 1) {                this.transitionAlpha = 1;
                if (this.transitionCallback) {                    this.transitionCallback();
                    this.transitionCallback = null;                }
            }
        }
 else if (this.transitionAlpha > 0 && !this.transitionCallback) {            this.transitionAlpha -= this.transitionSpeed * dt;
            if (this.transitionAlpha <= 0) {                this.transitionAlpha = 0;            }
        }
    },    renderTransition(ctx) {        if (this.transitionAlpha <= 0) return;
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,' + this.transitionAlpha + ')';
        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, CANVAS_W, CANVAS_H);    },    triggerFlash(color, duration) {        this.screenFlash.active = true;
        this.screenFlash.color = color || '#ffffff';
        this.screenFlash.alpha = 0.5;
        this.screenFlash.duration = duration || 0.3;
        this.screenFlash.timer = 0;    },    triggerFreeze(duration) {        this.screenFreeze.active = true;
        this.screenFreeze.timer = duration || 0.3;    },    updateFlash(dt) {        if (!this.screenFlash.active) return;
        this.screenFlash.timer += dt;
        this.screenFlash.alpha = 0.5 * (1 - this.screenFlash.timer / this.screenFlash.duration);
        if (this.screenFlash.timer >= this.screenFlash.duration) {            this.screenFlash.active = false;
            this.screenFlash.alpha = 0;        }
    },    renderFlash(ctx) {        if (!this.screenFlash.active || this.screenFlash.alpha <= 0) return;
        ctx.globalAlpha = this.screenFlash.alpha;
        ctx.fillStyle = this.screenFlash.color;
        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, CANVAS_W, CANVAS_H);
        ctx.globalAlpha = 1;    },    updateFreeze(dt) {        if (!this.screenFreeze.active) return false;
        this.screenFreeze.timer -= dt;
        if (this.screenFreeze.timer <= 0) {            this.screenFreeze.active = false;
            return false;        }
        return true;    },    updateClock(dt) {        this.gameClock += dt;
        this.dayNightCycle = (Math.sin(this.gameClock * 0.01) + 1) * 0.5;
        this.playTime += dt;    },    getTimeOfDay() {        if (this.dayNightCycle > 0.75) return 'night';
        if (this.dayNightCycle > 0.5) return 'dusk';
        if (this.dayNightCycle > 0.25) return 'day';
        return 'dawn';    },    getDayNightTint() {        var t = this.dayNightCycle;
        var r = Math.floor(255 - t * 80);
        var g = Math.floor(255 - t * 80);
        var b = Math.floor(255 - t * 40);
        return 'rgb(' + r + ',' + g + ',' + b + ')';    },    updateEncounterSystem(dt) {        if (this.encounterCooldown > 0) {            this.encounterCooldown -= dt;        }
    },    checkRandomEncounter() {        if (this.encounterCooldown > 0) return false;
        var rate = this.encounterRates[currentArea] || 0;
        if (rate <= 0) return false;
        if (Math.random() > rate) return false;
        this.encounterCooldown = 2;
        this.encounterTimer = 0;
        return true;    },    onBattleStart() {        this.battleCount++;
        this.encounterCooldown = 3;    },    onBattleEnd(exp, gold) {        this.totalExpGained += exp || 0;
        this.totalGoldGained += gold || 0;    },    getStats() {        var hrs = Math.floor(this.playTime / 3600);
        var mins = Math.floor((this.playTime % 3600) / 60);
        return {            playTime: hrs + 'h ' + mins + 'm',            battles: this.battleCount,            totalExp: this.totalExpGained,            totalGold: this.totalGoldGained        };
},    formatTime(seconds) {        var h = Math.floor(seconds / 3600);
        var m = Math.floor((seconds % 3600) / 60);
        var s = Math.floor(seconds % 60);
        return (h < 10 ? '0' : '') + h + ':' + (m < 10 ? '0' : '') + m + ':' + (s < 10 ? '0' : '') + s;    },    updateFPS(dt) {        this.frameCount++;
        this.fpsTimer += dt;
        if (this.fpsTimer >= 1) {            this.fps = this.frameCount;
            this.frameCount = 0;
            this.fpsTimer -= 1;        }
    }
};
/* ============================================================   CAMERA SYSTEM - Smooth Follow, Bounds, Screen Shake   ============================================================ */const Camera = {    x: 0,    y: 0,    targetX: 0,    targetY: 0,    width: CANVAS_W,    height: CANVAS_H,    tileSize: TILE_SIZE,    lerpSpeed: 5,    shakeIntensity: 0,    shakeDuration: 0,    shakeTimer: 0,    shakeOffsetX: 0,    shakeOffsetY: 0,    boundsMinX: 0,    boundsMinY: 0,    boundsMaxX: 0,    boundsMaxY: 0,    zoom: 1,    following: false,    init(mapWidth, mapHeight) {        this.boundsMinX = 0;
        this.boundsMinY = 0;
        this.boundsMaxX = mapWidth * this.tileSize - this.width;
        this.boundsMaxY = mapHeight * this.tileSize - this.height;
        if (this.boundsMaxX < 0) this.boundsMaxX = 0;
        if (this.boundsMaxY < 0) this.boundsMaxY = 0;    },    follow(targetX, targetY) {        this.targetX = targetX * this.tileSize + this.tileSize / 2 - this.width / 2;
        this.targetY = targetY * this.tileSize + this.tileSize / 2 - this.height / 2;
        this.following = true;    },    update(dt) {        if (this.following) {            var lerp = 1 - Math.exp(-this.lerpSpeed * dt);
            this.x += (this.targetX - this.x) * lerp;
            this.y += (this.targetY - this.y) * lerp;        }        this.x = Math.max(this.boundsMinX, Math.min(this.boundsMaxX, this.x));
        this.y = Math.max(this.boundsMinY, Math.min(this.boundsMaxY, this.y));
        if (this.shakeTimer > 0) {            this.shakeTimer -= dt;
            var progress = this.shakeTimer / this.shakeDuration;
            var intensity = this.shakeIntensity * progress;
            this.shakeOffsetX = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * intensity;
            this.shakeOffsetY = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * intensity;        }
 else {            this.shakeOffsetX = 0;
            this.shakeOffsetY = 0;        }
    },    shake(intensity, duration) {        this.shakeIntensity = intensity || 8;
        this.shakeDuration = duration || 0.3;
        this.shakeTimer = this.shakeDuration;
},    worldToScreen(wx, wy) {        return {            x: wx - this.x + this.shakeOffsetX,            y: wy - this.y + this.shakeOffsetY        };
},    screenToWorld(sx, sy) {        return {            x: sx + this.x - this.shakeOffsetX,            y: sy + this.y - this.shakeOffsetY        };
},    tileToScreen(tx, ty) {        return this.worldToScreen(tx * this.tileSize, ty * this.tileSize);
},    screenToTile(sx, sy) {        var w = this.screenToWorld(sx, sy);
        return {            x: Math.floor(w.x / this.tileSize),            y: Math.floor(w.y / this.tileSize)        };    },    isVisible(wx, wy, w, h) {        var sx = wx - this.x;
        var sy = wy - this.y;
        return sx + w > 0 && sx < this.width && sy + h > 0 && sy < this.height;    },    isTileVisible(tx, ty) {        var sx = tx * this.tileSize - this.x;
        var sy = ty * this.tileSize - this.y;
        return sx + this.tileSize > -this.tileSize &&               sx < this.width + this.tileSize &&               sy + this.tileSize > -this.tileSize &&               sy < this.height + this.tileSize;
},    reset() {        this.x = 0;
        this.y = 0;
        this.targetX = 0;
        this.targetY = 0;
        this.shakeTimer = 0;
        this.shakeOffsetX = 0;
        this.shakeOffsetY = 0;
        this.following = false;    },    centerOn(tx, ty) {        this.x = tx * this.tileSize + this.tileSize / 2 - this.width / 2;
        this.y = ty * this.tileSize + this.tileSize / 2 - this.height / 2;
        this.targetX = this.x;
        this.targetY = this.y;    }
};
/* ============================================================   RENDERING SYSTEM - Sprite Drawing, Tiles, Character Rendering   ============================================================ */const SpriteRenderer = {    animTimers: {},    walkFrame: 0,    walkTimer: 0,    walkFrameDuration: 0.15,    idleFrame: 0,    idleTimer: 0,    breatheTimer: 0,    updateAnimation(dt) {        this.walkTimer += dt;
        if (this.walkTimer >= this.walkFrameDuration) {            this.walkTimer -= this.walkFrameDuration;
            this.walkFrame = (this.walkFrame + 1) % 4;        }        this.idleTimer += dt;
        if (this.idleTimer >= 0.5) {            this.idleTimer -= 0.5;
            this.idleFrame = (this.idleFrame + 1) % 2;        }        this.breatheTimer += dt;    },    drawPlayer(cx, classId, dir, frame, x, y, size) {        var s = size || 32;
        var half = s / 2;
        var colors = SPRITE_COLORS[CLASS_NAMES[classId].toLowerCase()];
        if (!colors) return;
        var bodyOffsetY = Math.sin(this.breatheTimer * 3) * 1;
        var legOffset = 0;
        if (frame > 0 && frame < 4) {            legOffset = Math.sin(frame * Math.PI / 2) * 3;        }        cx.fillStyle = colors.detail;
        cx.fillRect(x + s * 0.25, y + s * 0.65 + bodyOffsetY, s * 0.2, s * 0.3);
        cx.fillRect(x + s * 0.55, y + s * 0.65 + bodyOffsetY + legOffset * 0.3, s * 0.2, s * 0.3);
        cx.fillStyle = colors.armor;
        cx.fillRect(x + s * 0.2, y + s * 0.35 + bodyOffsetY, s * 0.6, s * 0.35);
        if (classId === CharacterClass.WARRIOR) {            cx.fillStyle = '#888';
            cx.fillRect(x + s * 0.15, y + s * 0.4 + bodyOffsetY, s * 0.1, s * 0.2);        }
 else if (classId === CharacterClass.MAGE) {            cx.fillStyle = '#6688ff';
            cx.fillRect(x + s * 0.35, y + s * 0.38 + bodyOffsetY, s * 0.08, s * 0.15);        }
 else {            cx.fillStyle = '#aa44aa';
            cx.fillRect(x + s * 0.7, y + s * 0.42 + bodyOffsetY, s * 0.12, s * 0.08);        }        cx.fillStyle = colors.skin;
        cx.fillRect(x + s * 0.28, y + s * 0.1 + bodyOffsetY, s * 0.44, s * 0.28);
        cx.fillStyle = colors.hair;
        cx.fillRect(x + s * 0.25, y + s * 0.05 + bodyOffsetY, s * 0.5, s * 0.15);
        if (dir === Direction.DOWN || dir === Direction.LEFT || dir === Direction.RIGHT) {            cx.fillStyle = '#333';
            cx.fillRect(x + s * 0.35, y + s * 0.18 + bodyOffsetY, s * 0.08, s * 0.08);
            cx.fillRect(x + s * 0.57, y + s * 0.18 + bodyOffsetY, s * 0.08, s * 0.08);
            cx.fillStyle = '#fff';
            cx.fillRect(x + s * 0.37, y + s * 0.19 + bodyOffsetY, s * 0.04, s * 0.04);
            cx.fillRect(x + s * 0.59, y + s * 0.19 + bodyOffsetY, s * 0.04, s * 0.04);        }
        if (dir === Direction.UP) {            cx.fillStyle = colors.hair;
            cx.fillRect(x + s * 0.25, y + s * 0.05 + bodyOffsetY, s * 0.5, s * 0.3);        }        cx.fillStyle = colors.skin;
        if (dir === Direction.DOWN) {            cx.fillRect(x + s * 0.42, y + s * 0.28 + bodyOffsetY, s * 0.16, s * 0.04);        }
    },    drawNpc(cx, npcData, x, y, size) {        var s = size || 32;
        var colors = npcData.portraitColors;
        var bodyY = Math.sin(this.breatheTimer * 2 + npcData.id) * 0.5;
        cx.fillStyle = colors[2];
        cx.fillRect(x + s * 0.2, y + s * 0.35 + bodyY, s * 0.6, s * 0.35);
        cx.fillStyle = '#f0c8a0';
        cx.fillRect(x + s * 0.28, y + s * 0.1 + bodyY, s * 0.44, s * 0.28);
        cx.fillStyle = colors[1];
        cx.fillRect(x + s * 0.25, y + s * 0.05 + bodyY, s * 0.5, s * 0.15);
        cx.fillStyle = '#333';
        cx.fillRect(x + s * 0.35, y + s * 0.18 + bodyY, s * 0.08, s * 0.08);
        cx.fillRect(x + s * 0.57, y + s * 0.18 + bodyY, s * 0.08, s * 0.08);
        cx.fillStyle = '#c9a959';
        cx.fillRect(x + s * 0.4, y + s * 0.28 + bodyY, s * 0.2, s * 0.04);
        cx.fillStyle = colors[2];
        cx.fillRect(x + s * 0.25, y + s * 0.65 + bodyY, s * 0.2, s * 0.3);
        cx.fillRect(x + s * 0.55, y + s * 0.65 + bodyY, s * 0.2, s * 0.3);    },    drawEnemy(cx, enemyData, x, y, size, animState) {        var s = size || 64;
        var colors = enemyData.spriteColors;
        var t = this.breatheTimer;
        var bounce = animState === 'idle' ? Math.sin(t * 2) * 2 : 0;
        var hurt = animState === 'hurt';
        var atk = animState === 'attack';
        var die = animState === 'dead';
        if (die) {            cx.globalAlpha = 0.5;        }
        if (hurt) {            cx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';        }        cx.fillStyle = colors[0];
        cx.fillRect(x + s * 0.15, y + s * 0.1 + bounce, s * 0.7, s * 0.75);
        cx.fillStyle = colors[1];
        cx.fillRect(x + s * 0.25, y + s * 0.2 + bounce, s * 0.5, s * 0.45);
        cx.fillStyle = colors[2];
        cx.fillRect(x + s * 0.2, y + s * 0.05 + bounce, s * 0.6, s * 0.2);
        cx.fillStyle = '#ff0000';
        cx.fillRect(x + s * 0.32, y + s * 0.25 + bounce, s * 0.12, s * 0.1);
        cx.fillRect(x + s * 0.56, y + s * 0.25 + bounce, s * 0.12, s * 0.1);
        cx.fillStyle = '#000';
        cx.fillRect(x + s * 0.36, y + s * 0.28 + bounce, s * 0.05, s * 0.05);
        cx.fillRect(x + s * 0.60, y + s * 0.28 + bounce, s * 0.05, s * 0.05);
        if (atk) {            cx.fillStyle = '#ff4444';
            cx.fillRect(x + s * 0.1, y + s * 0.3, s * 0.8, s * 0.4);
            cx.globalAlpha = 0.3;        }
        if (hurt) {            cx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
            cx.globalAlpha = 0.4;
            cx.fillRect(x + s * 0.1, y + s * 0.1, s * 0.8, s * 0.8);
            cx.globalAlpha = 1;        }        cx.globalAlpha = 1;    },    drawBoss(cx, bossData, x, y, phase, animState) {        var s = 96;
        var colors = bossData.spriteColors;
        var t = this.breatheTimer;
        var pulse = 1 + Math.sin(t * 3) * 0.02;
        var phaseGlow = ['#2d5a1e', '#6a4aaa', '#aa44ff'][phase] || colors[0];
        cx.save();
        cx.translate(x + s / 2, y + s / 2);
        cx.scale(pulse, pulse);
        cx.translate(-s / 2, -s / 2);
        cx.fillStyle = phaseGlow;
        cx.fillRect(s * 0.05, s * 0.05, s * 0.9, s * 0.9);
        cx.fillStyle = colors[0];
        cx.fillRect(s * 0.1, s * 0.15, s * 0.8, s * 0.7);
        cx.fillStyle = colors[1];
        cx.fillRect(s * 0.2, s * 0.25, s * 0.6, s * 0.4);
        cx.fillStyle = colors[2];
        cx.fillRect(s * 0.05, s * 0.08, s * 0.9, s * 0.15);
        cx.fillStyle = '#ff0000';
        cx.fillRect(s * 0.3, s * 0.3, s * 0.12, s * 0.12);
        cx.fillRect(s * 0.58, s * 0.3, s * 0.12, s * 0.12);
        cx.fillStyle = '#ffff00';
        cx.fillRect(s * 0.34, s * 0.34, s * 0.05, s * 0.05);
        cx.fillRect(s * 0.62, s * 0.34, s * 0.05, s * 0.05);
        if (animState === 'attack') {            cx.fillStyle = colors[3] || '#ff4444';
            cx.globalAlpha = 0.5;
            cx.fillRect(0, s * 0.2, s, s * 0.6);
            cx.globalAlpha = 1;        }        cx.restore();    },    drawTileDetailed(cx, tileType, x, y, variation) {        var s = TILE_SIZE;
        var v = variation || 0;
        switch (tileType) {            case TileType.GRASS:                cx.fillStyle = '#2d5a1e';
                cx.fillRect(x, y, s, s);
                if (v % 3 === 0) {                    cx.fillStyle = '#3a6b2a';
                    cx.fillRect(x + 4, y + 8, 2, 6);
                    cx.fillRect(x + 18, y + 14, 2, 5);                }
                if (v % 5 === 0) {                    cx.fillStyle = '#4a7b3a';
                    cx.fillRect(x + 12, y + 4, 3, 4);                }
                if (v % 7 === 0) {                    cx.fillStyle = '#c9a959';
                    cx.fillRect(x + 22, y + 20, 2, 3);                }                break;
            case TileType.WATER:                var wt = (Date.now() / 1000 + x * 0.1 + y * 0.1) % (Math.PI * 2);
                var shade = Math.sin(wt) * 10;
                cx.fillStyle = 'rgb(' + (26 + shade) + ',' + (74 + shade) + ',' + (138 + shade) + ')';
                cx.fillRect(x, y, s, s);
                cx.fillStyle = 'rgba(100,180,255,0.3)';
                cx.fillRect(x + Math.sin(wt) * 4 + 8, y + 6, 12, 2);
                cx.fillRect(x + Math.cos(wt + 1) * 4 + 4, y + 16, 10, 2);
                cx.fillRect(x + Math.sin(wt + 2) * 4 + 14, y + 24, 8, 2);                break;
            case TileType.WALL:                cx.fillStyle = '#4a4a4a';
                cx.fillRect(x, y, s, s);
                cx.fillStyle = '#5a5a5a';
                cx.fillRect(x + 1, y + 1, s - 2, s / 2 - 1);
                cx.fillStyle = '#3a3a3a';
                cx.fillRect(x, y + s / 2, s, s / 2);
                cx.strokeStyle = '#333';
                cx.lineWidth = 0.5;
                cx.strokeRect(x + 1, y + 1, s - 2, s - 2);                break;
            case TileType.FLOOR:                cx.fillStyle = '#8a7a5a';
                cx.fillRect(x, y, s, s);
                cx.fillStyle = '#9a8a6a';
                cx.fillRect(x + 1, y + 1, s - 2, s - 2);
                if (v % 2 === 0) {                    cx.fillStyle = '#7a6a4a';
                    cx.fillRect(x + 4, y + 4, 4, 4);                }                break;
            case TileType.DOOR:                cx.fillStyle = '#6a4a2a';
                cx.fillRect(x, y, s, s);
                cx.fillStyle = '#8a6a3a';
                cx.fillRect(x + 4, y + 2, s - 8, s - 4);
                cx.fillStyle = '#c9a959';
                cx.fillRect(x + s - 10, y + s / 2 - 2, 4, 4);                break;
            case TileType.CHEST:                cx.fillStyle = '#8a7a5a';
                cx.fillRect(x, y, s, s);
                if (openChests.has(currentArea + '_' + Math.floor(x / s) + '_' + Math.floor(y / s))) {                    cx.fillStyle = '#6a5a3a';
                    cx.fillRect(x + 6, y + 8, 20, 16);
                    cx.fillStyle = '#5a4a2a';
                    cx.fillRect(x + 6, y + 8, 20, 6);                }
 else {                    cx.fillStyle = '#c9a959';
                    cx.fillRect(x + 6, y + 10, 20, 14);
                    cx.fillStyle = '#aa8929';
                    cx.fillRect(x + 6, y + 10, 20, 5);
                    cx.fillStyle = '#ddd';
                    cx.fillRect(x + 13, y + 14, 6, 4);                }                break;
            case TileType.NPC_TILE:                cx.fillStyle = '#8a7a5a';
                cx.fillRect(x, y, s, s);                break;
            case TileType.TREE:                cx.fillStyle = '#2d5a1e';
                cx.fillRect(x, y, s, s);
                cx.fillStyle = '#3a2a1a';
                cx.fillRect(x + 12, y + 16, 8, 16);
                cx.fillStyle = '#1a6a1a';
                cx.fillRect(x + 4, y + 2, 24, 18);
                cx.fillStyle = '#2a8a2a';
                cx.fillRect(x + 8, y + 4, 16, 12);                break;
            case TileType.ROCK:                cx.fillStyle = '#2d5a1e';
                cx.fillRect(x, y, s, s);
                cx.fillStyle = '#5a5a5a';
                cx.fillRect(x + 6, y + 10, 20, 18);
                cx.fillStyle = '#6a6a6a';
                cx.fillRect(x + 8, y + 12, 16, 12);                break;
            case TileType.BRIDGE:                cx.fillStyle = '#6a5a3a';
                cx.fillRect(x, y, s, s);
                cx.fillStyle = '#8a7a4a';
                cx.fillRect(x, y + 4, s, s - 8);
                cx.fillStyle = '#5a4a2a';
                cx.fillRect(x + 2, y + 2, 2, s - 4);
                cx.fillRect(x + s - 4, y + 2, 2, s - 4);                break;
            case TileType.LAVA:                var lt = (Date.now() / 800 + x * 0.15) % (Math.PI * 2);
                var lr = Math.floor(255 + Math.sin(lt) * 20);
                var lg = Math.floor(68 + Math.sin(lt + 1) * 30);
                cx.fillStyle = 'rgb(' + lr + ',' + lg + ',0)';
                cx.fillRect(x, y, s, s);
                cx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,200,0,0.4)';
                cx.fillRect(x + Math.sin(lt) * 6 + 8, y + 8, 8, 3);
                cx.fillRect(x + Math.cos(lt) * 6 + 4, y + 20, 6, 3);                break;
            case TileType.DARK_FLOOR:                cx.fillStyle = '#3a3040';
                cx.fillRect(x, y, s, s);
                cx.fillStyle = '#4a4050';
                cx.fillRect(x + 1, y + 1, s - 2, s - 2);
                if (v % 4 === 0) {                    cx.fillStyle = '#2a2030';
                    cx.fillRect(x + 8, y + 8, 6, 6);                }                break;
            case TileType.STONE:                cx.fillStyle = '#5a5a6a';
                cx.fillRect(x, y, s, s);
                cx.fillStyle = '#6a6a7a';
                cx.fillRect(x + 1, y + 1, s / 2 - 1, s / 2 - 1);
                cx.fillRect(x + s / 2, y + s / 2, s / 2 - 1, s / 2 - 1);
                cx.strokeStyle = '#4a4a5a';
                cx.lineWidth = 0.5;
                cx.strokeRect(x, y, s, s);                break;
            case TileType.CARPET:                cx.fillStyle = '#6a1a1a';
                cx.fillRect(x, y, s, s);
                cx.fillStyle = '#8a2a2a';
                cx.fillRect(x + 2, y + 2, s - 4, s - 4);
                cx.fillStyle = '#c9a959';
                cx.fillRect(x + 4, y + 4, 2, 2);
                cx.fillRect(x + s - 6, y + s - 6, 2, 2);                break;
            case TileType.WATER_DEEP:                var dw = (Date.now() / 1200 + x * 0.08) % (Math.PI * 2);
                cx.fillStyle = '#0a2a5a';
                cx.fillRect(x, y, s, s);
                cx.fillStyle = 'rgba(30,60,120,0.5)';
                cx.fillRect(x + Math.sin(dw) * 4 + 6, y + 10, 14, 2);
                cx.fillRect(x + Math.cos(dw) * 4 + 4, y + 22, 10, 2);                break;
            case TileType.SAND:                cx.fillStyle = '#c9a959';
                cx.fillRect(x, y, s, s);
                if (v % 3 === 0) {                    cx.fillStyle = '#b99949';
                    cx.fillRect(x + 10, y + 8, 4, 3);                }
                if (v % 5 === 0) {                    cx.fillStyle = '#d9b969';
                    cx.fillRect(x + 4, y + 20, 3, 2);                }                break;
            case TileType.SNOW:                cx.fillStyle = '#e8e8f0';
                cx.fillRect(x, y, s, s);
                cx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
                cx.fillRect(x + 6, y + 6, 4, 4);
                cx.fillRect(x + 20, y + 14, 3, 3);
                cx.fillRect(x + 12, y + 24, 4, 4);                break;
            case TileType.ICE:                cx.fillStyle = '#aaccff';
                cx.fillRect(x, y, s, s);
                cx.fillStyle = 'rgba(200,230,255,0.4)';
                cx.fillRect(x + 4, y + 4, 10, 2);
                cx.fillRect(x + 16, y + 18, 8, 2);
                cx.fillRect(x + 8, y + 26, 6, 2);                break;
            case TileType.LAVA_ROCK:                cx.fillStyle = '#4a2a1a';
                cx.fillRect(x, y, s, s);
                cx.fillStyle = '#5a3a2a';
                cx.fillRect(x + 2, y + 2, s - 4, s - 4);
                cx.fillStyle = '#ff4400';
                cx.fillRect(x + 10, y + 14, 4, 3);                break;
            case TileType.SHADOW_TILE:                var st = (Date.now() / 1500 + x * 0.2 + y * 0.2) % (Math.PI * 2);
                var sa = Math.floor(42 + Math.sin(st) * 15);
                cx.fillStyle = 'rgb(' + sa + ',' + Math.floor(sa * 0.4) + ',' + Math.floor(sa * 1.2) + ')';
                cx.fillRect(x, y, s, s);
                cx.fillStyle = 'rgba(170,68,255,0.15)';
                cx.fillRect(x + Math.sin(st) * 4 + 8, y + 8, 8, 2);                break;            default:                cx.fillStyle = '#000';
                cx.fillRect(x, y, s, s);        }
    }
};
/* ============================================================   PARTICLE SYSTEM - Effects for Combat, Spells, Environment   ============================================================ */const ParticleSystem = {    particles: [],    maxParticles: 300,    emit(config) {        var count = config.count || 10;
        for (var i = 0; i < count; i++) {            if (this.particles.length >= this.maxParticles) break;
            var angle = config.angle !== undefined ? config.angle + (Math.random() - 0.5) * (config.spread || Math.PI * 2) : Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
            var speed = (config.speed || 50) + Math.random() * (config.speedVar || 30);
            this.particles.push({                x: config.x + (Math.random() - 0.5) * (config.posVar || 0),                y: config.y + (Math.random() - 0.5) * (config.posVar || 0),                vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,                vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,                life: config.life || 1,                maxLife: config.life || 1,                size: (config.size || 3) + Math.random() * (config.sizeVar || 2),                color: config.colors ? config.colors[Math.floor(Math.random() * config.colors.length)] : (config.color || '#fff'),                gravity: config.gravity || 0,                friction: config.friction || 0.98,                fadeOut: config.fadeOut !== false,                shrink: config.shrink || false,                glow: config.glow || false            });
}
    },    update(dt) {        for (var i = this.particles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {            var p = this.particles[i];            p.life -= dt;
            if (p.life <= 0) {                this.particles.splice(i, 1);
continue;
}            p.vy += p.gravity * dt;
p.vx *= p.friction;
p.vy *= p.friction;
p.x += p.vx * dt;
p.y += p.vy * dt;
}
    },    render(cx) {        for (var i = 0; i < this.particles.length; i++) {            var p = this.particles[i];
            var alpha = p.fadeOut ? (p.life / p.maxLife) : 1;
            var size = p.shrink ? p.size * (p.life / p.maxLife) : p.size;
            cx.globalAlpha = alpha;
            if (p.glow) {                cx.shadowColor = p.color;
                cx.shadowBlur = size * 2;            }            cx.fillStyle = p.color;
            cx.fillRect(p.x - size / 2, p.y - size / 2, size, size);
            if (p.glow) {                cx.shadowBlur = 0;            }
        }        cx.globalAlpha = 1;
},    emitFire(x, y) {        this.emit({ x: x, y: y, count: 5, colors: ['#ff4400', '#ff6622', '#ffaa00', '#ff2200'], speed: 30, speedVar: 20, size: 4, sizeVar: 3, life: 0.6, gravity: -80, fadeOut: true, shrink: true });
},    emitIce(x, y) {        this.emit({ x: x, y: y, count: 8, colors: ['#44ccff', '#88ddff', '#aaeeff', '#ffffff'], speed: 25, speedVar: 15, size: 3, sizeVar: 2, life: 0.8, gravity: 40, fadeOut: true, glow: true });
},    emitLightning(x, y) {        this.emit({ x: x, y: y, count: 12, colors: ['#ffff44', '#ffffaa', '#ffffff'], speed: 80, speedVar: 60, size: 2, sizeVar: 1, life: 0.3, fadeOut: true, glow: true });
},    emitShadow(x, y) {        this.emit({ x: x, y: y, count: 6, colors: ['#4a2a6a', '#6a4a8a', '#2a1a3a'], speed: 20, speedVar: 15, size: 5, sizeVar: 3, life: 1, gravity: -20, fadeOut: true });
},    emitHeal(x, y) {        this.emit({ x: x, y: y, count: 10, colors: ['#44ff44', '#88ff88', '#aaffaa', '#ffffff'], speed: 15, speedVar: 10, size: 3, sizeVar: 2, life: 1, gravity: -60, fadeOut: true, glow: true });
},    emitPoison(x, y) {        this.emit({ x: x, y: y, count: 4, colors: ['#44aa44', '#66cc66', '#228822'], speed: 10, speedVar: 8, size: 4, sizeVar: 2, life: 1.5, gravity: 30, fadeOut: true });
},    emitBurn(x, y) {        this.emit({ x: x, y: y, count: 3, colors: ['#ff4400', '#ff6622', '#ffaa00'], speed: 15, speedVar: 10, size: 3, sizeVar: 2, life: 0.8, gravity: -50, fadeOut: true, shrink: true });
},    emitCritical(x, y) {        this.emit({ x: x, y: y, count: 15, colors: ['#ffff44', '#ffaa00', '#ffffff', '#ff8800'], speed: 100, speedVar: 50, size: 3, sizeVar: 2, life: 0.5, fadeOut: true, glow: true });
},    emitLevelUp(x, y) {        this.emit({ x: x, y: y, count: 25, colors: ['#c9a959', '#ffd700', '#ffffff', '#ffaa00'], speed: 60, speedVar: 40, size: 4, sizeVar: 3, life: 1.2, gravity: -40, fadeOut: true, glow: true });
},    emitVictory(x, y) {        this.emit({ x: x, y: y, count: 30, colors: ['#c9a959', '#ffd700', '#ff4444', '#44ff44', '#4444ff', '#ffffff'], speed: 80, speedVar: 50, size: 3, sizeVar: 3, life: 1.5, gravity: 30, fadeOut: true, glow: true });
},    emitDeath(x, y) {        this.emit({ x: x, y: y, count: 20, colors: ['#4a2a6a', '#2a1a3a', '#6a4a8a', '#1a0a2a'], speed: 40, speedVar: 30, size: 5, sizeVar: 3, life: 1, gravity: -10, fadeOut: true });
},    clear() {        this.particles = [];
}
};
/* ============================================================   DAMAGE NUMBERS - Floating Combat Text   ============================================================ */const DamageNumbers = {    numbers: [],    add(x, y, text, color, size) {        this.numbers.push({            x: x + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 20,            y: y,            text: String(text),            color: color || '#ff4444',            size: size || 14,            life: 1.2,            maxLife: 1.2,            vy: -60,            vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 30        });
},    addDamage(x, y, amount, isCrit) {        this.add(x, y, String(amount), isCrit ? '#ffff44' : '#ff4444', isCrit ? 18 : 14);
        if (isCrit) {            this.add(x, y - 15, 'CRITICAL!', '#ffaa00', 10);        }
    },    addHeal(x, y, amount) {        this.add(x, y, '+' + amount, '#44ff44', 14);
},    addMiss(x, y) {        this.add(x, y, 'MISS', '#888888', 12);
},    addStatus(x, y, statusName) {        this.add(x, y, statusName, STATUS_COLORS[0] || '#ffaa00', 10);
},    update(dt) {        for (var i = this.numbers.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {            var n = this.numbers[i];
n.life -= dt;
            if (n.life <= 0) {                this.numbers.splice(i, 1);
continue;
}            n.y += n.vy * dt;
n.x += n.vx * dt;
n.vy += 20 * dt;
}
    },    render(cx) {        for (var i = 0; i < this.numbers.length; i++) {            var n = this.numbers[i];
            var alpha = Math.min(1, n.life / (n.maxLife * 0.3));
            var scale = n.life > n.maxLife * 0.8 ? 1 + (1 - (n.life - n.maxLife * 0.8) / (n.maxLife * 0.2)) * 0.5 : 1;
            cx.globalAlpha = alpha;
            cx.font = 'bold ' + Math.floor(n.size * scale) + 'px "Press Start 2P", monospace';
            cx.textAlign = 'center';
            cx.fillStyle = '#000';
            cx.fillText(n.text, n.x + 1, n.y + 1);
            cx.fillStyle = n.color;
            cx.fillText(n.text, n.x, n.y);        }        cx.globalAlpha = 1;
        cx.textAlign = 'left';    },    clear() {        this.numbers = [];    }
};
/* ============================================================   CANVAS HUD - HP/MP Bars, Minimap, Status, Compass   ============================================================ */const CanvasHUD = {    showMinimap: true,    showStatusEffects: true,    minimapSize: 120,    minimapX: CANVAS_W - 130,    minimapY: CANVAS_H - 160,    renderMinimap(cx) {        if (!this.showMinimap || !MapTileData[currentArea]) return;
        var data = MapTileData[currentArea];
        var mw = this.minimapSize;
        var mh = this.minimapSize;
        var mx = this.minimapX;
        var my = this.minimapY;
        cx.fillStyle = 'rgba(10,5,18,0.85)';
        cx.fillRect(mx - 5, my - 5, mw + 10, mh + 10);
        cx.strokeStyle = '#4a2c8a';
        cx.lineWidth = 2;
        cx.strokeRect(mx - 5, my - 5, mw + 10, mh + 10);
        var scaleX = mw / data.width;
        var scaleY = mh / data.height;
        for (var y = 0; y < data.height; y++) {            for (var xx = 0; xx < data.width; xx++) {                var tile = data.tiles[y * data.width + xx];
                cx.fillStyle = TILE_COLORS[tile] || '#000';
                cx.fillRect(mx + xx * scaleX, my + y * scaleY, scaleX + 0.5, scaleY + 0.5);            }
        }
        if (data.npcs) {            data.npcs.forEach(function(npc) {                cx.fillStyle = '#c9a959';
                cx.fillRect(mx + npc.x * scaleX - 1, my + npc.y * scaleY - 1, 3, 3);            });        }
        if (player) {            cx.fillStyle = '#ff4444';
            cx.fillRect(mx + player.x * scaleX - 2, my + player.y * scaleY - 2, 5, 5);
            var dirColor = '#ff8888';
            cx.fillStyle = dirColor;
            var pdir = player.direction;
            if (pdir === Direction.UP) cx.fillRect(mx + player.x * scaleX, my + player.y * scaleY - 4, 1, 3);
            else if (pdir === Direction.DOWN) cx.fillRect(mx + player.x * scaleX, my + player.y * scaleY + 4, 1, 3);
            else if (pdir === Direction.LEFT) cx.fillRect(mx + player.x * scaleX - 4, my + player.y * scaleY, 3, 1);
            else cx.fillRect(mx + player.x * scaleX + 4, my + player.y * scaleY, 3, 1);        }
    },    renderPlayerBars(cx) {        if (!player) return;
        var bx = 15;
        var by = 15;
        var bw = 160;
        var bh = 10;
        cx.fillStyle = 'rgba(10,5,18,0.85)';
        cx.fillRect(bx - 5, by - 5, bw + 60, 85);
        cx.strokeStyle = '#4a2c8a';
        cx.lineWidth = 1;
        cx.strokeRect(bx - 5, by - 5, bw + 60, 85);
        cx.font = '8px "Press Start 2P", monospace';
        cx.textAlign = 'left';
        cx.fillStyle = '#c9a959';
        cx.fillText('Lv.' + player.level + ' ' + player.name, bx, by + 6);
        cx.fillStyle = '#8a1414';
        cx.fillRect(bx, by + 14, bw, bh);
        cx.fillStyle = '#ff4444';
        cx.fillRect(bx, by + 14, bw * (player.hp / player.maxHp), bh);
        cx.fillStyle = '#fff';
        cx.font = '7px "Press Start 2P", monospace';
        cx.fillText('HP ' + player.hp + '/' + player.maxHp, bx + 2, by + 22);
        cx.fillStyle = '#14148a';
        cx.fillRect(bx, by + 28, bw, bh);
        cx.fillStyle = '#4444ff';
        cx.fillRect(bx, by + 28, bw * (player.mp / player.maxMp), bh);
        cx.fillStyle = '#fff';
        cx.fillText('MP ' + player.mp + '/' + player.maxMp, bx + 2, by + 36);
        cx.fillStyle = '#148a14';
        cx.fillRect(bx, by + 42, bw, bh);
        cx.fillStyle = '#44ff44';
        cx.fillRect(bx, by + 42, bw * (player.exp / player.getExpToNext()), bh);
        cx.fillStyle = '#fff';
        cx.fillText('EXP ' + player.exp + '/' + player.getExpToNext(), bx + 2, by + 50);
        cx.fillStyle = '#c9a959';
        cx.font = '8px "Press Start 2P", monospace';
        cx.fillText('Gold: ' + player.gold, bx, by + 66);    },    renderStatusEffects(cx) {        if (!player || !this.showStatusEffects) return;
        var sx = 15;
        var sy = 105;
        player.statusEffects.forEach(function(effect, i) {            cx.fillStyle = STATUS_COLORS[effect.type] || '#aaa';
            cx.fillRect(sx + i * 22, sy, 18, 18);
            cx.strokeStyle = '#4a2c8a';
            cx.lineWidth = 1;
            cx.strokeRect(sx + i * 22, sy, 18, 18);
            cx.fillStyle = '#fff';
            cx.font = '7px "Press Start 2P", monospace';
            cx.textAlign = 'center';
            cx.fillText(STATUS_NAMES[effect.type][0], sx + i * 22 + 9, sy + 13);        });
        cx.textAlign = 'left';
},    renderLocationName(cx) {        var areaNames = {            village: 'Starting Village',            darkForest: 'Dark Forest',            ancientCaves: 'Ancient Caves',            ruinedCastle: 'Ruined Castle',            shadowRealm: 'Shadow Realm'        };
        var name = areaNames[currentArea] || currentArea;
        cx.fillStyle = 'rgba(10,5,18,0.7)';
        cx.fillRect(15, CANVAS_H - 45, cx.measureText(name).width + 30 || 200, 25);
        cx.strokeStyle = '#4a2c8a';
        cx.lineWidth = 1;
        cx.strokeRect(15, CANVAS_H - 45, cx.measureText(name).width + 30 || 200, 25);
        cx.fillStyle = '#c9a959';
        cx.font = '10px "Press Start 2P", monospace';
        cx.textAlign = 'left';
        cx.fillText(name, 28, CANVAS_H - 28);    },    renderCompass(cx) {        if (!player) return;
        var cx2 = CANVAS_W - 45;
        var cy = 45;
        var r = 20;
        cx.fillStyle = 'rgba(10,5,18,0.8)';
        cx.beginPath();
        cx.arc(cx2, cy, r + 5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        cx.fill();
        cx.strokeStyle = '#4a2c8a';
        cx.lineWidth = 2;
        cx.beginPath();
        cx.arc(cx2, cy, r + 5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        cx.stroke();
        var dirs = ['N', 'E', 'S', 'W'];
        var dirAngles = [-Math.PI / 2, 0, Math.PI / 2, Math.PI];
        cx.font = '7px "Press Start 2P", monospace';
        cx.textAlign = 'center';        dirs.forEach(function(d, i) {            var a = dirAngles[i];
            var dx = cx2 + Math.cos(a) * (r + 2);
            var dy = cy + Math.sin(a) * (r + 2);
            cx.fillStyle = '#888';
            cx.fillText(d, dx, dy + 3);        });
        var pa = dirAngles[player.direction];
        cx.fillStyle = '#ff4444';
        cx.beginPath();
        cx.moveTo(cx2 + Math.cos(pa) * (r - 5), cy + Math.sin(pa) * (r - 5));
        cx.lineTo(cx2 + Math.cos(pa + 0.3) * 4, cy + Math.sin(pa + 0.3) * 4);
        cx.lineTo(cx2 + Math.cos(pa - 0.3) * 4, cy + Math.sin(pa - 0.3) * 4);
        cx.fill();
        cx.textAlign = 'left';    },    renderInteractPrompt(cx, text) {        var px = CANVAS_W / 2;
        var py = CANVAS_H - 80;
        cx.fillStyle = 'rgba(10,5,18,0.8)';
        cx.fillRect(px - 100, py - 12, 200, 24);
        cx.strokeStyle = '#c9a959';
        cx.lineWidth = 1;
        cx.strokeRect(px - 100, py - 12, 200, 24);
        cx.fillStyle = '#c9a959';
        cx.font = '9px "Press Start 2P", monospace';
        cx.textAlign = 'center';
        cx.fillText(text || '[E] Interact', px, py + 4);
        cx.textAlign = 'left';    },    renderAll(cx) {        if (gameState === GameState.EXPLORE && player) {            this.renderPlayerBars(cx);
            this.renderStatusEffects(cx);
            this.renderMinimap(cx);
            this.renderLocationName(cx);
            this.renderCompass(cx);        }
    }
};
/* ============================================================   TEXT RENDERER - Word Wrap, Typewriter, Styled Text   ============================================================ */const TextRenderer = {    typewriterText: '',    typewriterDisplay: '',    typewriterIndex: 0,    typewriterSpeed: 30,    typewriterTimer: 0,    typewriterComplete: false,    typewriterCallback: null,    startTypewriter(text, speed, callback) {        this.typewriterText = text;
        this.typewriterDisplay = '';
        this.typewriterIndex = 0;
        this.typewriterSpeed = speed || 30;
        this.typewriterTimer = 0;
        this.typewriterComplete = false;
        this.typewriterCallback = callback || null;    },    updateTypewriter(dt) {        if (this.typewriterComplete) return;
        this.typewriterTimer += dt * 1000;
        while (this.typewriterTimer >= this.typewriterSpeed && this.typewriterIndex < this.typewriterText.length) {            this.typewriterTimer -= this.typewriterSpeed;
            this.typewriterIndex++;
            this.typewriterDisplay = this.typewriterText.substring(0, this.typewriterIndex);        }
        if (this.typewriterIndex >= this.typewriterText.length) {            this.typewriterComplete = true;
            if (this.typewriterCallback) this.typewriterCallback();        }
    },    skipTypewriter() {        this.typewriterIndex = this.typewriterText.length;
        this.typewriterDisplay = this.typewriterText;
        this.typewriterComplete = true;
        if (this.typewriterCallback) this.typewriterCallback();    },    wordWrap(cx, text, maxWidth) {        var words = text.split(' ');
        var lines = [];
        var currentLine = '';
        for (var i = 0; i < words.length; i++) {            var testLine = currentLine ? currentLine + ' ' + words[i] : words[i];
            var metrics = cx.measureText(testLine);
            if (metrics.width > maxWidth && currentLine) {                lines.push(currentLine);                currentLine = words[i];            }
 else {                currentLine = testLine;            }
        }
        if (currentLine) lines.push(currentLine);
        return lines;    },    drawWrappedText(cx, text, x, y, maxWidth, lineHeight) {        var lines = this.wordWrap(cx, text, maxWidth);
        var lh = lineHeight || 16;        lines.forEach(function(line, i) {            cx.fillText(line, x, y + i * lh);        });
        return lines.length;    },    drawDialogueBox(cx, speaker, text, x, y, w, h) {        cx.fillStyle = 'rgba(26,10,46,0.95)';
        cx.fillRect(x, y, w, h);
        cx.strokeStyle = '#4a2c8a';
        cx.lineWidth = 3;
        cx.strokeRect(x, y, w, h);
        cx.strokeStyle = '#c9a959';
        cx.lineWidth = 1;
        cx.strokeRect(x + 3, y + 3, w - 6, h - 6);
        if (speaker) {            cx.fillStyle = '#c9a959';
            cx.font = '10px "Press Start 2P", monospace';
            cx.fillText(speaker, x + 15, y + 22);        }        cx.fillStyle = '#d4d4d4';
        cx.font = '9px "Press Start 2P", monospace';
        var ty = speaker ? y + 40 : y + 20;
        this.drawWrappedText(cx, text, x + 15, ty, w - 30, 14);    },    drawStyledText(cx, text, x, y, color, size, shadow) {        cx.font = (size || 12) + 'px "Press Start 2P", monospace';
        if (shadow !== false) {            cx.fillStyle = '#000';
            cx.fillText(text, x + 1, y + 1);        }        cx.fillStyle = color || '#fff';
        cx.fillText(text, x, y);    },    drawProgressBar(cx, x, y, w, h, value, max, color1, color2) {        var pct = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, value / max));
        cx.fillStyle = '#0a0512';
        cx.fillRect(x, y, w, h);
        var grd = cx.createLinearGradient(x, y, x + w * pct, y);        grd.addColorStop(0, color1);        grd.addColorStop(1, color2);
        cx.fillStyle = grd;
        cx.fillRect(x, y, w * pct, h);
        cx.strokeStyle = '#4a2c8a';
        cx.lineWidth = 1;
        cx.strokeRect(x, y, w, h);    }
};
/* ============================================================   COMBAT RENDERER - Canvas-based Combat Visuals   ============================================================ */const CombatRenderer = {    animState: 'idle',    animTimer: 0,    targetIndex: -1,    flashTimer: 0,    shakeX: 0,    shakeY: 0,    renderCombatScene(cx, combat) {        cx.fillStyle = '#0a0512';
        cx.fillRect(0, 0, CANVAS_W, CANVAS_H);
        this.renderCombatBackground(cx, currentArea);
        var enemyCount = 0;
        if (combat.bossBattle && combat.boss) {            this.renderBossInCombat(cx, combat.boss, CANVAS_W / 2 - 48, 80);            enemyCount = 1;        }
 else {            var aliveEnemies = combat.enemies.filter(function(e) { return e.isAlive(); });
enemyCount = aliveEnemies.length;
aliveEnemies.forEach(function(e, i) {                var spacing = Math.min(180, (CANVAS_W - 100) / Math.max(aliveEnemies.length, 1));
                var ex = 100 + i * spacing;
                var ey = 120;                CombatRenderer.renderEnemyInCombat(cx, e, ex, ey);            });        }        this.renderPlayerInCombat(cx, player, 100, CANVAS_H - 200);
        this.renderCombatUI(cx, combat);
},    renderCombatBackground(cx, area) {        var colors = {            village: ['#2d5a1e', '#1a4a1a'],            darkForest: ['#1a3a1a', '#0a2a0a'],            ancientCaves: ['#3a3040', '#2a2030'],            ruinedCastle: ['#3a3040', '#2a1a2a'],            shadowRealm: ['#1a0a2a', '#0a001a']        };
        var c = colors[area] || ['#1a0a2e', '#0a0512'];
        var grd = cx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, CANVAS_H);        grd.addColorStop(0, c[0]);        grd.addColorStop(1, c[1]);
        cx.fillStyle = grd;
        cx.fillRect(0, 0, CANVAS_W, CANVAS_H);
        cx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.3)';
        for (var i = 0; i < 20; i++) {            var px = (Math.sin(Date.now() / 2000 + i * 1.5) * 0.5 + 0.5) * CANVAS_W;
            var py = (Math.cos(Date.now() / 3000 + i * 2) * 0.5 + 0.5) * CANVAS_H;
            cx.beginPath();
            cx.arc(px, py, 2 + Math.sin(Date.now() / 1000 + i) * 1, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            cx.fill();        }        cx.fillStyle = '#2a2a3a';
        cx.fillRect(0, CANVAS_H - 120, CANVAS_W, 120);
        cx.fillStyle = '#3a3a4a';
        cx.fillRect(0, CANVAS_H - 120, CANVAS_W, 5);    },    renderEnemyInCombat(cx, enemy, x, y) {        var t = Date.now() / 1000;
        var bounce = Math.sin(t * 2 + enemy.id) * 3;
        var colors = enemy.spriteColors;
        cx.fillStyle = colors[0];
        cx.fillRect(x, y + bounce, 64, 64);
        cx.fillStyle = colors[1];
        cx.fillRect(x + 8, y + 8 + bounce, 48, 40);
        cx.fillStyle = colors[2];
        cx.fillRect(x + 12, y + bounce, 40, 16);
        cx.fillStyle = '#ff0000';
        cx.fillRect(x + 16, y + 14 + bounce, 10, 8);
        cx.fillRect(x + 38, y + 14 + bounce, 10, 8);
        cx.fillStyle = '#000';
        cx.fillRect(x + 20, y + 16 + bounce, 4, 4);
        cx.fillRect(x + 42, y + 16 + bounce, 4, 4);
        cx.fillStyle = '#aaa';
        cx.fillRect(x + 4, y + 52 + bounce, 16, 12);
        cx.fillRect(x + 44, y + 52 + bounce, 16, 12);
        var hpPct = enemy.currentHp / enemy.hp;
        cx.fillStyle = '#8a1414';
        cx.fillRect(x, y - 10, 64, 6);
        cx.fillStyle = hpPct > 0.5 ? '#44ff44' : hpPct > 0.25 ? '#ffaa00' : '#ff4444';
        cx.fillRect(x, y - 10, 64 * hpPct, 6);
        cx.strokeStyle = '#4a2c8a';
        cx.lineWidth = 1;
        cx.strokeRect(x, y - 10, 64, 6);
        cx.fillStyle = '#fff';
        cx.font = '7px "Press Start 2P", monospace';
        cx.textAlign = 'center';
        cx.fillText(enemy.currentHp + '/' + enemy.hp, x + 32, y - 14);
        cx.textAlign = 'left';    },    renderBossInCombat(cx, boss, x, y) {        var t = Date.now() / 1000;
        var pulse = 1 + Math.sin(t * 3) * 0.02;
        var colors = boss.spriteColors;
        var phaseColor = ['#2d5a1e', '#6a4aaa', '#aa44ff', '#ff4444'][boss.currentPhase] || colors[0];
        cx.save();
        cx.translate(x + 48, y + 48);
        cx.scale(pulse, pulse);
        cx.translate(-48, -48);
        cx.fillStyle = phaseColor;
        cx.globalAlpha = 0.3;
        cx.fillRect(-10, -10, 116, 116);
        cx.globalAlpha = 1;
        cx.fillStyle = colors[0];
        cx.fillRect(8, 12, 80, 72);
        cx.fillStyle = colors[1];
        cx.fillRect(16, 24, 64, 40);
        cx.fillStyle = colors[2];
        cx.fillRect(4, 4, 88, 16);
        cx.fillStyle = '#ff0000';
        cx.fillRect(28, 30, 12, 12);
        cx.fillRect(56, 30, 12, 12);
        cx.fillStyle = '#ffff00';
        cx.fillRect(32, 34, 5, 5);
        cx.fillRect(60, 34, 5, 5);
        if (boss.currentPhase >= 2) {            for (var i = 0; i < 6; i++) {                var a = t * 2 + i * Math.PI / 3;
                var dx2 = Math.cos(a) * 50;
                var dy2 = Math.sin(a) * 50;
                cx.fillStyle = 'rgba(170,68,255,0.4)';
                cx.beginPath();
                cx.arc(48 + dx2, 48 + dy2, 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                cx.fill();            }
        }        cx.restore();
        var hpPct = boss.currentHp / boss.maxHp;
        cx.fillStyle = '#8a1414';
        cx.fillRect(x, y - 15, 96, 8);
        cx.fillStyle = '#ff4444';
        cx.fillRect(x, y - 15, 96 * hpPct, 8);
        cx.strokeStyle = '#c9a959';
        cx.lineWidth = 2;
        cx.strokeRect(x, y - 15, 96, 8);
        cx.fillStyle = '#c9a959';
        cx.font = '8px "Press Start 2P", monospace';
        cx.textAlign = 'center';
        cx.fillText(boss.name + ' P' + (boss.currentPhase + 1), x + 48, y - 20);
        cx.fillStyle = '#fff';
        cx.fillText(boss.currentHp + '/' + boss.maxHp, x + 48, y - 28);
        cx.textAlign = 'left';    },    renderPlayerInCombat(cx, p, x, y) {        if (!p) return;
        var t = Date.now() / 1000;
        var breathe = Math.sin(t * 3) * 2;
        var colors = SPRITE_COLORS[CLASS_NAMES[p.classId].toLowerCase()];
        if (!colors) return;
        cx.fillStyle = colors.armor;
        cx.fillRect(x + 8, y + 16 + breathe, 48, 32);
        cx.fillStyle = colors.skin;
        cx.fillRect(x + 14, y + 2 + breathe, 36, 18);
        cx.fillStyle = colors.hair;
        cx.fillRect(x + 12, y - 2 + breathe, 40, 10);
        cx.fillStyle = '#333';
        cx.fillRect(x + 22, y + 8 + breathe, 6, 6);
        cx.fillRect(x + 38, y + 8 + breathe, 6, 6);
        cx.fillStyle = '#c9a959';
        cx.fillRect(x + 28, y + 14 + breathe, 10, 3);
        cx.fillStyle = colors.detail;
        cx.fillRect(x + 12, y + 44 + breathe, 14, 16);
        cx.fillRect(x + 38, y + 44 + breathe, 14, 16);
        if (p.classId === CharacterClass.WARRIOR) {            cx.fillStyle = '#aaa';
            cx.fillRect(x - 2, y + 20 + breathe, 8, 24);
            cx.fillStyle = '#888';
            cx.fillRect(x - 4, y + 18 + breathe, 12, 6);        }
 else if (p.classId === CharacterClass.MAGE) {            cx.fillStyle = '#6688ff';
            cx.fillRect(x + 58, y + 10 + breathe, 4, 30);
            cx.fillStyle = '#aaa';
            cx.fillRect(x + 56, y + 8 + breathe, 8, 6);        }
 else {            cx.fillStyle = '#aaa';
            cx.fillRect(x + 60, y + 22 + breathe, 12, 4);
            cx.fillRect(x + 70, y + 20 + breathe, 4, 8);        }
        var hpPct = p.hp / p.maxHp;
        cx.fillStyle = '#8a1414';
        cx.fillRect(x - 10, y + 66, 84, 6);
        cx.fillStyle = '#ff4444';
        cx.fillRect(x - 10, y + 66, 84 * hpPct, 6);
        var mpPct = p.mp / p.maxMp;
        cx.fillStyle = '#14148a';
        cx.fillRect(x - 10, y + 74, 84, 4);
        cx.fillStyle = '#4444ff';
        cx.fillRect(x - 10, y + 74, 84 * mpPct, 4);    },    renderCombatUI(cx, combat) {        cx.fillStyle = 'rgba(10,5,18,0.9)';
        cx.fillRect(0, CANVAS_H - 110, CANVAS_W, 110);
        cx.strokeStyle = '#4a2c8a';
        cx.lineWidth = 2;
        cx.strokeRect(0, CANVAS_H - 110, CANVAS_W, 2);
        if (player) {            cx.fillStyle = '#c9a959';
            cx.font = '9px "Press Start 2P", monospace';
            cx.fillText(player.name + ' Lv.' + player.level, 15, CANVAS_H - 90);
TextRenderer.drawProgressBar(cx, 15, CANVAS_H - 78, 120, 8, player.hp, player.maxHp, '#8a1414', '#ff4444');
TextRenderer.drawProgressBar(cx, 15, CANVAS_H - 66, 120, 6, player.mp, player.maxMp, '#14148a', '#4444ff');
            cx.fillStyle = '#fff';
            cx.font = '7px "Press Start 2P", monospace';
            cx.fillText('HP:' + player.hp + '/' + player.maxHp, 140, CANVAS_H - 72);
            cx.fillText('MP:' + player.mp + '/' + player.maxMp, 140, CANVAS_H - 62);        }
    },    update(dt) {        this.animTimer += dt;
        if (this.flashTimer > 0) {            this.flashTimer -= dt;        }
        if (this.shakeX !== 0 || this.shakeY !== 0) {            this.shakeX *= 0.9;
            this.shakeY *= 0.9;
            if (Math.abs(this.shakeX) < 0.5) this.shakeX = 0;
            if (Math.abs(this.shakeY) < 0.5) this.shakeY = 0;        }
    },    flash() {        this.flashTimer = 0.2;    },    shake(intensity) {        this.shakeX = (Math.random() - 0.5) * (intensity || 10);
        this.shakeY = (Math.random() - 0.5) * (intensity || 10);    }
};
/* ============================================================   MAP SYSTEM - Transitions, Collision, NPC Placement, Chests   ============================================================ */const MapSystem = {    currentMap: null,    mapWidth: 0,    mapHeight: 0,    transitionZones: [],    npcPositions: {},    chestStates: {},    transitions: {        village: [            { x: 0, y: 6, w: 1, h: 3, target: 'darkForest', tx: 28, ty: 9, dir: Direction.RIGHT },            { x: 19, y: 6, w: 1, h: 3, target: 'darkForest', tx: 1, ty: 9, dir: Direction.LEFT },            { x: 9, y: 0, w: 3, h: 1, target: 'darkForest', tx: 14, ty: 18, dir: Direction.DOWN },            { x: 9, y: 14, w: 3, h: 1, target: 'darkForest', tx: 14, ty: 1, dir: Direction.UP }        ],        darkForest: [            { x: 0, y: 8, w: 1, h: 3, target: 'village', tx: 17, ty: 6, dir: Direction.RIGHT },            { x: 29, y: 8, w: 1, h: 3, target: 'ancientCaves', tx: 1, ty: 8, dir: Direction.LEFT }        ],        ancientCaves: [            { x: 0, y: 7, w: 1, h: 3, target: 'darkForest', tx: 27, ty: 9, dir: Direction.RIGHT },            { x: 29, y: 7, w: 1, h: 3, target: 'ruinedCastle', tx: 1, ty: 5, dir: Direction.LEFT },            { x: 12, y: 0, w: 3, h: 1, target: 'ruinedCastle', tx: 13, ty: 17, dir: Direction.DOWN }        ],        ruinedCastle: [            { x: 0, y: 4, w: 1, h: 3, target: 'ancientCaves', tx: 27, ty: 8, dir: Direction.RIGHT },            { x: 29, y: 4, w: 1, h: 3, target: 'shadowRealm', tx: 1, ty: 8, dir: Direction.LEFT },            { x: 12, y: 19, w: 3, h: 1, target: 'ancientCaves', tx: 13, ty: 1, dir: Direction.UP }        ],        shadowRealm: [            { x: 0, y: 7, w: 1, h: 3, target: 'ruinedCastle', tx: 27, ty: 5, dir: Direction.RIGHT }        ]    },    init() {        this.chestStates = {};
        this.npcPositions = {};    },    loadMap(areaName) {        this.currentMap = MapTileData[areaName];
        if (!this.currentMap) return false;
        this.mapWidth = this.currentMap.width;
        this.mapHeight = this.currentMap.height;
        this.transitionZones = this.transitions[areaName] || [];        Camera.init(this.mapWidth, this.mapHeight);
        return true;    },    getTile(x, y) {        if (!this.currentMap) return TileType.WALL;
        if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= this.mapWidth || y >= this.mapHeight) return TileType.WALL;
        return this.currentMap.tiles[y * this.mapWidth + x];    },    setTile(x, y, tileId) {        if (!this.currentMap) return;
        if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= this.mapWidth || y >= this.mapHeight) return;
        this.currentMap.tiles[y * this.mapWidth + x] = tileId;    },    isWalkable(x, y) {        var tile = this.getTile(x, y);
        return TILE_WALKABLE[tile] === true;    },    isBlocked(x, y) {        if (!this.isWalkable(x, y)) return true;
        if (this.currentMap && this.currentMap.npcs) {            for (var i = 0; i < this.currentMap.npcs.length; i++) {                var npc = this.currentMap.npcs[i];
                if (npc.x === x && npc.y === y) return true;            }
        }
        return false;    },    getMapNpcs() {        return this.currentMap ? (this.currentMap.npcs || []) : [];    },    getNpcAt(x, y) {        var npcs = this.getMapNpcs();
        for (var i = 0; i < npcs.length; i++) {            if (npcs[i].x === x && npcs[i].y === y) return npcs[i];        }
        return null;
},    getMapChests() {        return this.currentMap ? (this.currentMap.chests || []) : [];
},    isChestOpened(x, y) {        return this.chestStates[currentArea + '_' + x + '_' + y] === true;
},    openChest(x, y) {        var key = currentArea + '_' + x + '_' + y;
        this.chestStates[key] = true;        openChests.add(key);    },    getChestContents(x, y) {        var chests = this.getMapChests();
        for (var i = 0; i < chests.length; i++) {            if (chests[i].x === x && chests[i].y === y) return chests[i].contents;        }
        return null;    },    checkTransition(x, y) {        for (var i = 0; i < this.transitionZones.length; i++) {            var z = this.transitionZones[i];
            if (x >= z.x && x < z.x + z.w && y >= z.y && y < z.y + z.h) {                return z;            }
        }
        return null;    },    interactWithTile(x, y) {        var tile = this.getTile(x, y);
        if (tile === TileType.CHEST && !this.isChestOpened(x, y)) {            var contents = this.getChestContents(x, y);
            if (contents) {                this.openChest(x, y);
contents.forEach(function(c) {                    player.addItem(c.itemId, c.qty);
});
AudioSystem.playSound('chest_open');
CombatLog.add('Found items in the chest!');
                return { type: 'chest', contents: contents };            }
        }
        if (tile === TileType.DOOR) {            var trans = this.checkTransition(x, y);
            if (trans) {                AudioSystem.playSound('door_open');
                return { type: 'transition', transition: trans };            }
        }
        return null;    },    getTileVariation(x, y) {        return ((x * 7 + y * 13 + x * y * 3) % 100);    },    renderMapTiles(cx) {        if (!this.currentMap) return;
        var startTileX = Math.max(0, Math.floor(player.x - MAP_VIEW_TILES_X / 2));
        var startTileY = Math.max(0, Math.floor(player.y - MAP_VIEW_TILES_Y / 2));
        var offsetX = Math.floor((CANVAS_W - MAP_VIEW_TILES_X * TILE_SIZE) / 2);
        var offsetY = Math.floor((CANVAS_H - MAP_VIEW_TILES_Y * TILE_SIZE) / 2);
        for (var ty = 0; ty < MAP_VIEW_TILES_Y + 1; ty++) {            for (var tx = 0; tx < MAP_VIEW_TILES_X + 1; tx++) {                var mapX = startTileX + tx;
                var mapY = startTileY + ty;
                var tile = this.getTile(mapX, mapY);
                var drawX = offsetX + tx * TILE_SIZE;
                var drawY = offsetY + ty * TILE_SIZE;                SpriteRenderer.drawTileDetailed(cx, tile, drawX, drawY, this.getTileVariation(mapX, mapY));            }
        }
    },    renderNpcs(cx) {        if (!this.currentMap || !this.currentMap.npcs) return;
        var startTileX = Math.max(0, Math.floor(player.x - MAP_VIEW_TILES_X / 2));
        var startTileY = Math.max(0, Math.floor(player.y - MAP_VIEW_TILES_Y / 2));
        var offsetX = Math.floor((CANVAS_W - MAP_VIEW_TILES_X * TILE_SIZE) / 2);
        var offsetY = Math.floor((CANVAS_H - MAP_VIEW_TILES_Y * TILE_SIZE) / 2);
        this.currentMap.npcs.forEach(function(npc) {            var npcData = NPCDatabase.find(function(n) { return n.id === npc.npcId; });
            if (!npcData) return;
            var screenX = offsetX + (npc.x - startTileX) * TILE_SIZE;
            var screenY = offsetY + (npc.y - startTileY) * TILE_SIZE;
            if (screenX < -TILE_SIZE * 2 || screenX > CANVAS_W + TILE_SIZE) return;
            if (screenY < -TILE_SIZE * 2 || screenY > CANVAS_H + TILE_SIZE) return;
SpriteRenderer.drawNpc(cx, npcData, screenX, screenY, TILE_SIZE);
});
},    renderPlayerSprite(cx) {        if (!player) return;
        var startTileX = Math.max(0, Math.floor(player.x - MAP_VIEW_TILES_X / 2));
        var startTileY = Math.max(0, Math.floor(player.y - MAP_VIEW_TILES_Y / 2));
        var offsetX = Math.floor((CANVAS_W - MAP_VIEW_TILES_X * TILE_SIZE) / 2);
        var offsetY = Math.floor((CANVAS_H - MAP_VIEW_TILES_Y * TILE_SIZE) / 2);
        var screenX = offsetX + (player.x - startTileX) * TILE_SIZE;
        var screenY = offsetY + (player.y - startTileY) * TILE_SIZE;
        var frame = player.isMoving ? SpriteRenderer.walkFrame : 0;
SpriteRenderer.drawPlayer(cx, player.classId, player.direction, frame, screenX, screenY, TILE_SIZE);
},    renderDayNightOverlay(cx) {        var tint = GameEngine.getDayNightTint();
        if (tint !== 'rgb(255,255,255)') {            cx.fillStyle = tint;
            cx.globalAlpha = 0.15;
            cx.fillRect(0, 0, CANVAS_W, CANVAS_H);
            cx.globalAlpha = 1;        }
    },    getAreaMusic(areaName) {        var musicMap = {            village: 'village',            darkForest: 'forest',            ancientCaves: 'ancientCaves',            ruinedCastle: 'ruinedCastle',            shadowRealm: 'shadowRealm'        };
        return musicMap[areaName] || 'village';
},    getAreaEnemies(areaName) {        return AREA_ENEMIES[areaName] || [];
},    getMapByName(name) {        var normalized = name.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, '');
        for (var key in MapTileData) {            if (key.toLowerCase() === normalized) return key;        }
        return null;    }
};
/* ============================================================   PLAYER SYSTEM - Creation, Movement, Stats, Level Up   ============================================================ */const PlayerSystem = {    moveTimer: 0,    moveSpeed: 0.15,    isMoving: false,    moveProgress: 0,    prevX: 0,    prevY: 0,    targetX: 0,    targetY: 0,    facingNPC: null,    facingTile: null,    interactCooldown: 0,    createPlayer(classId) {        player = new Player(classId);
        player.isMoving = false;
        return player;    },    initStartingItems() {        if (!player) return;
        if (player.classId === CharacterClass.WARRIOR) {            player.addItem(100, 1);        }
 else if (player.classId === CharacterClass.MAGE) {            player.addItem(200, 1);        }
 else {            player.addItem(300, 1);        }        player.addItem(0, 3);
        player.addItem(4, 2);    },    update(dt) {        if (!player || gameState !== GameState.EXPLORE) return;
        if (GameEngine.updateFreeze(dt)) return;
        this.interactCooldown = Math.max(0, this.interactCooldown - dt);        SpriteRenderer.updateAnimation(dt);
        if (this.isMoving) {            this.moveProgress += dt / this.moveSpeed;
            if (this.moveProgress >= 1) {                this.moveProgress = 1;
                this.isMoving = false;
                player.x = this.targetX;
                player.y = this.targetY;
                this.onArrive();            }
 else {                player.visualX = this.prevX + (this.targetX - this.prevX) * this.easeInOut(this.moveProgress);
                player.visualY = this.prevY + (this.targetY - this.prevY) * this.easeInOut(this.moveProgress);            }            return;        }        this.handleMovementInput(dt);
        this.checkFacingInteraction();    },    handleMovementInput(dt) {        var dir = InputHandler.getMovementDirection();
        if (dir.dx === 0 && dir.dy === 0) return;
        var newX = player.x + dir.dx;
        var newY = player.y + dir.dy;
        if (dir.dy < 0) player.direction = Direction.UP;
        else if (dir.dy > 0) player.direction = Direction.DOWN;
        else if (dir.dx < 0) player.direction = Direction.LEFT;
        else if (dir.dx > 0) player.direction = Direction.RIGHT;
        if (MapSystem.isBlocked(newX, newY)) return;
        this.prevX = player.x;
        this.prevY = player.y;
        this.targetX = newX;
        this.targetY = newY;
        this.isMoving = true;
        this.moveProgress = 0;
        player.isMoving = true;    },    onArrive() {        player.isMoving = false;
        player.visualX = player.x;
        player.visualY = player.y;
        var transition = MapSystem.checkTransition(player.x, player.y);
        if (transition) {            this.changeArea(transition.target, transition.tx, transition.ty);            return;        }
        var tileResult = MapSystem.interactWithTile(player.x, player.y);
        if (tileResult && tileResult.type === 'transition') {            this.changeArea(tileResult.transition.target, tileResult.transition.tx, tileResult.transition.ty);            return;        }
        if (MapSystem.getTile(player.x, player.y) === TileType.CHEST) {            var key = currentArea + '_' + player.x + '_' + player.y;
            if (!openChests.has(key)) {                openChests.add(key);                MapSystem.openChest(player.x, player.y);
                var contents = MapSystem.getChestContents(player.x, player.y);
                if (contents) {                    contents.forEach(function(c) {                        player.addItem(c.itemId, c.qty);
});
AudioSystem.playSound('chest_open');
CombatLog.add('Found items in the chest!');
}
            }
        }
        var npc = MapSystem.getNpcAt(player.x, player.y);
        if (!npc) {            var nearNpc = this.getNearbyNpc();
            if (nearNpc) {                CanvasHUD.renderInteractPrompt = function(cx) {                    cx.fillStyle = 'rgba(10,5,18,0.8)';
                    cx.fillRect(CANVAS_W / 2 - 80, CANVAS_H - 80, 160, 24);
                    cx.strokeStyle = '#c9a959';
                    cx.lineWidth = 1;
                    cx.strokeRect(CANVAS_W / 2 - 80, CANVAS_H - 80, 160, 24);
                    cx.fillStyle = '#c9a959';
                    cx.font = '8px "Press Start 2P", monospace';
                    cx.textAlign = 'center';
                    cx.fillText('[E] Talk', CANVAS_W / 2, CANVAS_H - 64);
                    cx.textAlign = 'left';                };            }
        }
        if (GameEngine.checkRandomEncounter()) {            var enemyList = MapSystem.getAreaEnemies(currentArea);
            if (enemyList.length > 0) {                var numEnemies = 1 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 3);
                var encounterIds = [];
                for (var i = 0; i < numEnemies; i++) {                    encounterIds.push(enemyList[Math.floor(Math.random() * enemyList.length)]);
}                AudioSystem.playSound('boss_encounter');
GameEngine.onBattleStart();
Combat.startBattle(encounterIds, false);
}
        }        Quest.updateObjective('move_' + currentArea, 1);
        var enemies = MapSystem.getAreaEnemies(currentArea);
        if (enemies.indexOf(1) !== -1 && currentArea === 'darkForest') {            if (Math.random() < 0.1) Quest.updateObjective('herb', 1);
}        Minimap.render();
UI.updateHUD();
},    changeArea(newArea, px, py) {        AudioSystem.playSound('area_transition');
currentArea = newArea;
        player.x = px;
        player.y = py;
        player.visualX = px;
        player.visualY = py;
        this.isMoving = false;
        this.moveProgress = 0;        MapSystem.loadMap(newArea);        Camera.centerOn(px, py);        UI.updateHUD();        Minimap.render();
        var music = MapSystem.getAreaMusic(newArea);        AudioSystem.fadeInMusic(music, 0.5);    },    getNearbyNpc() {        var npcs = MapSystem.getMapNpcs();
        for (var i = 0; i < npcs.length; i++) {            var npc = npcs[i];
            var dx = Math.abs(player.x - npc.x);
            var dy = Math.abs(player.y - npc.y);
            if (dx <= 1 && dy <= 1) return npc;        }
        return null;    },    checkFacingInteraction() {        if (this.interactCooldown > 0) return;
        var dirVec = DIR_OFFSETS[player.direction];
        var faceX = player.x + dirVec.x;
        var faceY = player.y + dirVec.y;
        this.facingTile = MapSystem.getTile(faceX, faceY);
        var npc = MapSystem.getNpcAt(faceX, faceY);
        this.facingNPC = npc;
        if (InputHandler.isActionPressed('interact') || InputHandler.isKeyJustPressed('e') || InputHandler.isKeyJustPressed('E')) {            if (npc) {                this.interactCooldown = 0.3;
                var npcData = NPCDatabase.find(function(n) { return n.id === npc.npcId; });
                if (npcData) {                    AudioSystem.playSound('menu_select');
                    this.handleNpcInteraction(npcData);                }
            }
 else if (this.facingTile === TileType.CHEST) {                var key = currentArea + '_' + faceX + '_' + faceY;
                if (!openChests.has(key)) {                    this.interactCooldown = 0.3;                    openChests.add(key);                    MapSystem.openChest(faceX, faceY);
                    var contents = MapSystem.getChestContents(faceX, faceY);
                    if (contents) {                        contents.forEach(function(c) { player.addItem(c.itemId, c.qty); });
AudioSystem.playSound('chest_open');
CombatLog.add('Found items in the chest!');
}
                }
            }
 else if (this.facingTile === TileType.DOOR) {                this.interactCooldown = 0.3;
                var trans = MapSystem.checkTransition(faceX, faceY);
                if (trans) {                    this.changeArea(trans.target, trans.tx, trans.ty);                }
            }
        }
    },    handleNpcInteraction(npcData) {        if (npcData.role === 'shop_items') {            var items = ShopInventory.fiona_consumables.slice();
UI.openShop('Fiona\'s Shop', items);
}
 else if (npcData.role === 'shop_upgrade') {            var shopItems = [];
            if (player.classId === CharacterClass.WARRIOR) {                shopItems = ShopInventory.marcus_warrior_weapons.concat(ShopInventory.marcus_warrior_armor);            }
 else if (player.classId === CharacterClass.MAGE) {                shopItems = ShopInventory.fiona_mage_weapons.concat(ShopInventory.fiona_mage_armor);            }
 else {                shopItems = ShopInventory.marcus_rogue_weapons.concat(ShopInventory.marcus_rogue_armor);            }
            if (player.level >= 15) shopItems = shopItems.concat(ShopInventory.late_shop_weapons, ShopInventory.late_shop_armor);            UI.openShop('Marcus\'s Forge', shopItems);        }
 else if (npcData.role === 'healer') {            player.hp = player.maxHp;
            player.mp = player.maxMp;
            player.statusEffects = [];            AudioSystem.playSound('healing');            CombatLog.add('Fully healed by Healer Lyra!');            UI.updateHUD();        }
 else if (npcData.role === 'inn') {            if (player.gold >= 20) {                player.gold -= 20;
                player.hp = player.maxHp;
                player.mp = player.maxMp;
                player.statusEffects = [];
AudioSystem.playSound('healing');
CombatLog.add('Rested at the inn. Fully restored!');
UI.updateHUD();
}
 else {                CombatLog.add('Not enough gold to rest!');            }
        }
 else if (npcData.role === 'trainer') {            UI.openSkills();        }
 else if (npcData.role === 'quest_giver') {            DialogueSystem.startNPC(npcData.id);        }
 else {            DialogueSystem.startNPC(npcData.id);        }
    },    levelUp() {        if (!player) return;        AudioSystem.playSound('level_up');
        var cx = document.getElementById('gameCanvas').getContext('2d');
        var screenX = CANVAS_W / 2;
        var screenY = CANVAS_H / 2;
ParticleSystem.emitLevelUp(screenX, screenY);
CombatLog.add('Level Up! Now level ' + player.level + '!');
},    gainExp(amount) {        if (!player) return;
        player.gainExp(amount);        UI.updateHUD();    },    gainGold(amount) {        if (!player) return;
        player.gold += amount;        UI.updateHUD();    },    heal(amount) {        if (!player) return;
        var before = player.hp;
        player.heal(amount);
        var healed = player.hp - before;
        if (healed > 0) AudioSystem.playSound('healing');        UI.updateHUD();    },    useMana(amount) {        if (!player) return false;
        if (player.mp < amount) return false;
        player.mp -= amount;        UI.updateHUD();
        return true;    },    recalcStats() {        if (!player) return;
        var base = BASE_STATS[player.classId];
        var growth = STAT_GROWTH[player.classId];
        var lvl = player.level - 1;
        player.maxHp = base.hp + Math.floor(growth.hp * lvl);
        player.maxMp = base.mp + Math.floor(growth.mp * lvl);
        player.atk = base.atk + Math.floor(growth.atk * lvl);
        player.def = base.def + Math.floor(growth.def * lvl);
        player.int = base.int + Math.floor(growth.int * lvl);
        player.spd = base.spd + Math.floor(growth.spd * lvl);
        player.luck = base.luck + Math.floor(growth.luck * lvl);
        if (player.weapon) {            player.atk += player.weapon.atk || 0;
            player.def += player.weapon.def || 0;
            player.maxHp += player.weapon.hp || 0;
            player.maxMp += player.weapon.mp || 0;
            player.spd += player.weapon.spd || 0;
            player.luck += player.weapon.luck || 0;        }
        if (player.armor) {            player.atk += player.armor.atk || 0;
            player.def += player.armor.def || 0;
            player.maxHp += player.armor.hp || 0;
            player.maxMp += player.armor.mp || 0;
            player.spd += player.armor.spd || 0;
            player.luck += player.armor.luck || 0;        }
        if (player.accessory) {            player.atk += player.accessory.atk || 0;
            player.def += player.accessory.def || 0;
            player.maxHp += player.accessory.hp || 0;
            player.maxMp += player.accessory.mp || 0;
            player.spd += player.accessory.spd || 0;
            player.luck += player.accessory.luck || 0;        }        player.hp = Math.min(player.hp, player.maxHp);
        player.mp = Math.min(player.mp, player.maxMp);
},    easeInOut(t) {        return t < 0.5 ? 2 * t * t : 1 - Math.pow(-2 * t + 2, 2) / 2;
},    getExpToLevel(level) {        return level * 100 + level * level * 10;
}
};
/* ============================================================   INTEGRATION - Override Game Loop, Connect All Systems   ============================================================ */(function() {    var _origGameLoop = gameLoop;
    var _origHandlePlayerMovement = handlePlayerMovement;
    var _origCheckAreaTransitions = checkAreaTransitions;
    var _origCheckRandomEncounter = checkRandomEncounter;    InputHandler.init();    AudioSystem.init();    GameEngine.init();
    document.addEventListener('click', function() {        AudioSystem.resume();    }, { once: true });
    document.addEventListener('keydown', function() {        AudioSystem.resume();    }, { once: true });
    var _lastTime = performance.now();
    var _animFrame = 0;
gameLoop = function(timestamp) {        var dt = Math.min((timestamp - _lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
_lastTime = timestamp;
_animFrame++;
InputHandler.update();
GameEngine.updateFPS(dt);
        if (GameEngine.transitioning) {            GameEngine.updateTransitions(dt);
}        GameEngine.updateFlash(dt);
GameEngine.updateFreeze(dt);
GameEngine.updateClock(dt);
GameEngine.updateEncounterSystem(dt);
ParticleSystem.update(dt);
DamageNumbers.update(dt);
SpriteRenderer.updateAnimation(dt);
CombatRenderer.update(dt);
        if (gameState === GameState.EXPLORE && player && gameStarted) {            PlayerSystem.update(dt);
            var data = MapTileData[currentArea];
            if (data) {                Camera.follow(player.x, player.y);                Camera.update(dt);            }
            var ctx2 = document.getElementById('gameCanvas').getContext('2d');
ctx2.fillStyle = '#000';
ctx2.fillRect(0, 0, CANVAS_W, CANVAS_H);
MapSystem.renderMapTiles(ctx2);
MapSystem.renderNpcs(ctx2);
MapSystem.renderPlayerSprite(ctx2);
MapSystem.renderDayNightOverlay(ctx2);
ParticleSystem.render(ctx2);
DamageNumbers.render(ctx2);
CanvasHUD.renderAll(ctx2);
Minimap.render();
            if (player.facingNPC || PlayerSystem.facingNPC) {                CanvasHUD.renderInteractPrompt(ctx2, '[E] Talk');            }
            if (InputHandler.isActionPressed('inventory')) UI.openInventory();
            if (InputHandler.isActionPressed('skills')) UI.openSkills();
            if (InputHandler.isActionPressed('quests')) UI.openQuests();
            if (InputHandler.isActionPressed('map')) UI.openMap();
            if (InputHandler.isActionPressed('pause')) UI.openPause();
            if (InputHandler.isKeyJustPressed('c') || InputHandler.isKeyJustPressed('C')) {                UI.openCraft();
}            GameEngine.renderFlash(ctx2);
GameEngine.renderTransition(ctx2);
}
        if (gameState === GameState.COMBAT) {            var ctx3 = document.getElementById('gameCanvas').getContext('2d');
CombatRenderer.renderCombatScene(ctx3, Combat);
ParticleSystem.render(ctx3);
DamageNumbers.render(ctx3);
}
        if (gameState === GameState.TITLE) {            var ctx4 = document.getElementById('gameCanvas').getContext('2d');
ctx4.fillStyle = '#0a0512';
ctx4.fillRect(0, 0, CANVAS_W, CANVAS_H);
