            for (var i = 0; i < 30; i++) {                var px = (Math.sin(timestamp / 1000 + i * 0.7) * 300 + CANVAS_W / 2);
                var py = (Math.cos(timestamp / 800 + i * 1.3) * 200 + CANVAS_H / 2);
                var sz = Math.sin(timestamp / 500 + i) * 3 + 4;
                var alpha = 0.15 + Math.sin(timestamp / 1000 + i) * 0.1;
ctx4.fillStyle = 'rgba(170,68,255,' + alpha + ')';
ctx4.beginPath();
ctx4.arc(px, py, sz, 0, Math.PI * 2);
ctx4.fill();
}
            for (var j = 0; j < 15; j++) {                var sx2 = (Math.cos(timestamp / 1500 + j * 1.1) * 350 + CANVAS_W / 2);
                var sy2 = (Math.sin(timestamp / 1200 + j * 0.9) * 250 + CANVAS_H / 2);
                var sz2 = Math.sin(timestamp / 700 + j) * 2 + 2;
ctx4.fillStyle = 'rgba(201,169,89,' + (0.1 + Math.sin(timestamp / 2000 + j) * 0.05) + ')';
ctx4.beginPath();
ctx4.arc(sx2, sy2, sz2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
ctx4.fill();
}            ctx4.font = '10px "Press Start 2P", monospace';
ctx4.fillStyle = 'rgba(212,168,255,' + (0.5 + Math.sin(timestamp / 1000) * 0.3) + ')';
ctx4.textAlign = 'center';
ctx4.fillText('Press any key to begin...', CANVAS_W / 2, CANVAS_H - 60);
ctx4.textAlign = 'left';
GameEngine.renderTransition(ctx4);
}
        if (gameState === GameState.INVENTORY) {            var ctx5 = document.getElementById('gameCanvas').getContext('2d');
ctx5.fillStyle = '#0a0512';
ctx5.fillRect(0, 0, CANVAS_W, CANVAS_H);
drawInventoryCanvas(ctx5);
}
        if (gameState === GameState.SHOP) {            var ctx6 = document.getElementById('gameCanvas').getContext('2d');
ctx6.fillStyle = '#0a0512';
ctx6.fillRect(0, 0, CANVAS_W, CANVAS_H);
drawShopCanvas(ctx6);
}
        if (gameState === GameState.SKILLS) {            var ctx7 = document.getElementById('gameCanvas').getContext('2d');
ctx7.fillStyle = '#0a0512';
ctx7.fillRect(0, 0, CANVAS_W, CANVAS_H);
drawSkillsCanvas(ctx7);
}
        if (gameState === GameState.QUEST_LOG) {            var ctx8 = document.getElementById('gameCanvas').getContext('2d');
ctx8.fillStyle = '#0a0512';
ctx8.fillRect(0, 0, CANVAS_W, CANVAS_H);
drawQuestsCanvas(ctx8);
}
        if (gameState === GameState.DIALOGUE) {            var ctx9 = document.getElementById('gameCanvas').getContext('2d');
            if (MapTileData[currentArea]) {                MapSystem.renderMapTiles(ctx9);
MapSystem.renderNpcs(ctx9);
MapSystem.renderPlayerSprite(ctx9);
}            ParticleSystem.render(ctx9);
}        requestAnimationFrame(gameLoop);
};
    function drawInventoryCanvas(cx) {        cx.fillStyle = 'rgba(26,10,46,0.95)';
        cx.fillRect(50, 30, CANVAS_W - 100, CANVAS_H - 60);
        cx.strokeStyle = '#4a2c8a';
        cx.lineWidth = 3;
        cx.strokeRect(50, 30, CANVAS_W - 100, CANVAS_H - 60);
        cx.fillStyle = '#c9a959';
        cx.font = '14px "Press Start 2P", monospace';
        cx.textAlign = 'center';
        cx.fillText('INVENTORY', CANVAS_W / 2, 60);
        cx.textAlign = 'left';
        if (player) {            cx.font = '8px "Press Start 2P", monospace';
            cx.fillStyle = '#d4d4d4';
            cx.fillText('Gold: ' + player.gold, 70, 85);
            if (player.weapon) {                cx.fillStyle = '#ff6b6b';
                cx.fillText('Weapon: ' + player.weapon.name, 70, 105);            }
            if (player.armor) {                cx.fillStyle = '#6b9fff';
                cx.fillText('Armor: ' + player.armor.name, 70, 120);            }
            if (player.accessory) {                cx.fillStyle = '#6bff6b';
                cx.fillText('Accessory: ' + player.accessory.name, 70, 135);            }
            var items = player.inventory;
            var cols = 6;
            var slotSize = 44;
            var startX = 80;
            var startY = 160;
            for (var i = 0; i < Math.min(items.length, 30); i++) {                var col = i % cols;
                var row = Math.floor(i / cols);
                var sx = startX + col * (slotSize + 6);
                var sy = startY + row * (slotSize + 6);
                cx.fillStyle = '#0a0512';
                cx.fillRect(sx, sy, slotSize, slotSize);
                cx.strokeStyle = '#2d1b4e';
                cx.lineWidth = 2;
                cx.strokeRect(sx, sy, slotSize, slotSize);
                cx.fillStyle = '#c9a959';
                cx.font = '18px sans-serif';
                cx.textAlign = 'center';
                cx.fillText(ITEM_ICONS[items[i].type] || '?', sx + slotSize / 2, sy + slotSize / 2 + 6);
                cx.textAlign = 'left';            }
            for (var j = items.length; j < 30; j++) {                var col2 = j % cols;
                var row2 = Math.floor(j / cols);
                cx.fillStyle = '#0a0512';
                cx.fillRect(startX + col2 * (slotSize + 6), startY + row2 * (slotSize + 6), slotSize, slotSize);
                cx.strokeStyle = '#1a0a2e';
                cx.lineWidth = 1;
                cx.strokeRect(startX + col2 * (slotSize + 6), startY + row2 * (slotSize + 6), slotSize, slotSize);            }
        }        cx.fillStyle = '#888';
        cx.font = '8px "Press Start 2P", monospace';
        cx.textAlign = 'center';
        cx.fillText('Press ESC to close', CANVAS_W / 2, CANVAS_H - 50);
        cx.textAlign = 'left';    }

        cx.textAlign = 'center';

        cx.fillRect(30, 20, CANVAS_W - 60, CANVAS_H - 40);
        cx.strokeStyle = '#4a2c8a';

});
bSkills.sort(function(a, b) { return a.unlockLevel - b.unlockLevel; });

                    cx.fillText('Lv.' + skill.unlockLevel, bx + 10, sy + 40);                });            });        }        cx.fillStyle = '#888';
