    var _origConfirmClass = Game.confirmClass;    Game.confirmClass = function() {        _origConfirmClass();
        if (player) {            PlayerSystem.recalcStats();
            player.hp = player.maxHp;
            player.mp = player.maxMp;
            player.visualX = player.x;
            player.visualY = player.y;
MapSystem.loadMap(currentArea);
Camera.centerOn(player.x, player.y);
AudioSystem.fadeInMusic('village', 1);
UI.updateHUD();
}
    };
    var _origCheckRandomEncounter2 = checkRandomEncounter;
checkRandomEncounter = function() {        if (GameEngine.checkRandomEncounter()) {            var enemyList = MapSystem.getAreaEnemies(currentArea);
            if (enemyList.length > 0) {                var numEnemies = 1 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 3);
                var encounterIds = [];
                for (var i = 0; i < numEnemies; i++) {                    encounterIds.push(enemyList[Math.floor(Math.random() * enemyList.length)]);
}                AudioSystem.playSound('boss_encounter');
GameEngine.onBattleStart();
Combat.startBattle(encounterIds, false);
}
        }
    };
    var _origEndBattle = Combat.endBattle;    Combat.endBattle = function(won) {        if (won) {            var totalExp = 0;
            var totalGold = 0;
            if (this.bossBattle) {                totalExp = this.boss.level * 100;                totalGold = this.boss.level * 50;            }
 else {                this.enemies.forEach(function(e) {                    totalExp += e.exp;
totalGold += e.gold;
});
}            GameEngine.onBattleEnd(totalExp, totalGold);
AudioSystem.playSound('victory');
            var cx2 = document.getElementById('gameCanvas').getContext('2d');            ParticleSystem.emitVictory(CANVAS_W / 2, CANVAS_H / 2);            Camera.shake(5, 0.3);        }
 else {            AudioSystem.playSound('death');        }        _origEndBattle.call(Combat, won);    };
    var _origStartBattle = Combat.startBattle;    Combat.startBattle = function(enemyIds, isBoss, bossId) {        _origStartBattle.call(Combat, enemyIds, isBoss, bossId);
        if (isBoss) {            AudioSystem.fadeOutMusic(0.3);            setTimeout(function() { AudioSystem.playMusic('boss'); }, 500);        }
 else {            AudioSystem.fadeOutMusic(0.2);            setTimeout(function() { AudioSystem.playMusic(currentArea); }, 300);        }
    };
    var _origPlayerAttack = Combat.playerAttack;    Combat.playerAttack = function() {        if (!this.playerTurn) return;
        var targets = this.bossBattle ? [this.boss] : this.enemies.filter(function(e) { return e.isAlive(); });
        if (targets.length === 0) { this.endBattle(true); return; }        this.showTargetSelection(targets, function(target) {            var damage = player.getStat('atk') + Math.floor(Math.random() * 5);
            var isCrit = Math.random() * 100 < player.getStat('luck');
            var finalDmg = isCrit ? damage * 2 : damage;
            var actual = target.takeDamage(finalDmg);
            if (isCrit) {                AudioSystem.playSound('critical_hit');                CombatRenderer.shake(15);                CombatRenderer.flash();
                var cx2 = document.getElementById('gameCanvas').getContext('2d');                ParticleSystem.emitCritical(CANVAS_W / 2, CANVAS_H / 3);            }
 else {                AudioSystem.playSound('sword_slash');
CombatRenderer.shake(5);
}            CombatLog.add(player.name + ' attacks ' + target.name + ' for ' + actual + ' damage!' + (isCrit ? ' CRITICAL!' : ''));
Combat.renderEnemies();
Combat.currentTurn++;
setTimeout(function() { Combat.processTurn(); }, 600);
});
};
    var _origEnemyAction = Combat.enemyAction;
Combat.enemyAction = function(enemy) {        AudioSystem.playSound('damage_taken');
CombatRenderer.shake(8);
_origEnemyAction.call(Combat, enemy);
};
    var _origUseSkill = Combat.useSkill;    Combat.useSkill = function(skill) {        AudioSystem.playSound('magic_cast');        CombatRenderer.shake(6);
        if (skill.element === Element.FIRE) {            ParticleSystem.emitFire(CANVAS_W / 2, CANVAS_H / 3);        }
 else if (skill.element === Element.ICE) {            ParticleSystem.emitIce(CANVAS_W / 2, CANVAS_H / 3);        }
 else if (skill.element === Element.LIGHTNING) {            ParticleSystem.emitLightning(CANVAS_W / 2, CANVAS_H / 3);        }
 else if (skill.element === Element.SHADOW) {            ParticleSystem.emitShadow(CANVAS_W / 2, CANVAS_H / 3);        }
 else if (skill.type === 'heal') {            ParticleSystem.emitHeal(CANVAS_W / 2, CANVAS_H - 200);        }        _origUseSkill.call(Combat, skill);    };
    var _origUseItem = Combat.useItem;
Combat.useItem = function(item) {        if (item.subtype === 'heal_hp' || item.subtype === 'heal_both' || item.subtype === 'full_heal') {            AudioSystem.playSound('healing');
ParticleSystem.emitHeal(CANVAS_W / 2, CANVAS_H - 200);
}
 else if (item.subtype === 'damage') {            AudioSystem.playSound('magic_cast');
ParticleSystem.emitFire(CANVAS_W / 2, CANVAS_H / 3);
}        _origUseItem.call(Combat, item);
};
    var _origGainExp = Player.prototype.gainExp;    Player.prototype.gainExp = function(amount) {        _origGainExp.call(this, amount);
        while (this.exp >= this.getExpToNext() && this.level < 40) {            this.exp -= this.getExpToNext();
            this.levelUp();            PlayerSystem.levelUp();        }
    };
    var _origDialogueClose = DialogueSystem.close;    DialogueSystem.close = function() {        _origDialogueClose();
        if (MapTileData[currentArea]) {            var music = MapSystem.getAreaMusic(currentArea);
            if (!AudioSystem.musicPlaying) {                AudioSystem.fadeInMusic(music, 0.5);            }
        }
    };
    var _origStartNPC = DialogueSystem.startNPC;    DialogueSystem.startNPC = function(npcId) {        var npc = NPCDatabase.find(function(n) { return n.id === npcId; });
        if (!npc) return;
AudioSystem.playSound('menu_select');
_origStartNPC.call(DialogueSystem, npcId);
};
window.addEventListener('beforeunload', function() {        if (player && gameStarted) {            var data = {                classId: player.classId, level: player.level, exp: player.exp, gold: player.gold,                hp: player.hp, maxHp: player.maxHp, mp: player.mp, maxMp: player.maxMp,                atk: player.atk, def: player.def, int: player.int, spd: player.spd, luck: player.luck,                skills: player.skills, skillPoints: player.skillPoints,                weapon: player.weapon ? player.weapon.id : null,                armor: player.armor ? player.armor.id : null,                accessory: player.accessory ? player.accessory.id : null,                inventory: player.inventory.map(function(i) { return i.id; }),                area: currentArea, x: player.x, y: player.y,                className: CLASS_NAMES[player.classId],                timestamp: Date.now(), questFlags: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(questFlags)),                openChests: Array.from(openChests)            };
            try {                localStorage.setItem('ros_auto_save', JSON.stringify(data));            }
 catch (e) {}
        }
    });})();
/* ============================================================   ENEMY AI SYSTEM - Behavior Patterns, Boss AI, Target Selection   ============================================================ */const EnemyAI = {    patterns: {        aggressive: function(enemy, targets) {            var bestSkill = 'Attack';
            var bestPower = 0;
            var skills = enemy.skills || ['Attack'];            skills.forEach(function(skillName) {                var power = enemy.atk;
                var magicWords = ['Fire','Ice','Shadow','Dark','Bolt','Nova','Storm','Beam','Thunder','Curse','Drain'];
                var isMagic = false;                magicWords.forEach(function(w) { if (skillName.indexOf(w) !== -1) isMagic = true; });
                if (isMagic) power = enemy.int * 1.5;
                if (power > bestPower) { bestPower = power; bestSkill = skillName; }
            });
            var target = EnemyAI.selectTarget(targets);
            return { skill: bestSkill, target: target, isMagic: EnemyAI.isMagicSkill(bestSkill) };
},        cautious: function(enemy, targets) {            var hpRatio = enemy.currentHp / enemy.hp;
            if (hpRatio < 0.3) {                return { skill: 'Defend', target: enemy, isMagic: false };            }
            var skills = enemy.skills || ['Attack'];
            return { skill: skills[0], target: EnemyAI.selectTarget(targets), isMagic: EnemyAI.isMagicSkill(skills[0]) };
},        tactical: function(enemy, targets) {            var target = EnemyAI.selectWeakestTarget(targets);
            var skills = enemy.skills || ['Attack'];
            var debuffSkills = skills.filter(function(s) {                return s.indexOf('Curse') !== -1 || s.indexOf('Drain') !== -1 || s.indexOf('Web') !== -1 || s.indexOf('Poison') !== -1;
});
            if (debuffSkills.length > 0 && target && (!target.statusEffects || target.statusEffects.length === 0)) {                return { skill: debuffSkills[0], target: target, isMagic: true };
}
            return { skill: skills[Math.floor(Math.random() * skills.length)], target: target, isMagic: EnemyAI.isMagicSkill(skills[0]) };
},        berserker: function(enemy, targets) {            var skills = enemy.skills || ['Attack'];
            var hpRatio = enemy.currentHp / enemy.hp;
            var idx = Math.min(skills.length - 1, Math.floor((1 - hpRatio) * skills.length));
            return { skill: skills[idx], target: EnemyAI.selectTarget(targets), isMagic: EnemyAI.isMagicSkill(skills[idx]) };
},        summoner: function(enemy, targets, allies) {            if (allies && allies.length < 4 && Math.random() < 0.35) {                return { skill: 'Summon', target: null, isMagic: true };
}
            var skills = enemy.skills || ['Attack'];
            return { skill: skills[Math.floor(Math.random() * skills.length)], target: EnemyAI.selectTarget(targets), isMagic: EnemyAI.isMagicSkill(skills[0]) };
},        mage: function(enemy, targets) {            var skills = enemy.skills || ['Attack'];
            var magicSkills = skills.filter(function(s) { return EnemyAI.isMagicSkill(s); });
            if (magicSkills.length > 0) {                var useSkill = magicSkills[Math.floor(Math.random() * magicSkills.length)];
                var hpRatio = enemy.currentHp / enemy.hp;
                if (hpRatio < 0.35) {                    var healSkill = magicSkills.filter(function(s) { return s.indexOf('Heal') !== -1 || s.indexOf('Drain') !== -1; });
                    if (healSkill.length > 0) useSkill = healSkill[0];                }
                return { skill: useSkill, target: EnemyAI.selectTarget(targets), isMagic: true };            }
            return { skill: 'Attack', target: EnemyAI.selectTarget(targets), isMagic: false };        }
    },    isMagicSkill(skillName) {        var magicWords = ['Fire','Ice','Shadow','Dark','Bolt','Nova','Storm','Beam','Thunder','Curse','Drain','Life','Siphon','Blast','Surge','Terror','Wail','Flare','Rend','Rift','Gaze','Trap','Knell','Ball','Barrier','Pulse','Fang','Breath','Roar','Claw','Bellow','Whip','Slam','Smash','Charge','Slash','Bite','Tackle','Punch','Kick','Throw','Strike','Bash','Thrust','Cut'];
        var nonMagic = ['Slash','Bite','Tackle','Punch','Kick','Throw','Bash','Thrust','Cut','Headbutt','Ram','Stomp','Swipe','Rake','Gore','Lash','Claw','Bite','Tongue','Web','Root','Timber','Rock','Smash','Club','Horn','Smash'];
        for (var i = 0; i < nonMagic.length; i++) {            if (skillName.indexOf(nonMagic[i]) !== -1) return false;        }
        for (var j = 0; j < magicWords.length; j++) {            if (skillName.indexOf(magicWords[j]) !== -1) return true;        }
        return false;    },    selectTarget(targets) {        if (!targets || targets.length === 0) return null;
        var alive = targets.filter(function(t) { return t.isAlive ? t.isAlive() : t.currentHp > 0; });
        if (alive.length === 0) return null;        alive.sort(function(a, b) { return a.currentHp - b.currentHp; });
        return alive[0];    },    selectWeakestTarget(targets) {        if (!targets || targets.length === 0) return null;
        var alive = targets.filter(function(t) { return t.isAlive ? t.isAlive() : t.currentHp > 0; });
        if (alive.length === 0) return null;        alive.sort(function(a, b) {            var aPower = (a.atk || 0) + (a.int || 0) * 1.5;
            var bPower = (b.atk || 0) + (b.int || 0) * 1.5;
            return bPower - aPower;        });
        return alive[0];    },    selectAction(enemy, targets, allies) {        var pattern = this.getPattern(enemy);
        var behavior = this.patterns[pattern];
        if (!behavior) behavior = this.patterns.aggressive;
        return behavior(enemy, targets, allies);    },    getPattern(enemy) {        if (enemy.aiPattern) return enemy.aiPattern;
        if (enemy.int > enemy.atk * 1.2) return 'mage';
        if (enemy.spd > 15 && enemy.luck > 8) return 'tactical';
        if (enemy.hp > 200) return 'berserker';
        if (enemy.level < 3) return 'aggressive';
        if (enemy.name.indexOf('Witch') !== -1 || enemy.name.indexOf('Sorcerer') !== -1) return 'mage';
        if (enemy.name.indexOf('Goblin') !== -1) return 'tactical';
        if (enemy.name.indexOf('Troll') !== -1 || enemy.name.indexOf('Minotaur') !== -1) return 'berserker';
        if (enemy.name.indexOf('Wraith') !== -1 || enemy.name.indexOf('Fiend') !== -1) return 'summoner';
        return 'aggressive';    },    bossAI(boss, phase) {        var phaseData = boss.getPhase();
        var behavior = phaseData.behavior || 'balanced';
        var skills = phaseData.skills || ['Attack'];
        var hpRatio = boss.currentHp / boss.maxHp;
        var result = { skill: skills[0], target: 'player', isMagic: false };
        switch (behavior) {            case 'attack_heavy':                result.skill = skills[skills.length - 1];                break;
            case 'summon_magic':                result.skill = hpRatio > 0.5 ? skills[0] : skills[Math.floor(Math.random() * skills.length)];                break;
            case 'berserk':                result.skill = hpRatio < 0.5 ? skills[skills.length - 1] : skills[0];                break;
            case 'magic_heavy':                var magicOnly = skills.filter(function(s) { return EnemyAI.isMagicSkill(s); });
result.skill = magicOnly.length > 0 ? magicOnly[Math.floor(Math.random() * magicOnly.length)] : skills[0];
result.isMagic = true;
break;
            case 'summon_drain':                result.skill = hpRatio < 0.5 ? skills[Math.min(1, skills.length - 1)] : skills[0];                break;
            case 'ultimate':                result.skill = hpRatio < 0.4 ? skills[skills.length - 1] : skills[Math.floor(Math.random() * Math.min(3, skills.length))];                break;
            case 'balanced':                result.skill = skills[Math.floor(Math.random() * skills.length)];                break;
            case 'magic_extreme':                result.skill = skills[Math.floor(Math.random() * skills.length)];                result.isMagic = true;                break;
            case 'all_elements':                result.skill = skills[Math.floor(Math.random() * skills.length)];
result.isMagic = EnemyAI.isMagicSkill(result.skill);
break;
            case 'desperation':                result.skill = hpRatio < 0.5 ? skills[0] : skills[Math.floor(Math.random() * skills.length)];
break;
default:                result.skill = skills[Math.floor(Math.random() * skills.length)];
}        result.isMagic = EnemyAI.isMagicSkill(result.skill);
        return result;    },    selectEnemySkill(enemy) {        var skills = enemy.skills || ['Attack'];
        if (enemy.currentMp !== undefined) {            var usable = skills.filter(function(s) {                var sd = SkillDatabase.find(function(d) { return d.name === s; });
                if (sd && enemy.currentMp < sd.mpCost) return false;
                return true;            });
            if (usable.length > 0) return usable[Math.floor(Math.random() * usable.length)];        }
        return skills[Math.floor(Math.random() * skills.length)];    },    getDodgeChance(enemy, attacker) {        var baseChance = 5;        baseChance += (enemy.spd - (attacker.spd || 0)) * 2;
        if (enemy.statusEffects) {            enemy.statusEffects.forEach(function(e) {                if (e.type === StatusEffect.HASTE) baseChance += 10;
                if (e.type === StatusEffect.BLIND) baseChance += 15;
                if (e.type === StatusEffect.FREEZE) baseChance -= 50;
                if (e.type === StatusEffect.STUN) baseChance -= 100;            });        }
        return Math.max(0, Math.min(40, baseChance));    }
};
/* ============================================================   ENHANCED COMBAT SYSTEM - Formulas, Elements, Multi-Hit, Defend   ============================================================ */const CombatEnhanced = {    combatState: 'IDLE',    COMBAT_STATES: {        IDLE: 'IDLE', INTRO: 'INTRO', PLAYER_TURN: 'PLAYER_TURN', ENEMY_TURN: 'ENEMY_TURN',        SKILL_SELECT: 'SKILL_SELECT', TARGET_SELECT: 'TARGET_SELECT', ITEM_SELECT: 'ITEM_SELECT',        ANIMATING: 'ANIMATING', ENEMY_DEAD: 'ENEMY_DEAD', PLAYER_DEAD: 'PLAYER_DEAD',        VICTORY: 'VICTORY', DEFEAT: 'DEFEAT', FLEE_SUCCESS: 'FLEE_SUCCESS', BOSS_INTRO: 'BOSS_INTRO'    },    animating: false,    animTimer: 0,    pendingAction: null,    turnCount: 0,    combatRewards: { exp: 0, gold: 0, items: [] },    getElementMultiplier(attackElement, defenderElement) {        if (attackElement === Element.NONE || defenderElement === Element.NONE) return 1.0;
        if (ELEMENT_WEAKNESS[attackElement] === defenderElement) return 1.5;
        if (ELEMENT_WEAKNESS[defenderElement] === attackElement) return 0.6;
        if (attackElement === Element.EARTH && defenderElement === Element.LIGHTNING) return 0.5;
        if (attackElement === Element.LIGHTNING && defenderElement === Element.EARTH) return 0.5;
        return 1.0;    },    calcPhysicalDamage(attacker, defender, power, isCrit) {        var atk = attacker.getStat ? attacker.getStat('atk') : attacker.atk;
        var def = defender.getStat ? defender.getStat('def') : defender.def;
        var baseDmg = (atk * 2 - def) * (power / 100);
        var variance = 0.85 + Math.random() * 0.30;
        var critMult = isCrit ? 2.0 : 1.0;
        var defendMult = 1.0;
        if (defender.statusEffects) {            defender.statusEffects.forEach(function(e) {                if (e.type === StatusEffect.SHIELD) defendMult *= 0.5;
                if (e.type === StatusEffect.HASTE) atk *= 0.9;            });        }
        return Math.max(1, Math.floor(baseDmg * variance * critMult * defendMult));
},    calcMagicDamage(attacker, defender, power, element) {        var int = attacker.getStat ? attacker.getStat('int') : attacker.int;
        var defInt = defender.getStat ? defender.getStat('int') : (defender.int || 1);
        var baseDmg = (int * 2.5 - defInt * 0.5) * (power / 100);
        var elemMult = this.getElementMultiplier(element, defender.element || Element.NONE);
        var variance = 0.9 + Math.random() * 0.2;
        var defendMult = 1.0;
        if (defender.statusEffects) {            defender.statusEffects.forEach(function(e) {                if (e.type === StatusEffect.SHIELD) defendMult *= 0.5;            });        }
        return Math.max(1, Math.floor(baseDmg * elemMult * variance * defendMult));    },    calcHealAmount(caster, power) {        var int = caster.getStat ? caster.getStat('int') : caster.int;
        var base = int * 2 + (power || 0);
        var variance = 1.0 + Math.random() * 0.2;
        return Math.floor(base * variance);    },    calcCritChance(attacker) {        var luck = attacker.getStat ? attacker.getStat('luck') : attacker.luck;
        var baseCrit = 5 + luck * 0.8;
        if (attacker.statusEffects) {            attacker.statusEffects.forEach(function(e) {                if (e.type === StatusEffect.HASTE) baseCrit += 5;            });        }
        return Math.min(50, baseCrit);    },    calcFleeChance(playerUnit, enemies) {        var playerSpd = playerUnit.getStat ? playerUnit.getStat('spd') : playerUnit.spd;
        var avgEnemySpd = 0;
        var count = 0;        enemies.forEach(function(e) {            if (e.isAlive()) { avgEnemySpd += e.spd; count++; }
        });
        if (count > 0) avgEnemySpd /= count;
        var chance = 50 + (playerSpd - avgEnemySpd) * 5;
        return Math.max(10, Math.min(90, chance));
},    getSpeedOrder(playerUnit, enemies, boss) {        var units = [];
units.push({ type: 'player', spd: playerUnit.getStat('spd'), unit: playerUnit });
        if (boss && boss.isAlive()) {            units.push({ type: 'boss', spd: boss.spd, unit: boss });        }
 else {            enemies.forEach(function(e, i) {                if (e.isAlive()) units.push({ type: 'enemy', index: i, spd: e.spd, unit: e });
});
}        units.sort(function(a, b) { return b.spd - a.spd; });
        return units;    },    processStatusEffects(unit) {        if (!unit.statusEffects) return [];
        var msgs = [];
        var toRemove = [];
unit.statusEffects.forEach(function(effect, idx) {            switch (effect.type) {                case StatusEffect.POISON:                    var poisonDmg = Math.max(1, Math.floor(unit.hp * 0.08));
unit.hp = Math.max(0, unit.hp - poisonDmg);
msgs.push(unit.name + ' takes ' + poisonDmg + ' poison damage!');
break;
                case StatusEffect.BURN:                    var burnDmg = Math.max(1, Math.floor(unit.hp * 0.1));
unit.hp = Math.max(0, unit.hp - burnDmg);
msgs.push(unit.name + ' takes ' + burnDmg + ' burn damage!');
break;
                case StatusEffect.REGEN:                    var regenAmt = Math.max(1, Math.floor(unit.maxHp * 0.06));
unit.hp = Math.min(unit.maxHp, unit.hp + regenAmt);
msgs.push(unit.name + ' recovers ' + regenAmt + ' HP!');
break;
                case StatusEffect.FREEZE:                    if (Math.random() < 0.15) { toRemove.push(idx); msgs.push(unit.name + ' broke free from ice!'); }                    break;
                case StatusEffect.STUN:                    if (Math.random() < 0.25) { toRemove.push(idx); msgs.push(unit.name + ' recovered from stun!'); }                    break;
                case StatusEffect.BLIND:                    if (Math.random() < 0.2) { toRemove.push(idx); msgs.push(unit.name + ' can see again!'); }                    break;
}            effect.turns--;
            if (effect.turns <= 0) toRemove.push(idx);        });
        for (var i = toRemove.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {            unit.statusEffects.splice(toRemove[i], 1);        }
        return msgs;    },    applyStatusEffect(target, type, duration, chance) {        if (!target.statusEffects) target.statusEffects = [];
        if (Math.random() * 100 > (chance || 100)) return false;
        var existing = target.statusEffects.find(function(e) { return e.type === type; });
        if (existing) { existing.turns = Math.max(existing.turns, duration || 3);
 return true; }        target.statusEffects.push({ type: type, turns: duration || 3 });
        return true;    },    isUnitStunned(unit) {        if (!unit.statusEffects) return false;
        return unit.statusEffects.some(function(e) { return e.type === StatusEffect.STUN || e.type === StatusEffect.FREEZE; });
},    isUnitBlinded(unit) {        if (!unit.statusEffects) return false;
        return unit.statusEffects.some(function(e) { return e.type === StatusEffect.BLIND; });    },    calcMultiHit(attacker, defender, skill) {        var hits = skill.hits || 1;
        var results = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < hits; i++) {            var hitChance = (skill.accuracy || 95) - (this.isUnitBlinded(attacker) ? 30 : 0);
            if (Math.random() * 100 > hitChance) { results.push({ hit: false, damage: 0 }); continue; }
            var isCrit = Math.random() * 100 < this.calcCritChance(attacker);
            var dmg;
            if (skill.type === 'magical') {                dmg = this.calcMagicDamage(attacker, defender, skill.power * (1 - i * 0.1), skill.element);            }
 else {                dmg = this.calcPhysicalDamage(attacker, defender, skill.power * (1 - i * 0.1), isCrit);            }            results.push({ hit: true, damage: dmg, crit: isCrit });        }
        return results;    },    calculateRewards(enemies, isBoss, boss) {        var totalExp = 0, totalGold = 0, drops = [];
        if (isBoss && boss) {            totalExp = boss.level * 100;            totalGold = boss.level * 50;
            if (boss.currentPhase >= boss.phases.length - 1) {                drops.push({ itemId: 712, qty: 1 });            }
        }
 else {            enemies.forEach(function(e) {                if (!e.isAlive()) return;                totalExp += e.exp || 0;                totalGold += e.gold || 0;
                if (e.dropTable) {                    e.dropTable.forEach(function(drop) {                        if (Math.random() * 100 < drop.chance) {                            drops.push({ itemId: drop.itemId, qty: 1 });
}
                    });                }
            });        }
        var luckBonus = 1 + (player.luck * 0.01);        totalGold = Math.floor(totalGold * luckBonus);
        if (player.accessory && player.accessory.id === 714) {            totalGold = Math.floor(totalGold * 1.5);
}        this.combatRewards = { exp: totalExp, gold: totalGold, items: drops };
        return this.combatRewards;    },    getVictoryDisplay() {        var r = this.combatRewards;
        var lines = ['Victory!', 'EXP: ' + r.exp, 'Gold: ' + r.gold];
        if (r.items.length > 0) {            r.items.forEach(function(item) {                var db = ItemDatabase.find(function(i) { return i.id === item.itemId; });
                if (db) lines.push('Item: ' + db.name);            });        }
        return lines;
},    getDefeatDisplay() {        return ['Defeated...', 'Your party has fallen.', 'Load a save or return to title.'];
},    getBossIntroDialogue(bossId) {        var boss = BossDatabase.find(function(b) { return b.id === bossId; });
        if (!boss) return ['A powerful enemy appears!'];
        var phase = boss.phases[0];
        return phase.dialogue || ['Prepare yourself!'];    },    animateAttack(type, callback) {        this.animating = true;
        this.animTimer = 0;
        var duration = 0;
        switch (type) {            case 'slash': duration = 0.4; AudioSystem.playSound('sword_slash'); break;
            case 'magic': duration = 0.6; AudioSystem.playSound('magic_cast'); break;
            case 'heal': duration = 0.5; AudioSystem.playSound('healing'); break;
            case 'item': duration = 0.3; AudioSystem.playSound('item_pickup'); break;
            case 'defend': duration = 0.3; break;            default: duration = 0.4;        }
        var self = this;        setTimeout(function() {            self.animating = false;
            if (callback) callback();        }, duration * 1000);    },    startCombat(enemyIds, isBoss, bossId) {        this.combatState = this.COMBAT_STATES.INTRO;
        this.turnCount = 0;
        this.combatRewards = { exp: 0, gold: 0, items: [] };
        var self = this;        setTimeout(function() {            self.combatState = self.COMBAT_STATES.PLAYER_TURN;        }, 1000);    }
};
/* ============================================================   ENHANCED INVENTORY SYSTEM - Sorting, Filtering, Use Items   ============================================================ */const InventoryEnhanced = {    maxSlots: 40,    currentFilter: 'all',    sortMode: 'type',    addItem(itemId, qty) {        if (!player) return false;
qty = qty || 1;
        var template = ItemDatabase.find(function(i) { return i.id === itemId; });
        if (!template) return false;
        var existing = null;
        if (template.type === 'consumable') {            existing = player.inventory.find(function(i) { return i.id === itemId; });        }
        if (existing && template.type === 'consumable') {            existing.quantity = (existing.quantity || 1) + qty;        }
 else {            for (var i = 0; i < qty; i++) {                if (player.inventory.length >= this.maxSlots) return false;
                var newItem = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(template));
newItem.uid = Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9);
newItem.quantity = template.type === 'consumable' ? qty : 1;
                player.inventory.push(newItem);
                if (template.type !== 'consumable') break;            }
        }        AudioSystem.playSound('item_pickup');
        return true;    },    removeItem(itemId, qty) {        if (!player) return false;        qty = qty || 1;
        var template = ItemDatabase.find(function(i) { return i.id === itemId; });
        if (!template) return false;
        var removed = 0;
        for (var i = player.inventory.length - 1; i >= 0 && removed < qty; i--) {            if (player.inventory[i].id === itemId) {                if (template.type === 'consumable') {                    player.inventory[i].quantity = (player.inventory[i].quantity || 1) - 1;
                    if (player.inventory[i].quantity <= 0) player.inventory.splice(i, 1);                }
 else {                    player.inventory.splice(i, 1);                }                removed++;            }
        }
        return removed >= qty;    },    useItem(itemId, target) {        if (!player) return false;
        var item = player.inventory.find(function(i) { return i.id === itemId; });
        if (!item) return false;
        if (item.type !== 'consumable') return false;
        var tgt = target || player;
        var msgs = [];
        switch (item.subtype) {            case 'heal_hp':                var healAmt = item.healAmount || 50;
                var before = tgt.hp;                tgt.hp = Math.min(tgt.maxHp, tgt.hp + healAmt);
                var healed = tgt.hp - before;                msgs.push(tgt.name + ' restored ' + healed + ' HP!');                AudioSystem.playSound('healing');                break;
            case 'heal_mp':                var mpAmt = item.healAmount || 30;
                var mpBefore = tgt.mp;
tgt.mp = Math.min(tgt.maxMp, tgt.mp + mpAmt);
msgs.push(tgt.name + ' restored ' + (tgt.mp - mpBefore) + ' MP!');
AudioSystem.playSound('healing');
break;
            case 'heal_both':                var hAmt = item.healAmount || 200;
                var mAmt = item.healAmountMP || 100;
                var hBefore = tgt.hp;
                var mBefore = tgt.mp;
tgt.hp = Math.min(tgt.maxHp, tgt.hp + hAmt);
tgt.mp = Math.min(tgt.maxMp, tgt.mp + mAmt);
msgs.push(tgt.name + ' restored ' + (tgt.hp - hBefore) + ' HP and ' + (tgt.mp - mBefore) + ' MP!');
AudioSystem.playSound('healing');
break;
            case 'full_heal':                tgt.hp = tgt.maxHp;
tgt.mp = tgt.maxMp;
tgt.statusEffects = [];
msgs.push(tgt.name + ' fully restored!');
AudioSystem.playSound('healing');
break;
            case 'cure':                if (tgt.statusEffects) {                    tgt.statusEffects = tgt.statusEffects.filter(function(e) { return e.type !== item.cureStatus; });
}                msgs.push('Status cured!');
AudioSystem.playSound('healing');
break;
            case 'cure_all':                tgt.statusEffects = [];                msgs.push('All status effects removed!');                AudioSystem.playSound('healing');                break;
            case 'revive':                if (tgt.hp <= 0) {                    tgt.hp = Math.floor(tgt.maxHp * (item.healAmount || 0.5));
msgs.push(tgt.name + ' has been revived!');
AudioSystem.playSound('healing');
}
 else {                    msgs.push('Target is not fallen!');
                    return false;                }                break;
            case 'damage':                msgs.push(item.name + ' deals ' + item.healAmount + ' damage!');                AudioSystem.playSound('magic_cast');                break;
            case 'escape':                msgs.push('Escaped from battle!');
break;
default:                msgs.push('Used ' + item.name + '!');
}        this.removeItem(itemId, 1);
        return msgs;    },    sortInventory(mode) {        if (!player) return;
        this.sortMode = mode || 'type';
        var typeOrder = { weapon: 0, armor: 1, accessory: 2, consumable: 3 };
        player.inventory.sort(function(a, b) {            switch (InventoryEnhanced.sortMode) {                case 'type':                    var ta = typeOrder[a.type] || 9;
                    var tb = typeOrder[b.type] || 9;
                    if (ta !== tb) return ta - tb;
                    return (a.name || '').localeCompare(b.name || '');
                case 'name':                    return (a.name || '').localeCompare(b.name || '');
                case 'quantity':                    return (b.quantity || 1) - (a.quantity || 1);
                case 'value':                    return (b.sellPrice || 0) - (a.sellPrice || 0);                default:                    return 0;            }
        });    },    getFiltered(filter) {        if (!player) return [];
        this.currentFilter = filter || 'all';
        if (filter === 'all') return player.inventory;
        return player.inventory.filter(function(i) { return i.type === filter; });    },    hasItem(itemId) {        if (!player) return false;
        return player.inventory.some(function(i) { return i.id === itemId; });    },    getItemCount(itemId) {        if (!player) return 0;
        var count = 0;
        player.inventory.forEach(function(i) {            if (i.id === itemId) count += (i.quantity || 1);        });
        return count;    },    getTooltipText(item) {        if (!item) return '';
        var lines = [item.name];        lines.push(item.type.toUpperCase());
        if (item.classReq !== undefined) lines.push('Class: ' + CLASS_NAMES[item.classReq]);
        if (item.description) lines.push(item.description);
        if (item.type !== 'consumable') {            var stats = [];
            if (item.atk) stats.push('ATK +' + item.atk);
            if (item.def) stats.push('DEF +' + item.def);
            if (item.hp) stats.push('HP ' + (item.hp > 0 ? '+' : '') + item.hp);
            if (item.mp) stats.push('MP +' + item.mp);
            if (item.spd) stats.push('SPD +' + item.spd);
            if (item.luck) stats.push('LUCK +' + item.luck);
            if (item.element !== Element.NONE) lines.push('Element: ' + ELEMENT_NAMES[item.element]);
            if (stats.length > 0) lines.push(stats.join(' | '));        }
        if (item.price) lines.push('Value: ' + item.price + 'g');
        return lines;    }
};
/* ============================================================   ENHANCED EQUIPMENT SYSTEM - Full Equip/Unequip, Best Gear   ============================================================ */const EquipmentEnhanced = {    slots: ['weapon', 'armor', 'accessory'],    equipItem(itemId, slot) {        if (!player) return false;
        var item = ItemDatabase.find(function(i) { return i.id === itemId; });
        if (!item) return false;
        if (item.classReq !== undefined && item.classReq !== player.classId) return false;
        if (item.type === 'consumable') return false;
        var targetSlot = slot || item.type;
        if (this.slots.indexOf(targetSlot) === -1) return false;
        var currentEquip = player[targetSlot];
        if (currentEquip) {            player.inventory.push(currentEquip);        }        player[targetSlot] = item;
        var idx = -1;
        for (var i = 0; i < player.inventory.length; i++) {            if (player.inventory[i].id === itemId && player.inventory[i].type !== 'consumable') {                idx = i;
break;
}
        }
        if (idx > -1) player.inventory.splice(idx, 1);        PlayerSystem.recalcStats();        UI.updateHUD();        AudioSystem.playSound('item_pickup');
        return true;    },    unequipSlot(slot) {        if (!player || !player[slot]) return false;
        var item = player[slot];        player[slot] = null;
        player.inventory.push(item);        PlayerSystem.recalcStats();        UI.updateHUD();
        return true;    },    getEquipped(slot) {        if (!player) return null;
        return player[slot] || null;    },    getEquipmentBonus(stat) {        if (!player) return 0;
        var bonus = 0;
        this.slots.forEach(function(slot) {            var item = player[slot];
            if (item && item[stat]) bonus += item[stat];        });
        return bonus;    },    isEquipmentBetter(itemId, currentSlot) {        var newItem = ItemDatabase.find(function(i) { return i.id === itemId; });
        var currentItem = this.getEquipped(currentSlot);
        if (!newItem) return false;
        if (!currentItem) return true;
        var scoreWeight = { atk: 2, def: 2, int: 1.5, hp: 0.5, mp: 0.5, spd: 1, luck: 0.8 };
        var newScore = 0, oldScore = 0;
        for (var stat in scoreWeight) {            newScore += (newItem[stat] || 0) * scoreWeight[stat];            oldScore += (currentItem[stat] || 0) * scoreWeight[stat];        }
        return newScore > oldScore;    },    suggestBestGear() {        if (!player) return [];
        var suggestions = [];
        var typeMap = { weapon: 'weapon', armor: 'armor', accessory: 'accessory' };
        var self = this;
        this.slots.forEach(function(slot) {            var equipable = player.inventory.filter(function(i) {                return i.type === typeMap[slot] && (!i.classReq || i.classReq === player.classId);
});
equipable.sort(function(a, b) {                var scoreWeight = { atk: 2, def: 2, int: 1.5, hp: 0.5, mp: 0.5, spd: 1, luck: 0.8 };
                var sa = 0, sb = 0;
                for (var stat in scoreWeight) {                    sa += (a[stat] || 0) * scoreWeight[stat];                    sb += (b[stat] || 0) * scoreWeight[stat];                }
                return sb - sa;            });
            if (equipable.length > 0 && self.isEquipmentBetter(equipable[0].id, slot)) {                suggestions.push({ slot: slot, item: equipable[0] });            }
        });
        return suggestions;    },    getWeaponElementColor() {        var w = this.getEquipped('weapon');
        if (!w) return '#888';
        var ec = { 1: '#ff4444', 2: '#44ccff', 3: '#ffff44', 4: '#88aa44', 5: '#aa44ff', 6: '#ffeedd' };
        return ec[w.element] || '#aaa';    },    getEquipmentSummary() {        if (!player) return '';
        var parts = [];
        if (player.weapon) parts.push('W: ' + player.weapon.name);
        if (player.armor) parts.push('A: ' + player.armor.name);
        if (player.accessory) parts.push('R: ' + player.accessory.name);
        return parts.length > 0 ? parts.join(' | ') : 'No equipment';    },    sellItem(itemId) {        if (!player) return 0;
        var item = player.inventory.find(function(i) { return i.id === itemId && i.type !== 'consumable'; });
        if (!item) return 0;
        var sellPrice = item.sellPrice || Math.floor((item.price || 0) * 0.5);
        var idx = player.inventory.indexOf(item);
        if (idx > -1) player.inventory.splice(idx, 1);
        player.gold += sellPrice;        PlayerSystem.recalcStats();        UI.updateHUD();        AudioSystem.playSound('item_pickup');
        return sellPrice;    }
};
/* ============================================================   ENHANCED SKILL SYSTEM - Learning, Cooldowns, Buffs, Passives   ============================================================ */const SkillSystemEnhanced = {    cooldowns: {},    passiveEffects: {},    buffTracker: {},    learnSkill(skillId) {        if (!player) return false;
        if (player.skills.indexOf(skillId) !== -1) return false;
        var skill = SkillDatabase.find(function(s) { return s.id === skillId; });
        if (!skill) return false;
        if (skill.class !== player.classId) return false;
        if (!skill.prerequisite || player.skills.indexOf(skill.prerequisite) !== -1) {            if (player.level >= skill.unlockLevel && player.skillPoints > 0) {                player.skills.push(skillId);
                player.skillPoints--;                AudioSystem.playSound('level_up');
                return true;            }
        }
        return false;    },    useSkill(skillId, user, targets) {        var skill = SkillDatabase.find(function(s) { return s.id === skillId; });
        if (!skill) return null;
        if (user.mp < skill.mpCost) return { success: false, message: 'Not enough MP!' };
        if (this.isOnCooldown(skillId)) return { success: false, message: 'Skill is on cooldown!' };        user.mp -= skill.mpCost;
        if (skill.cooldown) this.cooldowns[skillId] = skill.cooldown;
        var results = [];
        var self = this;
        if (skill.target === 'self') {            if (skill.type === 'buff') {                this.applyBuff(user, skill.statusEffect, 5, skill);
results.push({ target: user.name, type: 'buff', value: STATUS_NAMES[skill.statusEffect] });
}
 else if (skill.type === 'heal') {                var healAmt = CombatEnhanced.calcHealAmount(user, skill.power);
user.hp = Math.min(user.maxHp, user.hp + healAmt);
results.push({ target: user.name, type: 'heal', value: healAmt });
}
        }
 else if (skill.target === 'all') {            var hitTargets = Array.isArray(targets) ? targets : [targets];
hitTargets.forEach(function(t) {                if (!t || (!t.isAlive || !t.isAlive())) return;
                var accuracy = skill.accuracy || 95;
                if (Math.random() * 100 > accuracy) {                    results.push({ target: t.name, type: 'miss' });                    return;                }
                var dmg;
                if (skill.type === 'magical') {                    dmg = CombatEnhanced.calcMagicDamage(user, t, skill.power, skill.element);
                    var actual = t.takeMagicalDamage(dmg);                }
 else {                    var isCrit = Math.random() * 100 < CombatEnhanced.calcCritChance(user);                    dmg = CombatEnhanced.calcPhysicalDamage(user, t, skill.power, isCrit);
                    var actual = t.takeDamage(dmg);
                    if (isCrit) actual = Math.floor(actual * 1.5);
}                results.push({ target: t.name, type: 'damage', value: actual, element: skill.element, crit: isCrit });
                if (skill.statusEffect && skill.statusEffect !== StatusEffect.NONE) {                    var applied = CombatEnhanced.applyStatusEffect(t, skill.statusEffect, 3, skill.statusChance || 20);
                    if (applied) results.push({ target: t.name, type: 'status', value: STATUS_NAMES[skill.statusEffect] });                }
            });        }
 else {            var hitTargets = Array.isArray(targets) ? targets : [targets];            hitTargets.forEach(function(t) {                if (!t || (!t.isAlive || !t.isAlive())) return;
                var accuracy = skill.accuracy || 95;
                if (CombatEnhanced.isUnitBlinded(user)) accuracy -= 30;
                if (Math.random() * 100 > accuracy) {                    results.push({ target: t.name, type: 'miss' });                    return;                }
                var dmg;
                if (skill.type === 'magical') {                    dmg = CombatEnhanced.calcMagicDamage(user, t, skill.power, skill.element);
                    var actual = t.takeMagicalDamage(dmg);                }
 else {                    var hits = skill.hits || 1;
                    var totalDmg = 0;
                    for (var h = 0; h < hits; h++) {                        var isCrit = Math.random() * 100 < CombatEnhanced.calcCritChance(user);
                        var hitDmg = CombatEnhanced.calcPhysicalDamage(user, t, skill.power * (1 - h * 0.1), isCrit);
                        var actualHit = t.takeDamage(hitDmg);                        totalDmg += actualHit;
                        if (isCrit && h === 0) results.push({ target: t.name, type: 'crit' });                    }
                    var actual = totalDmg;                }                results.push({ target: t.name, type: 'damage', value: actual, element: skill.element });
                if (skill.statusEffect && skill.statusEffect !== StatusEffect.NONE) {                    var applied = CombatEnhanced.applyStatusEffect(t, skill.statusEffect, 3, skill.statusChance || 20);
                    if (applied) results.push({ target: t.name, type: 'status', value: STATUS_NAMES[skill.statusEffect] });                }
            });        }
        return { success: true, results: results, skill: skill };    },    applyBuff(target, buffType, duration, skill) {        if (!target.statusEffects) target.statusEffects = [];
        var existing = target.statusEffects.find(function(e) { return e.type === buffType; });
        if (existing) {            existing.turns = Math.max(existing.turns, duration || 5);        }
 else {            target.statusEffects.push({ type: buffType, turns: duration || 5 });        }
        if (!this.buffTracker[target.name]) this.buffTracker[target.name] = [];
        this.buffTracker[target.name].push({ type: buffType, turns: duration || 5, source: skill ? skill.name : 'unknown' });
},    removeBuff(target, buffType) {        if (!target || !target.statusEffects) return;
target.statusEffects = target.statusEffects.filter(function(e) { return e.type !== buffType; });
        if (this.buffTracker[target.name]) {            this.buffTracker[target.name] = this.buffTracker[target.name].filter(function(b) { return b.type !== buffType; });        }
    },    getAvailableSkills(classId) {        return SkillDatabase.filter(function(s) { return s.class === classId; });    },    getSkillTree(classId) {        var branches = {};
        var classSkills = this.getAvailableSkills(classId);
classSkills.forEach(function(skill) {            if (!branches[skill.branch]) branches[skill.branch] = [];
branches[skill.branch].push(skill);
});
        for (var branch in branches) {            branches[branch].sort(function(a, b) { return a.unlockLevel - b.unlockLevel; });        }
        return branches;
},    isOnCooldown(skillId) {        return this.cooldowns[skillId] && this.cooldowns[skillId] > 0;
},    tickCooldowns() {        for (var id in this.cooldowns) {            if (this.cooldowns[id] > 0) this.cooldowns[id]--;
            if (this.cooldowns[id] <= 0) delete this.cooldowns[id];        }
    },    getPassiveEffects(classId) {        var passives = {};
        switch (classId) {            case CharacterClass.WARRIOR:                passives.defBonus = 5;                passives.physResist = 0.05;                break;
            case CharacterClass.MAGE:                passives.mpRegen = 2;                passives.magicPower = 1.1;                break;
            case CharacterClass.ROGUE:                passives.critBonus = 8;                passives.dodgeBonus = 5;                break;        }
        return passives;    },    getSkillDescription(skillId) {        var skill = SkillDatabase.find(function(s) { return s.id === skillId; });
        if (!skill) return '';
        var desc = skill.description + '
';
desc += 'MP Cost: ' + skill.mpCost + '
';
desc += 'Power: ' + skill.power + '
';
desc += 'Accuracy: ' + skill.accuracy + '%
';
desc += 'Element: ' + ELEMENT_NAMES[skill.element] + '
';
desc += 'Target: ' + skill.target + '
';
        if (skill.statusEffect !== StatusEffect.NONE) {            desc += 'Status: ' + STATUS_NAMES[skill.statusEffect] + ' (' + skill.statusChance + '%)
';        }
        if (skill.hits && skill.hits > 1) desc += 'Hits: ' + skill.hits + '
';
        return desc;    }
};
/* ============================================================   ENHANCED NPC/DIALOGUE SYSTEM - Typewriter, Choices, State   ============================================================ */const DialogueEnhanced = {    state: 'INACTIVE',    DIALOGUE_STATES: { INACTIVE: 0, TALKING: 1, CHOOSING: 2, SHOPPING: 3, HEALING: 4, INN: 5 },    currentNPC: null,    currentNode: null,    typewriterText: '',    typewriterDisplay: '',    typewriterIndex: 0,    typewriterTimer: 0,    typewriterSpeed: 30,    typewriterComplete: false,    selectedChoice: 0,    dialogueHistory: {},    pendingAction: null,    startDialogue(npcId) {        var npc = NPCDatabase.find(function(n) { return n.id === npcId; });
        if (!npc) return false;
        this.currentNPC = npc;
        this.state = this.DIALOGUE_STATES.TALKING;
        var dialogueKey = this.getDialogueKey(npc);
        var node = npc.dialogue[dialogueKey] || npc.dialogue.default;
        this.currentNode = node;
        this.startTypewriter(node.text);
        return true;    },    getDialogueKey(npc) {        for (var qid in questFlags) {            if (qid.indexOf('quest_done_') === 0) {                var num = qid.replace('quest_done_', '');
                if (npc.dialogue['quest_complete_' + num]) return 'quest_complete_' + num;            }
        }
        for (var qid2 in questFlags) {            if (qid2.indexOf('quest_active_') === 0) {                var num2 = qid2.replace('quest_active_', '');
                if (npc.dialogue['quest_progress_' + num2]) return 'quest_progress_' + num2;            }
        }
        return 'default';    },    startTypewriter(text) {        this.typewriterText = text || '';
        this.typewriterDisplay = '';
        this.typewriterIndex = 0;
        this.typewriterTimer = 0;
        this.typewriterComplete = false;    },    updateTypewriter(dt) {        if (this.typewriterComplete || this.state === this.DIALOGUE_STATES.INACTIVE) return;
        this.typewriterTimer += dt * 1000;
        while (this.typewriterTimer >= this.typewriterSpeed && this.typewriterIndex < this.typewriterText.length) {            this.typewriterTimer -= this.typewriterSpeed;
            this.typewriterIndex++;
            this.typewriterDisplay = this.typewriterText.substring(0, this.typewriterIndex);        }
        if (this.typewriterIndex >= this.typewriterText.length) {            this.typewriterComplete = true;        }
    },    skipTypewriter() {        this.typewriterIndex = this.typewriterText.length;
        this.typewriterDisplay = this.typewriterText;
        this.typewriterComplete = true;    },    advanceDialogue() {        if (!this.typewriterComplete) {            this.skipTypewriter();            return;        }
        if (this.currentNode && this.currentNode.choices && this.currentNode.choices.length > 0) {            this.state = this.DIALOGUE_STATES.CHOOSING;        }
 else {            this.endDialogue();        }
    },    selectChoice(index) {        if (this.state !== this.DIALOGUE_STATES.CHOOSING) return;
        if (!this.currentNode || !this.currentNode.choices) { this.endDialogue(); return; }
        var choice = this.currentNode.choices[index];
        if (!choice) { this.endDialogue(); return; }
        if (choice.action) {            this.executeAction(choice.action);        }
        if (choice.next) {            var nextNode = this.currentNPC ? this.currentNPC.dialogue[choice.next] : null;
            if (nextNode) {                this.currentNode = nextNode;
                this.state = this.DIALOGUE_STATES.TALKING;
                this.startTypewriter(nextNode.text);            }
 else {                this.endDialogue();            }
        }
 else {            this.endDialogue();        }
    },    executeAction(action) {        if (action === 'shop_weapon' || action === 'shop_armor' || action === 'shop_accessory') {            this.state = this.DIALOGUE_STATES.SHOPPING;
            this.pendingAction = action;        }
 else if (action === 'heal') {            this.state = this.DIALOGUE_STATES.HEALING;
            player.hp = player.maxHp;
            player.mp = player.maxMp;
            player.statusEffects = [];            AudioSystem.playSound('healing');            UI.updateHUD();        }
 else if (action === 'rest') {            this.state = this.DIALOGUE_STATES.INN;
            if (player.gold >= 20) {                player.gold -= 20;
                player.hp = player.maxHp;
                player.mp = player.maxMp;
                player.statusEffects = [];                AudioSystem.playSound('healing');                UI.updateHUD();            }
        }
 else if (action === 'quest_accept') {            var npcDialogue = this.currentNPC ? this.currentNPC.dialogue : null;
            if (npcDialogue) {                QuestDatabase.forEach(function(q) {                    if (q.giver === (DialogueEnhanced.currentNPC ? DialogueEnhanced.currentNPC.name : '')) {                        if (!questFlags['quest_active_' + q.id] && !questFlags['quest_done_' + q.id]) {                            questFlags['quest_active_' + q.id] = true;
}
                    }
                });            }
        }
 else if (action === 'quest_complete') {            QuestDatabase.forEach(function(q) {                if (q.giver === (DialogueEnhanced.currentNPC ? DialogueEnhanced.currentNPC.name : '')) {                    if (questFlags['quest_active_' + q.id]) {                        var allDone = q.objectives.every(function(obj) {                            return (questFlags[q.id + '_' + obj.target] || 0) >= obj.required;
});
                        if (allDone) Quest.completeQuest(q.id);                    }
                }
            });        }
 else if (action.indexOf('start_boss_') === 0) {            var bossId = parseInt(action.replace('start_boss_', ''));
gameState = GameState.EXPLORE;
UI.showOverlay('gameHUD');
Combat.startBattle([], true, bossId);
}
 else if (action === 'new_game') {            Game.startNewGame();        }
 else if (action === 'title') {            UI.returnToTitle();        }
    },    getCurrentDialogue() {        return this.currentNode;    },    endDialogue() {        this.state = this.DIALOGUE_STATES.INACTIVE;
        this.currentNPC = null;
        this.currentNode = null;
        this.pendingAction = null;
gameState = GameState.EXPLORE;
UI.showOverlay('gameHUD');
},    isDialogueActive() {        return this.state !== this.DIALOGUE_STATES.INACTIVE;
},    getChoices() {        if (!this.currentNode || !this.currentNode.choices) return [];
        return this.currentNode.choices;
},    getSpeakerName() {        return this.currentNPC ? this.currentNPC.name : 'Narrator';
},    getPortraitColors() {        return this.currentNPC ? this.currentNPC.portraitColors : ['#888', '#666', '#444'];
}
};
/* ============================================================   ENHANCED QUEST SYSTEM - Progress, Auto-Complete, Notifications   ============================================================ */const QuestEnhanced = {    notifications: [],    maxNotifications: 5,    notificationTimer: 0,    startQuest(questId) {        if (!player) return false;
        var quest = QuestDatabase.find(function(q) { return q.id === questId; });
        if (!quest) return false;
        if (questFlags['quest_active_' + questId] || questFlags['quest_done_' + questId]) return false;
questFlags['quest_active_' + questId] = true;
quest.objectives.forEach(function(obj) {            questFlags[questId + '_' + obj.target] = questFlags[questId + '_' + obj.target] || 0;
});
        this.showNotification('Quest Accepted: ' + quest.name, 'quest_accept');        AudioSystem.playSound('item_pickup');
        return true;    },    completeQuest(questId) {        if (!player) return false;
        var quest = QuestDatabase.find(function(q) { return q.id === questId; });
        if (!quest) return false;
        if (!questFlags['quest_active_' + questId]) return false;
        var allDone = quest.objectives.every(function(obj) {            return (questFlags[questId + '_' + obj.target] || 0) >= obj.required;        });
        if (!allDone) return false;
        if (quest.rewards.exp) player.gainExp(quest.rewards.exp);
        if (quest.rewards.gold) player.gold += quest.rewards.gold;
        if (quest.rewards.items) {            quest.rewards.items.forEach(function(ri) {                InventoryEnhanced.addItem(ri.id, ri.qty || 1);
});
}        questFlags['quest_active_' + questId] = false;
questFlags['quest_done_' + questId] = true;
        this.showNotification('Quest Complete: ' + quest.name, 'quest_complete');        AudioSystem.playSound('victory');        UI.updateHUD();
        return true;
},    updateQuestProgress(type, target, amount) {        amount = amount || 1;
QuestDatabase.forEach(function(q) {            if (!questFlags['quest_active_' + q.id]) return;
q.objectives.forEach(function(obj) {                if (obj.target === target) {                    var key = q.id + '_' + target;
questFlags[key] = (questFlags[key] || 0) + amount;
}
                if (type === 'kill' && obj.target.indexOf('kill_') === 0) {                    var killType = obj.target.replace('kill_', '');
                    if (target.indexOf(killType) !== -1 || killType === 'forest' || killType === 'castle') {                        var kkey = q.id + '_' + obj.target;
questFlags[kkey] = (questFlags[kkey] || 0) + amount;
}
                }
            });
            this.checkAutoComplete(q.id);        }.bind(this));    },    checkAutoComplete(questId) {        var quest = QuestDatabase.find(function(q) { return q.id === questId; });
        if (!quest) return;
        if (!questFlags['quest_active_' + questId]) return;
        var allDone = quest.objectives.every(function(obj) {            return (questFlags[questId + '_' + obj.target] || 0) >= obj.required;        });
        if (allDone) {            this.showNotification('Quest Ready: ' + quest.name + ' (Return to NPC)', 'quest_ready');        }
    },    getActiveQuests() {        var active = [];        QuestDatabase.forEach(function(q) {            if (questFlags['quest_active_' + q.id]) active.push(q);        });
        return active;
},    getCompletedQuests() {        var completed = [];
QuestDatabase.forEach(function(q) {            if (questFlags['quest_done_' + q.id]) completed.push(q);
});
        return completed;
},    isQuestActive(questId) {        return !!questFlags['quest_active_' + questId];
},    isQuestCompleted(questId) {        return !!questFlags['quest_done_' + questId];
},    getQuestProgress(questId) {        var quest = QuestDatabase.find(function(q) { return q.id === questId; });
        if (!quest) return null;
        var progress = [];
quest.objectives.forEach(function(obj) {            var current = questFlags[questId + '_' + obj.target] || 0;
progress.push({                desc: obj.desc,                target: obj.target,                current: Math.min(current, obj.required),                required: obj.required,                done: current >= obj.required            });
});
        return { quest: quest, objectives: progress, isComplete: progress.every(function(p) { return p.done; }) };
},    showNotification(text, type) {        this.notifications.push({ text: text, type: type, timer: 4, alpha: 1 });
        if (this.notifications.length > this.maxNotifications) this.notifications.shift();
},    updateNotifications(dt) {        for (var i = this.notifications.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {            this.notifications[i].timer -= dt;
            if (this.notifications[i].timer < 1) {                this.notifications[i].alpha = this.notifications[i].timer;            }
            if (this.notifications[i].timer <= 0) {                this.notifications.splice(i, 1);            }
        }
    },    renderNotifications(cx) {        var ny = 140;
        this.notifications.forEach(function(n) {            cx.globalAlpha = n.alpha;
            var bgColor = n.type === 'quest_complete' ? 'rgba(26,58,26,0.9)' :                           n.type === 'quest_accept' ? 'rgba(45,27,78,0.9)' :                           n.type === 'quest_ready' ? 'rgba(58,58,10,0.9)' :                           'rgba(26,10,46,0.9)';
            var borderColor = n.type === 'quest_complete' ? '#6bff6b' :                              n.type === 'quest_accept' ? '#c9a959' :                              n.type === 'quest_ready' ? '#ffff44' : '#4a2c8a';
            cx.fillStyle = bgColor;
            cx.fillRect(CANVAS_W / 2 - 200, ny, 400, 28);
            cx.strokeStyle = borderColor;
            cx.lineWidth = 2;
            cx.strokeRect(CANVAS_W / 2 - 200, ny, 400, 28);
            cx.fillStyle = borderColor;
            cx.font = '8px "Press Start 2P", monospace';
            cx.textAlign = 'center';
            cx.fillText(n.text, CANVAS_W / 2, ny + 18);
            cx.textAlign = 'left';
            cx.globalAlpha = 1;            ny += 34;        });    }
};
/* ============================================================   ENHANCED SHOP SYSTEM - Buy/Sell, Gold Check, Shop Inventories   ============================================================ */const ShopEnhanced = {    currentShop: null,    shopType: 'general',    sellMode: false,    openShop(shopId, items) {        this.currentShop = { id: shopId, items: items || [] };
        this.shopType = shopId;
        this.sellMode = false;        gameState = GameState.SHOP;        UI.openShop(shopId, items);    },    buyItem(itemId) {        if (!player) return false;
        var item = ItemDatabase.find(function(i) { return i.id === itemId; });
        if (!item) return false;
        if (player.gold < item.price) {            CombatLog.add('Not enough gold!');
            return false;        }
        if (item.classReq !== undefined && item.classReq !== player.classId) {            CombatLog.add('Cannot equip: wrong class!');
            return false;
}        player.gold -= item.price;
InventoryEnhanced.addItem(itemId, 1);
AudioSystem.playSound('item_pickup');
CombatLog.add('Bought ' + item.name + '!');
UI.updateHUD();
        return true;    },    sellItem(itemId) {        if (!player) return false;
        var item = player.inventory.find(function(i) { return i.id === itemId; });
        if (!item) return false;
        var sellPrice = item.sellPrice || Math.floor((item.price || 0) * 0.5);
EquipmentEnhanced.sellItem(itemId);
AudioSystem.playSound('item_pickup');
CombatLog.add('Sold ' + item.name + ' for ' + sellPrice + ' gold!');
        return true;    },    getShopInventory(shopId) {        var inv = ShopInventory[shopId];
        if (!inv) return [];
        return inv.filter(function(itemId) {            var item = ItemDatabase.find(function(i) { return i.id === itemId; });
            if (!item) return false;
            if (item.classReq !== undefined && item.classReq !== player.classId) return false;
            return true;        });    },    getSellableItems() {        if (!player) return [];
        return player.inventory.filter(function(i) {            return i.type !== 'consumable' || true;
});
},    getBuyPrice(item) {        return item ? (item.price || 0) : 0;
},    getSellPrice(item) {        if (!item) return 0;
        return item.sellPrice || Math.floor((item.price || 0) * 0.5);    },    canAfford(itemId) {        var item = ItemDatabase.find(function(i) { return i.id === itemId; });
        return item && player && player.gold >= item.price;    },    getShopCategories(shopId) {        var items = this.getShopInventory(shopId);
        var categories = { weapons: [], armor: [], accessories: [], consumables: [] };
items.forEach(function(itemId) {            var item = ItemDatabase.find(function(i) { return i.id === itemId; });
            if (!item) return;
            if (item.type === 'weapon') categories.weapons.push(item);
            else if (item.type === 'armor') categories.armor.push(item);
            else if (item.type === 'accessory') categories.accessories.push(item);
            else if (item.type === 'consumable') categories.consumables.push(item);        });
        return categories;    }
};
/* ============================================================   ENHANCED CRAFTING SYSTEM - Recipes, Materials, Craft   ============================================================ */const CraftEnhanced = {    craft(recipeId) {        if (!player) return null;
        var recipe = CraftRecipes.find(function(r) { return r.id === recipeId; });
        if (!recipe) return null;
        if (player.gold < recipe.cost) return { success: false, message: 'Not enough gold!' };
        var canCraft = true;
        var missingMaterials = [];        recipe.ingredients.forEach(function(ing) {            if (ing.itemId && ing.qty > 0) {                var has = InventoryEnhanced.getItemCount(ing.itemId);
                if (has < ing.qty) {                    canCraft = false;
                    var matItem = ItemDatabase.find(function(i) { return i.id === ing.itemId; });
missingMaterials.push((matItem ? matItem.name : 'Unknown') + ' x' + (ing.qty - has));
}
            }
        });
        if (!canCraft) return { success: false, message: 'Missing: ' + missingMaterials.join(', ') };
        player.gold -= recipe.cost;
recipe.ingredients.forEach(function(ing) {            if (ing.itemId && ing.qty > 0) {                for (var i = 0; i < ing.qty; i++) {                    InventoryEnhanced.removeItem(ing.itemId, 1);
}
            }
        });
InventoryEnhanced.addItem(recipe.result.itemId, recipe.result.qty);
Quest.updateObjective('craft_potion', 1);
AudioSystem.playSound('item_pickup');
CombatLog.add('Crafted ' + recipe.name + '!');
UI.updateHUD();
        return { success: true, message: 'Crafted ' + recipe.name + '!' };
},    getAvailableRecipes() {        return CraftRecipes.filter(function(r) { return CraftEnhanced.canCraft(r.id); });
},    canCraft(recipeId) {        if (!player) return false;
        var recipe = CraftRecipes.find(function(r) { return r.id === recipeId; });
        if (!recipe) return false;
        if (player.gold < recipe.cost) return false;
        return recipe.ingredients.every(function(ing) {            if (!ing.itemId || ing.qty === 0) return true;
            return InventoryEnhanced.getItemCount(ing.itemId) >= ing.qty;
});
},    getCraftingMaterials(recipeId) {        var recipe = CraftRecipes.find(function(r) { return r.id === recipeId; });
        if (!recipe) return [];
        return recipe.ingredients.map(function(ing) {            var item = ItemDatabase.find(function(i) { return i.id === ing.itemId; });
            var have = ing.itemId ? InventoryEnhanced.getItemCount(ing.itemId) : 0;
            return {                name: item ? item.name : 'None',                required: ing.qty,                have: have,                sufficient: have >= ing.qty            };
});
},    getRecipeList() {        return CraftRecipes.map(function(r) {            var resultItem = ItemDatabase.find(function(i) { return i.id === r.result.itemId; });
            return {                id: r.id,                name: r.name,                description: r.description,                cost: r.cost,                resultItem: resultItem,                resultQty: r.result.qty,                materials: CraftEnhanced.getCraftingMaterials(r.id),                canCraft: CraftEnhanced.canCraft(r.id)            };
});
}
};
/* ============================================================   SAVE/LOAD SYSTEM - 3 Slots, Auto-Save, Full State   ============================================================ */const SaveLoadSystem = {    maxSlots: 3,    prefix: 'ros_save_',    saveGame(slot) {        if (!player || !gameStarted) return false;
slot = slot || 1;
        if (slot < 1 || slot > this.maxSlots) return false;
        var data = {            classId: player.classId,            level: player.level,            exp: player.exp,            gold: player.gold,            hp: player.hp,            maxHp: player.maxHp,            mp: player.mp,            maxMp: player.maxMp,            atk: player.atk,            def: player.def,            int: player.int,            spd: player.spd,            luck: player.luck,            skills: player.skills.slice(),            skillPoints: player.skillPoints,            weapon: player.weapon ? player.weapon.id : null,            armor: player.armor ? player.armor.id : null,            accessory: player.accessory ? player.accessory.id : null,            inventory: player.inventory.map(function(i) { return { id: i.id, quantity: i.quantity || 1 }; }),            area: currentArea,            x: player.x,            y: player.y,            className: CLASS_NAMES[player.classId],            timestamp: Date.now(),            questFlags: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(questFlags)),            openChests: Array.from(openChests),            playTime: GameEngine ? GameEngine.playTime : 0,            battleCount: GameEngine ? GameEngine.battleCount : 0        };
        try {            localStorage.setItem(this.prefix + slot, JSON.stringify(data));            AudioSystem.playSound('item_pickup');            CombatLog.add('Game saved to Slot ' + slot + '!');
            return true;        }
 catch (e) {            CombatLog.add('Save failed! Storage may be full.');
            return false;        }
    },    loadGame(slot) {        slot = slot || 1;
        if (slot < 1 || slot > this.maxSlots) return false;
        var raw = localStorage.getItem(this.prefix + slot);
        if (!raw) return false;
        try {            var data = JSON.parse(raw);
            this.applySaveData(data);            AudioSystem.playSound('door_open');            CombatLog.add('Game loaded from Slot ' + slot + '!');
            return true;        }
 catch (e) {            CombatLog.add('Load failed! Save data may be corrupted.');
            return false;        }
    },    applySaveData(data) {        player = new Player(data.classId);
        player.level = data.level || 1;
        player.exp = data.exp || 0;
        player.gold = data.gold || 0;
        player.hp = data.hp || data.maxHp;
        player.maxHp = data.maxHp || 100;
        player.mp = data.mp || data.maxMp;
        player.maxMp = data.maxMp || 50;
        player.atk = data.atk || 10;
        player.def = data.def || 8;
        player.int = data.int || 5;
        player.spd = data.spd || 8;
        player.luck = data.luck || 5;
        player.skills = data.skills || [];
        player.skillPoints = data.skillPoints || 0;
        if (data.weapon) player.weapon = ItemDatabase.find(function(i) { return i.id === data.weapon; });
        if (data.armor) player.armor = ItemDatabase.find(function(i) { return i.id === data.armor; });
        if (data.accessory) player.accessory = ItemDatabase.find(function(i) { return i.id === data.accessory; });
        player.inventory = (data.inventory || []).map(function(saved) {            var item = ItemDatabase.find(function(i) { return i.id === saved.id; });
            if (!item) return null;
            var copy = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(item));            copy.uid = Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9);            copy.quantity = saved.quantity || 1;
            return copy;        }).filter(function(i) { return i !== null; });        currentArea = data.area || 'village';
        player.x = data.x || 10;
        player.y = data.y || 6;
        player.direction = Direction.DOWN;
questFlags = data.questFlags || {};
openChests = new Set(data.openChests || []);
gameStarted = true;
gameState = GameState.EXPLORE;
PlayerSystem.recalcStats();
UI.showOverlay('gameHUD');
UI.updateHUD();
Minimap.render();
MapSystem.loadMap(currentArea);
Camera.centerOn(player.x, player.y);
},    deleteSave(slot) {        slot = slot || 1;
localStorage.removeItem(this.prefix + slot);
CombatLog.add('Save Slot ' + slot + ' deleted.');
},    hasSave(slot) {        return localStorage.getItem(this.prefix + slot) !== null;
},    getSaveInfo(slot) {        var raw = localStorage.getItem(this.prefix + slot);
        if (!raw) return null;
        try {            var data = JSON.parse(raw);
            return {                slot: slot,                className: data.className || 'Unknown',                level: data.level || 1,                area: data.area || 'unknown',                timestamp: data.timestamp,                playTime: data.playTime || 0,                gold: data.gold || 0            };
}
 catch (e) {            return null;        }
    },    autoSave() {        if (!player || !gameStarted) return;
        try {            var data = {                classId: player.classId, level: player.level, exp: player.exp, gold: player.gold,                hp: player.hp, maxHp: player.maxHp, mp: player.mp, maxMp: player.maxMp,                atk: player.atk, def: player.def, int: player.int, spd: player.spd, luck: player.luck,                skills: player.skills.slice(), skillPoints: player.skillPoints,                weapon: player.weapon ? player.weapon.id : null,                armor: player.armor ? player.armor.id : null,                accessory: player.accessory ? player.accessory.id : null,                inventory: player.inventory.map(function(i) { return { id: i.id, quantity: i.quantity || 1 }; }),                area: currentArea, x: player.x, y: player.y,                className: CLASS_NAMES[player.classId],                timestamp: Date.now(),                questFlags: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(questFlags)),                openChests: Array.from(openChests),                playTime: GameEngine ? GameEngine.playTime : 0,                battleCount: GameEngine ? GameEngine.battleCount : 0            };
localStorage.setItem('ros_auto_save', JSON.stringify(data));
}
 catch (e) {}
    },    getLatestSave() {        var latest = null;
        var latestTime = 0;
        for (var i = 1; i <= this.maxSlots; i++) {            var info = this.getSaveInfo(i);
            if (info && info.timestamp > latestTime) {                latestTime = info.timestamp;                latest = info;            }
        }
        return latest;    },    getSlotDisplay(slot) {        var info = this.getSaveInfo(slot);
        if (!info) return 'Slot ' + slot + ': Empty';
        var date = new Date(info.timestamp);
        var dateStr = date.toLocaleDateString() + ' ' + date.toLocaleTimeString();
        var timeStr = GameEngine ? GameEngine.formatTime(info.playTime) : '0:00';
        return 'Slot ' + slot + ': Lv.' + info.level + ' ' + info.className + ' | ' + info.area + ' | ' + timeStr + ' | ' + dateStr;    }
};

/* ============================================================   TITLE SCREEN & INIT - Animated Title, Char Select, Game Over   ============================================================ */const TitleScreen = {    particles: [],    titleAlpha: 0,    logoScale: 1,    selectedClass: -1,    animTimer: 0,    starField: [],    init() {        this.particles = [];
        this.starField = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < 60; i++) {            this.starField.push({                x: Math.random() * CANVAS_W,                y: Math.random() * CANVAS_H,                size: Math.random() * 2 + 1,                speed: Math.random() * 0.5 + 0.1,                alpha: Math.random() * 0.5 + 0.3            });
}        this.titleAlpha = 0;
},    update(dt) {        this.animTimer += dt;
        this.titleAlpha = Math.min(1, this.titleAlpha + dt);
        this.logoScale = 1 + Math.sin(this.animTimer * 2) * 0.02;
        for (var i = this.starField.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {            this.starField[i].y -= this.starField[i].speed;
            if (this.starField[i].y < -5) {                this.starField[i].y = CANVAS_H + 5;
                this.starField[i].x = Math.random() * CANVAS_W;            }
        }
        if (this.particles.length > 0) {            for (var j = this.particles.length - 1; j >= 0; j--) {                this.particles[j].life -= dt;
                if (this.particles[j].life <= 0) { this.particles.splice(j, 1); continue; }                this.particles[j].x += this.particles[j].vx * dt;
                this.particles[j].y += this.particles[j].vy * dt;
                this.particles[j].vy += 30 * dt;            }
        }
    },    renderTitle(cx) {        cx.fillStyle = '#0a0512';
        cx.fillRect(0, 0, CANVAS_W, CANVAS_H);
        var grd = cx.createRadialGradient(CANVAS_W / 2, CANVAS_H / 2, 50, CANVAS_W / 2, CANVAS_H / 2, 400);
grd.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(45,27,78,0.3)');
grd.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(10,5,18,0)');
        cx.fillStyle = grd;
        cx.fillRect(0, 0, CANVAS_W, CANVAS_H);
        this.starField.forEach(function(s) {            cx.fillStyle = 'rgba(200,200,255,' + s.alpha + ')';
            cx.fillRect(s.x, s.y, s.size, s.size);        });
        this.particles.forEach(function(p) {            cx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, p.life / p.maxLife);
            cx.fillStyle = p.color;
            cx.beginPath();
            cx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.size, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            cx.fill();        });
        cx.globalAlpha = this.titleAlpha;
        cx.save();
        cx.translate(CANVAS_W / 2, CANVAS_H / 3);
        cx.scale(this.logoScale, this.logoScale);
        cx.fillStyle = '#c9a959';
        cx.font = '32px "Press Start 2P", monospace';
        cx.textAlign = 'center';
        cx.shadowColor = '#c9a959';
        cx.shadowBlur = 20;
        cx.fillText('REALMS OF', 0, -15);
        cx.fillText('SHADOWS', 0, 25);
        cx.shadowBlur = 0;
        cx.fillStyle = '#d4a8ff';
        cx.font = '11px "Press Start 2P", monospace';
        cx.fillText('A Dark Fantasy RPG Adventure', 0, 55);
        cx.restore();
        cx.fillStyle = 'rgba(212,168,255,' + (0.4 + Math.sin(this.animTimer * 3) * 0.3) + ')';
        cx.font = '9px "Press Start 2P", monospace';
        cx.textAlign = 'center';
        cx.fillText('Press any key or click to begin...', CANVAS_W / 2, CANVAS_H - 50);
        cx.textAlign = 'left';
        cx.globalAlpha = 1;    },    renderCharSelect(cx) {        cx.fillStyle = 'rgba(10,5,18,0.95)';
        cx.fillRect(0, 0, CANVAS_W, CANVAS_H);
        cx.fillStyle = '#c9a959';
        cx.font = '16px "Press Start 2P", monospace';

            cx.fillStyle = colors[i % colors.length] + '44';
