            cx.fillText(opt, CANVAS_W / 2, iy + 21);
            if (isSelected) UIEnhanced.drawMenuCursor(cx, CANVAS_W / 2 - 128, iy + 9);        });
        cx.textAlign = 'left';    },    startNewGame(classId) {        this.selectedClass = classId;        Game.selectClass(classId);    },    initGame() {        this.titleAlpha = 0;
        this.animTimer = 0;
        this.selectedClass = -1;
},    spawnParticles(x, y, count, colors) {        for (var i = 0; i < count; i++) {            this.particles.push({                x: x + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 40,                y: y + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 40,                vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 100,                vy: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 100 - 50,                life: 1 + Math.random(),                maxLife: 1 + Math.random(),                size: Math.random() * 3 + 1,                color: colors[Math.floor(Math.random() * colors.length)]            });
}
    }
};
/* ============================================================   GAME INITIALIZATION - Bootstrap All Systems   ============================================================ */const GameInit = {    initialized: false,    init() {        if (this.initialized) return;
TitleScreen.init();
EnemyAI.dodgeCalc = CombatEnhanced.calcFleeChance;
        this.initialized = true;    },    startNewGame(classId) {        Game.selectClass(classId);
        if (typeof Game.confirmClass === 'function') Game.confirmClass();
        this.init();    },    continueGame() {        var latestSave = SaveLoadSystem.getLatestSave();
        if (latestSave) {            SaveLoadSystem.loadGame(latestSave.slot);        }
 else {            CombatLog.add('No save data found!');
}        this.init();
},    onAreaTransition(newArea) {        SaveLoadSystem.autoSave();
Minimap.render();
UI.updateHUD();
},    onBattleVictory(rewards) {        if (rewards) {            UIEnhanced.showNotification('+' + rewards.exp + ' EXP', 'success', 2);
            if (rewards.gold > 0) UIEnhanced.showNotification('+' + rewards.gold + ' Gold', 'item', 2);
rewards.items.forEach(function(item) {                var db = ItemDatabase.find(function(i) { return i.id === item.itemId; });
                if (db) UIEnhanced.showNotification('Got: ' + db.name, 'item', 2);            });        }
    },    onLevelUp(newLevel, statGains) {        UIEnhanced.showLevelUpDisplay(newLevel, statGains);
UIEnhanced.showNotification('Level Up! Now Lv.' + newLevel, 'levelup', 3);
TitleScreen.spawnParticles(CANVAS_W / 2, CANVAS_H / 2, 20, ['#c9a959', '#ffd700', '#fff']);
},    onItemGained(itemName) {        UIEnhanced.showNotification('Got: ' + itemName, 'item', 2);
},    onQuestUpdate(questName, status) {        var type = status === 'complete' ? 'success' : status === 'accept' ? 'warning' : 'info';
UIEnhanced.showNotification(questName + ' - ' + status, type, 3);
},    renderAllUI(cx) {        if (gameState === GameState.EXPLORE) {            UIEnhanced.renderNotifications(cx);
UIEnhanced.drawMinimapCanvas(cx);
UIEnhanced.updateLevelUpDisplay(1 / 60);
UIEnhanced.drawLevelUpDisplay(cx);
}
        if (gameState === GameState.COMBAT) {            QuestEnhanced.renderNotifications(cx);        }
        if (gameState === GameState.PAUSE) {            UIEnhanced.drawPauseMenuCanvas(cx);        }
        if (gameState === GameState.GAME_OVER) {            TitleScreen.renderGameOver(cx);        }
        if (gameState === GameState.VICTORY) {            TitleScreen.renderVictory(cx);        }
    }
};GameInit.init();
/* ============================================================   BATTLE REWARDS SYSTEM - Drop Tables, Loot, Exp Scaling   ============================================================ */const LootSystem = {    dropBonus: 0,    goldMultiplier: 1,    expMultiplier: 1,    calculateBattleRewards(enemies, isBoss, boss) {        var totalExp = 0, totalGold = 0, drops = [];
        if (isBoss && boss) {            totalExp = boss.level * 150;            totalGold = boss.level * 75;
            var bossDrops = this.getBossDrops(boss);            drops = drops.concat(bossDrops);        }
 else {            enemies.forEach(function(e) {                if (!e.isAlive || !e.isAlive()) return;
totalExp += Math.floor((e.exp || 10) * LootSystem.expMultiplier);
totalGold += Math.floor((e.gold || 5) * LootSystem.goldMultiplier);
                if (e.dropTable) {                    e.dropTable.forEach(function(drop) {                        var adjustedChance = drop.chance + LootSystem.dropBonus;
                        if (player && player.accessory && player.accessory.id === 704) adjustedChance += 10;
                        if (Math.random() * 100 < adjustedChance) {                            drops.push({ itemId: drop.itemId, qty: drop.qty || 1 });                        }
                    });                }
            });        }
        var luckBonus = 1 + (player ? player.luck * 0.005 : 0);        totalExp = Math.floor(totalExp * luckBonus);        totalGold = Math.floor(totalGold * luckBonus);
        if (player && player.accessory && player.accessory.id === 714) {            totalGold = Math.floor(totalGold * 1.5);        }
        return { exp: totalExp, gold: totalGold, items: drops };    },    getBossDrops(boss) {        var drops = [];
        switch (boss.templateId) {            case 0: drops.push({ itemId: 104, qty: 1 }); drops.push({ itemId: 8, qty: 3 }); break;
            case 1: drops.push({ itemId: 207, qty: 1 }); drops.push({ itemId: 9, qty: 2 }); break;
            case 2: drops.push({ itemId: 712, qty: 1 }); drops.push({ itemId: 18, qty: 3 }); break;            default: drops.push({ itemId: 0, qty: 5 }); break;        }
        return drops;    },    applyRewards(rewards) {        if (!player || !rewards) return;
        if (rewards.exp > 0) {            player.gainExp(rewards.exp);        }
        if (rewards.gold > 0) {            player.gold += rewards.gold;        }
        if (rewards.items && rewards.items.length > 0) {            rewards.items.forEach(function(drop) {                InventoryEnhanced.addItem(drop.itemId, drop.qty || 1);
                var db = ItemDatabase.find(function(i) { return i.id === drop.itemId; });
                if (db) UIEnhanced.showNotification('Got: ' + db.name, 'item', 2);
});
}        UI.updateHUD();
},    getExpForNextLevel(currentLevel) {        if (currentLevel >= EXP_TABLE.length) return 999999;
        return EXP_TABLE[currentLevel];    },    getLevelProgress() {        if (!player) return 0;
        return player.exp / player.getExpToNext();    },    getScalingFactor(areaName) {        var scales = { village: 0.5, darkForest: 1.0, ancientCaves: 1.5, ruinedCastle: 2.0, shadowRealm: 2.5 };
        return scales[areaName] || 1.0;    }
};
/* ============================================================   STATUS EFFECT RENDERING - Visual Indicators for Combat   ============================================================ */const StatusEffectRenderer = {    renderEnemyStatus(cx, enemy, x, y) {        if (!enemy.statusEffects || enemy.statusEffects.length === 0) return;
        var sx = x;        enemy.statusEffects.forEach(function(effect, i) {            var color = STATUS_COLORS[effect.type] || '#aaa';
            var name = STATUS_NAMES[effect.type] || '?';
            cx.fillStyle = color;
            cx.globalAlpha = 0.8;
            cx.fillRect(sx + i * 18, y, 15, 15);
            cx.strokeStyle = '#000';
            cx.lineWidth = 1;
            cx.strokeRect(sx + i * 18, y, 15, 15);
            cx.fillStyle = '#fff';
            cx.font = '6px "Press Start 2P", monospace';
            cx.textAlign = 'center';
            cx.fillText(name[0], sx + i * 18 + 8, y + 11);
            cx.textAlign = 'left';
            cx.globalAlpha = 1;        });    },    renderPlayerStatus(cx, x, y) {        if (!player || !player.statusEffects || player.statusEffects.length === 0) return;
        player.statusEffects.forEach(function(effect, i) {            var color = STATUS_COLORS[effect.type] || '#aaa';
            var name = STATUS_NAMES[effect.type] || '?';
            cx.fillStyle = color;
            cx.globalAlpha = 0.85;
            cx.fillRect(x + i * 22, y, 18, 18);
            cx.strokeStyle = '#4a2c8a';
            cx.lineWidth = 1;
            cx.strokeRect(x + i * 22, y, 18, 18);
            cx.fillStyle = '#fff';
            cx.font = '7px "Press Start 2P", monospace';
            cx.textAlign = 'center';
            cx.fillText(name[0], x + i * 22 + 9, y + 13);
            cx.textAlign = 'left';
            cx.globalAlpha = 1;        });    },    getStatusIcon(type) {        var icons = {            0: '?', 1: 'P', 2: 'B', 3: 'F', 4: 'S', 5: 'X', 6: 'R', 7: 'D', 8: 'H'        };
        return icons[type] || '?';    },    getEffectiveDuration(effect) {        var baseDuration = effect.turns || 3;
        return baseDuration;
},    isPositiveEffect(type) {        return type === StatusEffect.REGEN || type === StatusEffect.SHIELD || type === StatusEffect.HASTE;
},    isNegativeEffect(type) {        return type === StatusEffect.POISON || type === StatusEffect.BURN || type === StatusEffect.FREEZE ||               type === StatusEffect.STUN || type === StatusEffect.BLIND;
}
};
/* ============================================================   AREA TRANSITION SYSTEM - Door Detection, Zone Changes   ============================================================ */const AreaTransitionSystem = {    transitionCooldown: 0,    pendingTransition: null,    lastArea: null,    visitedAreas: new Set(),    checkTransition(x, y) {        if (this.transitionCooldown > 0) return null;
        var data = MapTileData[currentArea];
        if (!data) return null;
        var tile = World.getTile(currentArea, x, y);
        if (tile !== TileType.DOOR) return null;
        var trans = this.getTransitionForTile(x, y);
        if (trans) {            this.transitionCooldown = 0.5;
            return trans;        }
        return null;
},    getTransitionForTile(x, y) {        var transitions = {            village: [                { x: 4, y: 5, w: 1, h: 1, target: 'darkForest', tx: 1, ty: 9, dir: Direction.RIGHT },                { x: 9, y: 5, w: 1, h: 1, target: 'darkForest', tx: 14, ty: 18, dir: Direction.DOWN },                { x: 14, y: 5, w: 1, h: 1, target: 'darkForest', tx: 14, ty: 1, dir: Direction.UP }            ],            darkForest: [                { x: 0, y: 9, w: 1, h: 1, target: 'village', tx: 17, ty: 6, dir: Direction.LEFT },                { x: 29, y: 9, w: 1, h: 1, target: 'ancientCaves', tx: 1, ty: 8, dir: Direction.RIGHT }            ],            ancientCaves: [                { x: 0, y: 8, w: 1, h: 1, target: 'darkForest', tx: 28, ty: 9, dir: Direction.LEFT },                { x: 29, y: 8, w: 1, h: 1, target: 'ruinedCastle', tx: 1, ty: 5, dir: Direction.RIGHT },                { x: 6, y: 3, w: 1, h: 1, target: 'ruinedCastle', tx: 13, ty: 17, dir: Direction.DOWN }            ],            ruinedCastle: [                { x: 0, y: 5, w: 1, h: 1, target: 'ancientCaves', tx: 28, ty: 8, dir: Direction.LEFT },                { x: 29, y: 5, w: 1, h: 1, target: 'shadowRealm', tx: 1, ty: 8, dir: Direction.RIGHT },                { x: 11, y: 5, w: 1, h: 1, target: 'ancientCaves', tx: 13, ty: 1, dir: Direction.UP }            ],            shadowRealm: [                { x: 0, y: 8, w: 1, h: 1, target: 'ruinedCastle', tx: 28, ty: 5, dir: Direction.LEFT }            ]        };
        var areaTrans = transitions[currentArea] || [];
        for (var i = 0; i < areaTrans.length; i++) {            var t = areaTrans[i];
            if (x >= t.x && x < t.x + t.w && y >= t.y && y < t.y + t.h) {                return t;            }
        }
        return null;    },    executeTransition(trans) {        if (!trans) return;
        this.lastArea = currentArea;
        this.visitedAreas.add(currentArea);        currentArea = trans.target;
        player.x = trans.tx;
        player.y = trans.ty;
        player.direction = trans.dir || Direction.DOWN;
        this.transitionCooldown = 0.5;        AudioSystem.playSound('area_transition');
        var music = MapSystem.getAreaMusic(currentArea);        AudioSystem.fadeInMusic(music, 0.5);        Minimap.render();        UI.updateHUD();        SaveLoadSystem.autoSave();
        this.checkAreaQuests();    },    update(dt) {        if (this.transitionCooldown > 0) {            this.transitionCooldown -= dt;        }
    },    checkAreaQuests() {        if (currentArea === 'darkForest') {            QuestEnhanced.updateQuestProgress('move', 'darkForest', 1);        }
 else if (currentArea === 'ancientCaves') {            QuestEnhanced.updateQuestProgress('move', 'reach_cave_depths', 1);        }
 else if (currentArea === 'ruinedCastle') {            QuestEnhanced.updateQuestProgress('move', 'ruinedCastle', 1);        }
 else if (currentArea === 'shadowRealm') {            QuestEnhanced.updateQuestProgress('move', 'shadowRealm', 1);        }
    },    isAreaVisited(areaName) {        return this.visitedAreas.has(areaName);
},    getVisitedAreas() {        return Array.from(this.visitedAreas);
},    getAreaByName(name) {        var normalized = name.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, '');
        for (var key in MapTileData) {            if (key.toLowerCase() === normalized) return key;        }
        return null;    }
};
/* ============================================================   ENCOUNTER SYSTEM - Random Battles, Encounter Rates   ============================================================ */const EncounterSystem = {    stepCount: 0,    encounterRate: 0.06,    minSteps: 3,    encounterCooldown: 0,    lastEncounterArea: null,    totalEncounters: 0,    onPlayerStep() {        if (this.encounterCooldown > 0) {            this.encounterCooldown--;
            return null;        }        this.stepCount++;
        if (this.stepCount < this.minSteps) return null;
        var rate = this.getEncounterRate(currentArea);
        if (Math.random() < rate) {            this.stepCount = 0;
            this.encounterCooldown = 2;
            this.totalEncounters++;
            return this.generateEncounter();        }
        return null;
},    getEncounterRate(areaName) {        var rates = {            village: 0,            darkForest: 0.06,            ancientCaves: 0.08,            ruinedCastle: 0.07,            shadowRealm: 0.09        };
        return rates[areaName] || 0.05;    },    generateEncounter() {        var enemyList = AREA_ENEMIES[currentArea];
        if (!enemyList || enemyList.length === 0) return null;
        var numEnemies = 1 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 3);
        var encounterIds = [];
        var levelRange = this.getLevelRange(currentArea);
        for (var i = 0; i < numEnemies; i++) {            var possibleEnemies = enemyList.filter(function(eid) {                var e = EnemyDatabase.find(function(ed) { return ed.id === eid; });
                return e && e.level >= levelRange.min && e.level <= levelRange.max;            });
            if (possibleEnemies.length === 0) possibleEnemies = enemyList;            encounterIds.push(possibleEnemies[Math.floor(Math.random() * possibleEnemies.length)]);        }
        return encounterIds;
},    getLevelRange(areaName) {        var ranges = {            village: { min: 1, max: 3 },            darkForest: { min: 1, max: 8 },            ancientCaves: { min: 10, max: 16 },            ruinedCastle: { min: 18, max: 25 },            shadowRealm: { min: 26, max: 32 }
        };
        return ranges[areaName] || { min: 1, max: 10 };    },    canEncounter() {        if (currentArea === 'village') return false;
        if (this.encounterCooldown > 0) return false;
        return true;    },    resetEncounterCounter() {        this.stepCount = 0;
        this.encounterCooldown = 3;
},    getEncounterText() {        var texts = [            'A monster jumps out from the shadows!',            'You are ambushed!',            'Enemies appear!',            'You encounter hostile creatures!',            'Battle!'        ];
        return texts[Math.floor(Math.random() * texts.length)];    },    getTreasureDropChance() {        var base = 5;
        if (player && player.accessory && player.accessory.id === 704) base += 10;
        if (player) base += player.luck * 0.3;
        return Math.min(25, base);    },    rollTreasureDrop(areaName) {        if (Math.random() * 100 > this.getTreasureDropChance()) return null;
        var treasures = {            darkForest: [0, 4, 10],            ancientCaves: [1, 5, 14],            ruinedCastle: [8, 16, 17],            shadowRealm: [9, 11, 18]        };
        var pool = treasures[areaName] || [0, 4];
        return pool[Math.floor(Math.random() * pool.length)];    }
};
/* ============================================================   NOTIFICATION QUEUE - Persistent Toast Messages   ============================================================ */const NotificationQueue = {    queue: [],    maxVisible: 4,    displayDuration: 3.5,    fadeDuration: 0.5,    add(text, type, duration) {        this.queue.push({            text: text,            type: type || 'info',            duration: duration || this.displayDuration,            timer: duration || this.displayDuration,            alpha: 1        });
},    addQuestAccepted(questName) {        this.add('Quest Accepted: ' + questName, 'warning', 4);
},    addQuestComplete(questName) {        this.add('Quest Complete: ' + questName, 'success', 4);
},    addLevelUp(level) {        this.add('Level Up! Lv.' + level, 'levelup', 4);
},    addItemGet(itemName) {        this.add('Got: ' + itemName, 'item', 2.5);
},    addGoldGet(amount) {        this.add('+' + amount + ' Gold', 'item', 2);
},    addExpGet(amount) {        this.add('+' + amount + ' EXP', 'success', 2);
},    addError(text) {        this.add(text, 'error', 3);
},    addInfo(text) {        this.add(text, 'info', 3);
},    update(dt) {        for (var i = this.queue.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {            this.queue[i].timer -= dt;
            if (this.queue[i].timer <= 0) {                this.queue.splice(i, 1);            }
 else if (this.queue[i].timer < this.fadeDuration) {                this.queue[i].alpha = this.queue[i].timer / this.fadeDuration;            }
        }
    },    render(cx) {        var ny = 135;
        var visible = this.queue.slice(-this.maxVisible);        visible.forEach(function(n) {            cx.globalAlpha = n.alpha;
            var colors = {                info: { bg: 'rgba(26,10,46,0.9)', border: '#4a2c8a', text: '#d4d4d4' },                success: { bg: 'rgba(20,60,20,0.9)', border: '#6bff6b', text: '#6bff6b' },                warning: { bg: 'rgba(60,45,10,0.9)', border: '#c9a959', text: '#c9a959' },                error: { bg: 'rgba(60,10,10,0.9)', border: '#ff4444', text: '#ff4444' },                levelup: { bg: 'rgba(60,50,10,0.95)', border: '#ffd700', text: '#ffd700' },                item: { bg: 'rgba(10,30,60,0.9)', border: '#6b9fff', text: '#6b9fff' }
            };
            var c = colors[n.type] || colors.info;
            cx.font = '8px "Press Start 2P", monospace';
            var tw = cx.measureText(n.text).width + 30;
            var nx = CANVAS_W / 2 - tw / 2;
            cx.fillStyle = c.bg;
            cx.fillRect(nx, ny, tw, 26);
            cx.strokeStyle = c.border;
            cx.lineWidth = 2;
            cx.strokeRect(nx, ny, tw, 26);
            cx.fillStyle = c.text;
            cx.textAlign = 'center';
            cx.fillText(n.text, CANVAS_W / 2, ny + 17);
            cx.textAlign = 'left';
            cx.globalAlpha = 1;            ny += 30;        });    },    clear() {        this.queue = [];    },    hasNotifications() {        return this.queue.length > 0;    }
};
/* ============================================================   COMBAT ANIMATION MANAGER - Timing, Sequences, Effects   ============================================================ */const CombatAnimManager = {    animations: [],    currentAnim: null,    isAnimating: false,    queueAnimation(type, data, callback) {        this.animations.push({ type: type, data: data || {}, callback: callback, timer: 0, duration: 0.5 });
},    playAttackAnim(attackerName, targetName, isCrit, callback) {        var duration = isCrit ? 0.6 : 0.4;
        this.currentAnim = {            type: 'attack',            attacker: attackerName,            target: targetName,            isCrit: isCrit,            timer: 0,            duration: duration,            phase: 'windup',            callback: callback        };
        this.isAnimating = true;
        if (isCrit) {            AudioSystem.playSound('critical_hit');        }
 else {            AudioSystem.playSound('sword_slash');        }
    },    playMagicAnim(skillName, element, callback) {        var duration = 0.6;
        this.currentAnim = {            type: 'magic',            skill: skillName,            element: element,            timer: 0,            duration: duration,            phase: 'cast',            callback: callback        };
        this.isAnimating = true;        AudioSystem.playSound('magic_cast');
        if (element === Element.FIRE) ParticleSystem.emitFire(CANVAS_W / 2, CANVAS_H / 3);
        else if (element === Element.ICE) ParticleSystem.emitIce(CANVAS_W / 2, CANVAS_H / 3);
        else if (element === Element.LIGHTNING) ParticleSystem.emitLightning(CANVAS_W / 2, CANVAS_H / 3);
        else if (element === Element.SHADOW) ParticleSystem.emitShadow(CANVAS_W / 2, CANVAS_H / 3);
},    playHealAnim(targetName, callback) {        this.currentAnim = {            type: 'heal',            target: targetName,            timer: 0,            duration: 0.5,            phase: 'glow',            callback: callback        };
        this.isAnimating = true;
AudioSystem.playSound('healing');
ParticleSystem.emitHeal(CANVAS_W / 2, CANVAS_H - 200);
},    playDefendAnim(unitName, callback) {        this.currentAnim = {            type: 'defend',            unit: unitName,            timer: 0,            duration: 0.3,            phase: 'shield',            callback: callback        };
        this.isAnimating = true;
},    playStatusAnim(targetName, statusType, callback) {        this.currentAnim = {            type: 'status',            target: targetName,            status: statusType,            timer: 0,            duration: 0.4,            phase: 'apply',            callback: callback        };
        this.isAnimating = true;    },    update(dt) {        if (!this.isAnimating || !this.currentAnim) return;
        this.currentAnim.timer += dt;
        if (this.currentAnim.timer >= this.currentAnim.duration) {            var cb = this.currentAnim.callback;
            this.currentAnim = null;
            this.isAnimating = false;
            if (cb) cb();        }
    },    renderAnimations(cx) {        if (!this.currentAnim) return;
        var anim = this.currentAnim;
        var progress = anim.timer / anim.duration;
        cx.globalAlpha = 1 - progress * 0.5;
        if (anim.type === 'attack') {            var flashAlpha = Math.sin(progress * Math.PI) * 0.4;
            cx.fillStyle = anim.isCrit ? 'rgba(255,255,0,' + flashAlpha + ')' : 'rgba(255,255,255,' + flashAlpha + ')';
            cx.fillRect(0, 0, CANVAS_W, CANVAS_H);
            if (anim.isCrit) {                cx.fillStyle = '#ffff44';
                cx.font = '16px "Press Start 2P", monospace';
                cx.textAlign = 'center';
                cx.fillText('CRITICAL!', CANVAS_W / 2, CANVAS_H / 3);
                cx.textAlign = 'left';            }
        }
 else if (anim.type === 'magic') {            var elemColor = ELEMENT_COLORS[anim.element] || '#fff';
            var size = 30 + progress * 50;
            cx.fillStyle = elemColor;
            cx.globalAlpha = (1 - progress) * 0.3;
            cx.beginPath();
            cx.arc(CANVAS_W / 2, CANVAS_H / 3, size, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            cx.fill();        }
 else if (anim.type === 'heal') {            cx.fillStyle = 'rgba(68,255,68,' + (Math.sin(progress * Math.PI * 3) * 0.2) + ')';
            cx.fillRect(0, CANVAS_H - 200, CANVAS_W, 100);        }
 else if (anim.type === 'defend') {            cx.fillStyle = 'rgba(68,136,255,' + (1 - progress) * 0.3 + ')';
            cx.fillRect(CANVAS_W / 2 - 50, CANVAS_H - 250, 100, 100);        }        cx.globalAlpha = 1;    },    clearAnimations() {        this.animations = [];
        this.currentAnim = null;
        this.isAnimating = false;    }
};
